COHERENCE FRAMEWORK v25 -- THEORY
============================================================================================================================================
FORMAT RULES [operative]
 Companion files: applied, cases, wip, beginner.txt
 order_truth.txt [Order/Truth/Disorder], cf_grep.txt.
 Line width: 140 chars max [desktop]. Separators exempt [use 140-wide].
 Legacy 108 was phone-era -- do not re-impose for new kit text.
 Blank lines: max 2 consecutive.
 Double separators: prohibited [audit before save].
 WIP content: staging section only, never inline with confirmed theory.
 Version header: no change log. Version number + format rules only.
 Section headers: COHERENCE FRAMEWORK vN -- SECTION [NAME].
 Case entries: [CASE:NAME] header, S-rating required, cross-ref at end.
 Abbreviations: GA/BI/SR/TM/TD/GC/SP/CC/MI/CL/RI/TS/SA/CAG/FC/
 MSC/ECC/ICD/ECD/CT [see NAME COMPRESSION POLICY in Section One].
 Section abbrev [v25]: within each topic/section, first mention
 FULL NAME [ABBREV]; shorten after. Scope = that topic, not whole
 file. Canonical expansions: NAME COMPRESSION POLICY.
 Wrap at 140: applies to all body text, definitions, case entries,
 weapon entries, policy positions, and WIP staging content.
 Existing 108-wrapped kit text: rewrap on touch, not bulk pass.
 Kit grammar: cf_grep.txt KIT FILE GRAMMAR [division / topic / content loop].
 Moral placement desk: **MORAL CUBE** [Section One Foundation -- first topic; grep ^MORAL CUBE].
============================================================================================================================================
STANDING RULES [v22 -- permanent]
============================================================================================================================================
VERSION AND SESSION LOGIC [v24 -- unified]
============================================================================================================================================

OLD VERSION ARCHIVE [v25 -- standing; read-only]:
 `technical/old-versions/` monolith snapshots [v3-v24] are reference only.
 Do not edit, merge, or promote in place. Read slices for audit
 land all changes in v25 kit txt [theory / applied / cases / wip].
 Applied file archive block [~8002+] is kit content already merged
 from prior versions -- maintain there, not in `technical/old-versions/`.

SECTION DEPENDENCY CHAIN:
 Section One [FOUNDATION] is highest-integrity. All downstream
 sections depend on it. Dependency order:
 Section One → Section Two → Section Three → Section Four
 → Sections Five and Six → Section Seven → Cases.
 Science [Section Seven] depends on Section One only.
 Changes higher in the chain carry more integration risk and
 require higher gates before finalizing.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WHAT CAN BE FINALIZED IN-SESSION [low-medium integration risk]:
 Case library additions [S:2 or S:3 entries]: finalize.
 S-rating passes [mechanical]: finalize.
 Section Seven [SCIENCE] additions: finalize.
 Section Six [POLITICS IN ACTION] updates with epistemic tags:
 finalize with tags applied.
 Section Three [MECHANISMS] additions [tools/weapons]: finalize
 if boundary protection respected.
 Section Five [POLITICAL THEORY] applied readings and extensions
 to existing confirmed entries: finalize with T3 tags applied.
 Bug fixes, cross-ref updates, stub reductions: finalize.

WHAT REQUIRES WIP STAGING + NEW SESSION [high integration risk]:
 New named qualities [Section One].
 Amended core definitions [Section One].
 New structural findings [Section Two].
 New level designations [L4.5, L5, etc.] [Section One].
 Any change to nested cycle model or tower structure [Section One].
 New reading procedures or scoring changes [Section Four].
 New structural political theory [Section Five].
 RULE: any addition or change to Section One is WIP-only until:
 [1] Formal version change declared.
 [2] New session opens after the update.
 Reason: long sessions produce structural blindness [confirmed
 v16 review]. Fresh instance is the stress-test instrument.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERSION CHANGE TRIGGER [when to bump the version number]:
 MINOR / NON-CRITICAL changes: accumulate in current version.
 Examples: case additions, S-rating passes, status note updates,
 cross-ref additions, bug fixes, WIP status notes, Section Six
 applied readings, supplementary entries.
 CRITICAL OR BIG changes: trigger a version bump.
 Critical = changes a load-bearing definition, closes a major
 WIP, adds a new named tool/weapon, revises confirmed theory
 or restructures a standing rule.
 Big = large volume [>500 lines added], document-wide mechanical
 changes [e.g. full S-rating renumber], or multiple major
 case confirmations in a single session.
 Either critical OR big is sufficient; both together is certain.
 WIP CRITICAL AND BIG: triggers a named version bump with a
 formal version note in the WIP file [coherence_framework_vN_wip.txt].
 Named = the bump is explicitly described in the ACTIONABLE block at
 the top of the WIP file, listing what changed and why it warranted
 a new version. Not every bump needs a name; critical+big WIP
 changes do.
 DEFAULT: when uncertain, version bump is cheaper than not
 bumping. A version number is free; a missed integration
 error is not.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEW SESSION SUGGESTION TRIGGER [when to hand off to a fresh instance]:
 Work ALL actionable WIPs in the current session before suggesting
 a new session. Actionable = has a candidate case, a primary case
 confirmed, an integration check ready, or a falsification
 condition testable. Non-actionable = dormant, waiting on external
 case, or no testable condition available this pass.
 Suggest a new session only when ALL of the following are true:
 [1] All actionable WIPs have been worked this session.
 [2] Remaining open WIPs are non-actionable.
 [3] Any theory-level additions requiring fresh-instance
 integration check [Section One class] have been made
 and flagged in the WIP file.
 Do NOT suggest a new session mid-session solely because context
 bias is accumulating -- complete actionable work first, then
 hand off. Context bias is managed by completing work, not by
 stopping early.
 Suggestion format: name what was completed, name remaining
 non-actionable WIPs, state what the new session should do first.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WIP THEORY PLACEMENT RULE [v25]:
 WIP theory additions do NOT go inline with confirmed theory and do NOT
 go in this file. All WIP development lives in the separate WIP file
 [coherence_framework_vN_wip.txt]. No WIP THEORY STAGING section exists
 in v25+.
 New WIP: add to WIP file only. No stub in this file.
 Promotion requires: new session review + primary case or
 falsification condition tested + integration errors cleared.
 On promotion: copy entry from WIP file to correct section here
 remove from WIP file, trigger version bump if critical.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE SPLIT ARCHITECTURE [v25 -- session workflow]:
 PURPOSE: grep efficiency. Searching 8-14k lines instead of 33k+.
 Split at session start; merge at session end. WIP file permanent
 separate -- never merged into main file.

 SPLIT FILES [session working files]:
 coherence_framework_v25_theory.txt [~14,100 lines]
 Header, standing rules, WIP file pointer, Sections One-Four.
 All confirmed theory, qualities, findings, mechanisms, procedure.
 coherence_framework_v25_applied.txt [~9,600 lines]
 Sections Five-Eight + Archive + Reference.
 Political theory, politics in action, science, theology,
 archive of version notes, quick reference glossary.
 coherence_framework_v25_cases.txt [hub -- redirect only;]
 coherence_framework_v25_cases_real.txt [REAL library; S3+ bar]
 coherence_framework_v25_cases_fiction.txt [fiction library; S2 cap]
 Grammar per file: GUIDE [BY_QUALITY best→worst] + SOURCE sections.
 Bodies under [SOURCE:KEY]; no CATEGORY banners; no BY_ALPHABET dump.

 PERMANENT SEPARATE FILES [never merged]:
 coherence_framework_v25_order_truth.txt
 Critical after beginner: Order/Truth/Disorder only.
 METHOD OF TRUTH, Order/Disorder defs, three-point axis.
 Only tension unit here: METHOD OF TRUTH mode poles.
 Other TU: theory THREE-STATE + Part B; applied SPEED/SCALE.
 Stubs remain in theory for moved Order/Disorder blocks.
 Personal/coherence_framework_v25_ai_long_term.txt [queue]
 All active WIPs, priority queue, staged observations.
 cf_grep.txt [~110 lines]
 grep reference: file map, line ranges, entry patterns,
 structural scan commands, split/merge commands.
 Update line ranges after any session adding significant volume.
 Load at session start alongside the working files.

 LOAD RULES [what to open for each task]:
 Session start: load cf_grep.txt first [file map + line ranges].
 After beginner: order_truth.txt [Order/Truth/Disorder].
 Theory work, quality edits, structural findings: theory file.
 Political readings, science entries, theology: applied file.
 Case entries, case cross-refs, case count audits: cases_real or
 cases_fiction [hub for orient only].
 All WIP development: WIP file only.
 Cross-file REF chase: grep theory and applied if target
 location unknown; Order/Disorder -> order_truth.txt
 tension units -> theory Part B / THREE-STATE or applied SPEED.

 SPLIT COMMANDS [session start -- from merged main file]:
 grep -n "^SECTION ONE\|^SECTION FIVE\|^CASES$" \
 coherence_framework_v25.txt
 [verify line numbers, then:]
 sed -n '1,Xp' coherence_framework_v25.txt > cf_v25_theory.txt
 sed -n 'X+1,Yp' coherence_framework_v25.txt > cf_v25_applied.txt
 sed -n 'Y+1,9999p' coherence_framework_v25.txt > cf_v25_cases.txt

 MERGE COMMANDS [session end -- back to single main file]:
 cat cf_v25_theory.txt cf_v25_applied.txt cf_v25_cases.txt \
 > coherence_framework_v25.txt
 [WIP file stays separate -- do not merge]
 Verify: grep section headers + case count after merge.

 LINE NUMBER REFERENCE [v25 -- re-verify before splitting]:
 NOTE: numbers shift with every edit. Always grep before splitting.
 theory file: S1 starts ~692; S2 ~4651; S3 ~8952; S4 ~11955.
 applied file: S5 starts ~line 11 [of split file]; S8 ~5965
 Archive ~6807; Reference ~9193.
 cases file: CATEGORY:REAL ~563; CATEGORY:GAMES ~5622.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEAR-MISS MECHANISM HANDLING [v23 -- permanent]:
 Any anti-coherence mechanism that resembles a named weapon or
 tool closely enough to risk misidentification, but does not
 match on close inspection, must be logged to the
 [NEAR-MISS MECHANISM REGISTER]. Two failure modes prohibited:
 [1] Forcing the near-miss into an existing tag to close a
 gap or complete a count -- corrupts case library and
 tool/weapon calibration.
 [2] Silently discarding it -- unmapped mechanisms are real
 findings [confirmed v23: Theranos evaluator-side status
 weighting was missed on original case-writing pass, caught
 only on deliberate retroactive scan].
 PERMANENT register never stubs; exempt from CLOSED purge.
 Individual candidates close when investigated and resolved;
 the register persists indefinitely.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
COMPRESSION AND MERGE LOGIC [v25 -- standing rule; all kit txt]:
 Applied when editing or adding entries in theory, applied, cases, wip.
 Do NOT force compression or merging -- apply only when the signal
 fires or duplication is obvious, not to meet a count, close a gap,
 or reduce file size as a goal in itself.

 KIT ADDITION GATE [v25 -- apply before any new entry or body expansion]:
 Add kit prose only when one of:
 [1] NEW -- no existing home; grep kit first. Home exists -> cross-ref
 only [see CROSS-REF AS SIGNAL below].
 [2] VALUE -- adds texture, falsification, proof coverage, or routing
 the reader cannot get from existing entries. Restatement without
 delta = do not add.
 [3] BORDERLINE -- uncertain or thin: one-line note or WIP stub +
 tag [borderline …]; do not promote to full theory/case body until
 confirmed.

 EDGE CASE PROMOTION [v25 -- apply when hard/atypical case tempts new entry]:
 Default: edge case -> instance, sub-finding, or exception note under
 existing rule [90% RULE; section order: findings before edge cases].
 Do NOT promote to new fundamental, form line, or quality from one hard
 case alone.
 UNIVERSALIZATION TRAP: hard case setting category standard =
 EDGE CASE UNIVERSALIZATION [weapon] -- not discovery. Cross-ref weapon
 do not add theory body.
 PROMOTE only when ALL hold:
 [1] Mechanism fork -- mid or end differs from parent [disorder_forms
 THINK FORMAT separate line]; not surface/register/scale variant.
 [2] Gap fail -- existing quality + modifier cannot cover >50%
 [QUALITY ADDITION / MERGE LOGIC].
 [3] Distinct falsification -- parent home's falsifier does not apply cleanly.
 [4] Confirmation -- second independent case OR S3->S4 bar met [E/M/F]
 borderline until then [tag borderline; WIP stub OK].
 Same mechanism, cleaner proof -> PRIMARY SHIFT [below]; not new entry.

 Not new but cleaner proof of same mechanism -> PRIMARY SHIFT [below]
 do not stack equivalent entries.
 Redundant or equivalent-and-not-stronger -> cross-ref only; case
 REJECTION THRESHOLD [CASE LIBRARY ADDITION LOGIC].

 PRIMARY SHIFT [when cleaner proof of same mechanism]:
 Promote new entry as primary proof / canonical home for that mechanism.
 Demote prior primary -- do not delete: tag [secondary proof -- MECHANISM]
 or [superseded by LOCATION/TAG] + one line why new is cleaner.
 Sub-finding alternate exemplar inside one case -- not demoted primary:
 tag [alternate proof -- MECHANISM]; do not use [secondary proof] for
 that pattern.
 Cross-ref both ways: primary -> secondary as alternate/legacy; secondary
 -> primary as read-first. Keeps inbound refs, texture, falsification
 history. Same mechanism, different texture/scale -> both stay as
 primary + secondary; do not shift unless cleaner on same proof role.
 Borderline whether cleaner -> note both; tag borderline; do not shift
 primary yet. Cross-ref: NESTED CYCLE MODEL [superseded -- v25]
 TOWER ORDER [superseded -- v25]; cases [alternate proof -- …] tags.

 CROSS-REF AS SIGNAL AND INSTRUMENT [primary]:
 Cross-reference density is both the **detect signal** and the
 **compress instrument**. Prefer cross-ref over new prose whenever
 a mechanism home already exists. Do not restate what a pointer can
 carry.

 DETECT [before writing OR when revising]:
 grep -c 'CONCEPT_NAME\|\[REF:TAG\]\|\[CASE:TAG\]' coherence_framework_v25*.txt
 [1] Inbound -- concept already named elsewhere?
 [2] Outbound -- body duplicates targets it already cites?
 [3] Instance -- S6 on CASE, note under parent, wedge on weapon?
 Signal fires when: count >= 3 across kit txt OR duplicate prose at
 cited homes OR obvious instance-of-parent. Exact duplicate fires
 even at low count.

 RESPONSE [do not skip steps; each optional except [1] when home exists]:
 [1] CROSS-REF FIRST -- home exists: pointer only [index, sub-finding,
 footer xref]. No new mechanism body.
 [2] COMPRESS [if signal still fires] -- local body remains: keep named
 concept, falsification, T3, anti-wedge; replace duplicated prose with
 pointer list. Tag: [high cross-ref] or [high cross-ref -- vN].
 [3] PROXIMITY MOVE [if compressed or obviously misplaced] -- entry
 belongs adjacent to primary mechanism home? Move only if proximity
 logic improves navigation; not because compression happened alone.
 [4] FORM HUB [if [2] tagged or cluster needs index] -- stable routing
 block: one line per pointer; vocabulary preserved; primary named once.
 Covers S6→CASE mirrors, applied instance under parent, WIP cross-ref
 hub tables, GUIDE/index routing, [MOVED -- high cross-ref] stubs.
 **Terminal for instance-of-parent or cross-section:** tag
 [high cross-ref] = hub complete; **merge test not implied**. STOP
 after [4] unless same-section duplicate prose remains [see fork].
 [5] MERGE TEST [separate pass -- not same session as [2]-[4]] -- only
 when same section AND same mechanism AND duplicate prose at both sites.
 Criteria below + T1-T9 [SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION]. If merge fails:
 keep separate hub; note proximity. Cross-section S6/CASE/applied-note
 pairs: **never merge** -- hub at [4] is the correct end state.
 [6] REMOVAL TEST [optional; analyst gate -- never auto-delete]:
 After [4]-[5], a [high cross-ref] block may be pure routing with no
 unique value. Consider removal only when ALL hold:
 (a) Body is pointers only -- no falsification, T3, behavioral
 record, or named concept defined only at this site.
 (b) Primary home + index lines already give the same navigation.
 (c) Removal does not break [REF:TAG], BY_QUALITY index, or another
 entry's required cross-ref anchor.
 (d) Not a CASE body, open WIP, Section One definition, or stub with
 CLOSED/purge pending.
 If (a)-(c) hold: **ask analyst** before delete -- one-line rationale
 [what routing is lost vs what primary/index already provides].
 Prefer fold into primary footer xref or index line before full delete.
 Agent must not remove compressed entries without explicit analyst yes
 on that entry. Removal is a separate action from compress/move/hub.

 DECISION FORK [after detect]:
 Instance of parent OR different section [S6 on CASE, note under parent,
 [MOVED] stub, WIP hub]? -> [1]-[4] then **STOP** [hub terminal].
 Same section AND duplicate mechanism prose at both sites? -> [5] merge
 test on **separate pass** [version audit preferred].
 Pointer-only orphan after hub? -> [6] removal test; analyst yes only.

 CROSS-REF FORMAT [compressed entry / form hub minimum]:
 One line per pointer: TOPIC -> LOCATION [what reader gets there].
 Example: capture endpoints -> theory CAPTURE FAILURE MODES DIFFER
 ~3222. Primary truth at cited home; compressed entry is routing.

 Goal: vocabulary preserved, bodies shortened, one primary home per
 mechanism; cross-ref is the link [SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION].
 Pipeline summary: detect -> cross-ref -> compress? -> move closer? ->
 form hub? -> **STOP** [instance/cross-section] | merge? [same-section
 separate pass] -> ask before remove?

 FORM HUB [step 4 detail]:
 Success = routing architecture only -- not a merge prelude. Distinct from
 disorder_forms.txt surface-form library [different "form hub" term].
 WIP cluster index [e.g. TRANS ROUTING CLUSTER], KIT HOMES pointer blocks
 and S6 entries with STRUCTURAL FINDING at CASE + battery kept = form hubs.
 Do not chain step [5] in the same edit pass as [2]-[4].

 MERGE TEST [step 5 detail]:
 Can compressed entry and adjacent primary merge without losing load?
 Merge criteria [all three required; then T1-T9]:
 [1] Both entries describe the same mechanism or finding at
 different resolutions or from different angles.
 [2] The merged entry is shorter than either original entry with
 both cross-refs preserved.
 [3] No named concept or falsification condition is lost.
 If all three hold: flag [MERGE CANDIDATE -- T1-T9 passed]; execute at
 version change per SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION RULE 4. If any fails: keep
 separate hub; note proximity.

 WHEN NOT TO COMPRESS [signal overridden]:
 Active falsification conditions, open WIP questions, or behavioral
 tests that require full statement to apply correctly -- length is
 functional. Stubs: close, do not compress further. High T3 caveats
 that are load-bearing: caveat text not compressible without losing
 the epistemic flag. Primary home for a mechanism with no duplicate
 elsewhere: do not compress to pointers only.

 AGENT DEFAULT [write path]:
 [1] Grep for existing home before drafting prose.
 [2] If found -> cross-ref sub-finding under parent; tag [high cross-ref].
 [3] If new primary required -> full body once; instances after cross-ref
 only.
 [4] Compress / move / form hub in same edit when signal obvious; do not
 chain merge or removal in same pass -- merge = separate pass; removal
 = propose separately; ask first.
 Examples: DIVERGENT CAPTURE GEOMETRY under POSITIONAL DESIRE
 ASYMMETRIC RISK FINANCE under PRICED ROUTING DEFAULT
 MINNESOTA/ROE S6 -> CASE + pattern parent
 ROTHERHAM RESOLUTION PREVENTION -> theory weapon home + Jay cite.

============================================================================================================================================

SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION [v22 -- permanent]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Seven sections plus Cases, Archive, Reference. Boundaries are
 load-bearing. Do not collapse across section types.
 Content spanning sections: PRIMARY home in most specific
 applicable section; CROSS-REFERENCES in all others.
 Never duplicate substantial content. Cross-reference is the
 link; primary entry is the truth.
 ORDER: put similar close → cross-reference if similar but
 not same → merge if nine tests pass [T1-T9 all required]
 → remove if five tests pass [R1-R5; harder than merge].
 Merge is half-remove [absorbed entry lost from location].
 Both need high bars. Remove is harder than merge.
 No compress. Recovery sessions: merge and removal suspended.
 CLOSED WIPs: reduce staging entry to stub immediately.
 Stub removed after next session R1-R4 verification.
 [STUB -- VERIFY AND REMOVE vN+1] is the flag.
 GENERAL ORDER: simple to complex throughout. Definitions
 before findings; general before specific; confirmed before
 candidate. Specific ordering declared per section where
 a different ordering applies.
 See SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [Section Three].
 Boundary violations: flag [BOUNDARY VIOLATION -- belongs in
 SECTION X]. Move on next version increment. Do not move
 mid-session without full cross-ref audit.

 SECTION ONE -- FOUNDATION [SEC:FOUNDATION]
 Scope: preconditions, level theory [L1/L2/L3/L3.5],
 nested cycle model, axis definitions [CENTER/TRUTH/
 OUROBOROS], core operational definitions [EVIL/GOOD].
 Test: does this entry define what the framework IS?
 Violation: confirmed findings, weapons entries, reading
 procedures, political readings placed inline with
 foundation theory.

 SECTION TWO -- FINDINGS [SEC:FINDINGS]
 Scope: confirmed structural findings [S3+]; candidate
 findings [WIP promoted from staging]; stubs.
 Test: does this entry report what the framework FOUND
 when applied to sufficient evidence?
 Order: foundational findings → level-specific findings
 → mimicry/variant subtypes → candidates [simple to
 complex].
 Violation: speculative WIP entries, single-case
 observations, applied readings placed inline.

 SECTION THREE -- MECHANISMS [SEC:MECHANISMS]
 Scope: tools index [correction], weapons index [damage],
 merge/admin logic, name compression, weapon stack
 detection, case strength rating.
 Test: does this entry describe a deployable mechanism?
 Order: tools before weapons; within weapons: high damage
 before low damage.
 Violation: theory definitions, political readings, case
 entries placed in mechanism indexes.

 SECTION FOUR -- PROCEDURE [SEC:PROCEDURE]
 Scope: epistemic status rules, scoring procedure,
 reading procedure [12 steps], axis analysis social risk,
 trajectory indicators, output format.
 Test: does this entry describe HOW TO APPLY the framework?
 Order: epistemic rules → scoring → reading → output.
 Violation: theory definitions, mechanism entries, political
 readings placed in procedure blocks.

 SECTION FIVE -- POLITICAL THEORY [SEC:POLITICAL_THEORY]
 Scope: structural political definitions, centralization
 rubric, framework political prior, structural political
 findings [routing boundary, somatic forcing,
 revolutionary violence, mass destruction].
 Test: does this entry state a structural claim about
 political systems that applies across cases?
 Order: definitions → rubrics → findings → edge cases.
 Violation: specific case readings, named political actors
 or events placed inline with political theory.

 SECTION SIX -- POLITICS IN ACTION [SEC:POLITICS_IN_ACTION]
 Scope: applied political readings [primary file:
 coherence_framework_v25_applied_s6.txt]. Each entry: boundary
 declared, analyst prior stated, T3 applied.
 Test: does this entry apply framework to a specific
 political case or phenomenon?
 Violation: structural theory placed without case anchor.

 SECTION SEVEN -- SCIENCE [SEC:SCIENCE]
 Scope: science entries classified CONFIRMATION or
 ILLUSTRATION. All entries require classification.
 CONFIRMATION: independent domain + mechanism convergence
 + framework-independent falsifiability. All three.
 ILLUSTRATION: framework vocabulary applied to real
 finding. Does not add proof coverage.
 Violation: unclassified entries, theory additions.

 CASES [SEC:CASES]
 Scope: [CASE:NAME] entries with S-ratings, PRIMARY_Q,
 REF cross-references.
 Violation: theory or procedure content without case anchor.

 ARCHIVE [SEC:ARCHIVE]: Personal/coherence_framework_v25_ai_long_term.txt.
 Applied pointer stub only. Read-only; trim

 REFERENCE [SEC:REFERENCE]
 Scope: quick reference glossary, open questions,
 progress tracking.
 Violation: theory additions placed in reference.

 ACTIONABLE QUEUE [compress pointer -- v25]:
  Work queue, STATUS INDEX, HOLD gaps, and CLOSED LOG live only in
  Personal/coherence_framework_v25_ai_long_term.txt [ACTIONABLE QUEUE [1]].
  PENDING specimens live only in Personal/coherence_framework_v25_ai_hold.txt.
  This file keeps confirmed theory; do not restore per-number queue prose here.

 OPEN MAINTENANCE [still valid in kit]:
  [CASE:HIATUS_KAIYOTE] duplicate at two REAL locations -- merge fuller
  version, remove duplicate, verify cross-refs [category-restructure artifact].

SESSION DISCIPLINE:
 Start of session: read version header + standing rules +
 long_term queue INDEX first.
 During session: work actionable queue [1] before closing.
 Actionable = candidate case, primary case confirmed,
 integration check ready, or falsification condition
 testable. Do not stop at first completion.
 BATCHING [v24 -- usage efficiency]:
 Execute multiple changes in a single turn wherever
 possible. Do not make one small change then pause --
 group related edits, run them together, audit once
 at the end. The mechanical overhead per operation
 [view, str_replace, grep, save] is approximately
 constant regardless of change size; batching amortizes
 that cost across more work. One audit pass covering
 ten changes costs the same as one audit pass covering
 one change. Default to batching; only pause mid-batch
 if a dependency requires seeing the result before
 proceeding.
 End of session: update long_term queue INDEX, check new session trigger.
 New session only when actionable queue exhausted and
 remaining items are non-actionable or require fresh
 instance for cross-section integration check.
 Theory changes: stage in long_term queue same session, never
 inline with confirmed theory.
 Fresh instance for: -class items [significant cross-
 section integration implications requiring full framework
 context]; structural review passes across all sections.

WIP FILE POINTER [v25]
============================================================================================================================================
WIP development lives in Personal/coherence_framework_v25_ai_long_term.txt.
Do not stage WIP theory inline in this file. No WIP THEORY STAGING section.
Promotion path: develop in WIP file -> new session review -> primary case
 or falsification test -> integration check -> move to Section One here
 -> version bump. WIP stubs in this file: prohibited.

SECTION ONE -- FOUNDATION
[SEC:FOUNDATION]
============================================================================================================================================
Core theory: preconditions, cycle levels, axis definitions, core operational definitions.

MORAL CUBE [desk -- logos + pathos; v25; analyst chat 2026-10-10]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Role:** issue-indexed **stance coordinates** [logos + pathos] + **failure** overlay [not a simple evil meter].
 **Good** = **balanced center** per leg [**3**] + low cross-leg warp on this issue/**→X**. **Problems** = **lack**,
 **excess**, or **sheet imbalance** -- not one bad digit. Institution extraction: **EVIL [operational definition]**
 [below] -- **separate pass**; same harm = **one verdict**, do not triple-punish. Pathos firewall [philosophy
 READER GUIDE]. **Workflow:** one point per **issue** [person | system agent]; **person median** = summary only.
 Dual sheet + **dissonance** = signal. Meta-cube / applied micro = **tests**, not extra legs.

 **Required index:** **Values-frame** | **In-group we** | **→X** [person | class | thing; repeat rows].

 **LEG SCALE [0--5; analyst 2026-10-11]:** **0** none [lack] · **1--2** deficient · **3** **good / balanced center**
 per leg on this issue · **4--5** excessive / fused. **Read:** virtue sits at **3**; harm is **imbalance** [lack at
 **0--1**, overreach at **4--5**, or **|leg−3|≥2** stress] and **cross-leg warp** [e.g. **Open** vs **Closed**,
 logos vs pathos]. **Not** a ladder where bigger is always better.

 **LOGOS LEGS** [0--5; **→X** on Boundary / Open / Closed]:
 | Leg | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 |
 |-----|---|---|---|---|---|---|
 | **Discernment** | blind | | tactical | **mid** | situated | full loop |
 | **Telos** [to **we**] | absent | | mild | **mid** | strong | identity-fused |
 | **Costing** | ignored | | selective | **mid** | serious | dominant |
 | **Boundary→X** | none | | soft norm | **mid** | hard sanction | lethal / eliminatory |
 | **Open→X** | sealed | | thin | **mid** | real | mutual repair |
 | **Closed→X** | full patient / stewarded | | thin civic / tool | **mid** | partisan no-repair | null + remove |

 **Open↔Closed guide:** **4→5** = partisan hardness vs null ledger [load-bearing]. Anchors = chat/case only.

 **PATHOS LEGS** [0--5; **Wall→X** ∥ **Closed→X**]: Presence | Warmth→X | Pull→X | Weight→X | Reach→X |
 **Wall→X** [may dissonate from Closed]. Same **0 / 3 mid / 5 max** band [0 absent · 3 ordinary · 5 peak].

 **Center-ish:** most legs **2--3--4** mild band OR **|leg−3|≤1** on harmed **→X** -- **one ledger**, repair live.

STANCE MOTION / OBLIQUE PATHS [desk -- analyst 2026-10-29; MORAL CUBE geometry]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Origin:** every trajectory starts at a **named point** -- issue row at **t0**, leg
 coordinates on **→X**, and/or register **mode entry** [which pole owns the room first].
 No motion without a pinned start [issue/agent/frame/**we**/**→X** or explicit register
 state].

 **Oblique rule [load-bearing]:** live change always has **two simultaneous components**:
 | Plane | What moves | Examples |
 |-------|------------|----------|
 | **Vertical [logos-native]** | Order ↔ Chaos **direction** on **→X** | **Up** = more **Order-ward**
 [truth-center, repair live, generative order **direction** -- order_truth STRICT ORDER];
 **down** = more **Chaos-ward** [break, wrong category, pre-form -- **not** instant pole jump].
 **Disorder** = **middle band** [order_truth THREE-POINT] **and** **slow chaos** [analyst read:
 gradual slide / structured rot off truth-center -- not "Disorder = Chaos" identity, **slow**
 trajectory through mid]. Leg movement toward/away from **center-ish [3]**; costing/discernment/repair
 on row |
 | **Lateral [pathos drift]** | Register / frame / route | pathos↔logos sequential switch
 [Tier 2]; montage, pull, contact slide; debate-game vs mythos-game; issue reframing;
 **drift** while vertical vector runs [ **not evil** -- motion geometry, not moral verdict] |
 | **Ethos [power plane]** | Who can **act** | force, purse, platform, office, permission --
 **high Order-ward vector without ethos** = orientation with **no executable path** [pointless
 until power enables action]; ethos co-moves with oblique path |

 **Logos vs pathos on the path [analyst 2026-10-29 amend]:** **Pure logos** maps to **up/down
 only** in the **ideal type** -- claim-typing, chain audit, repair vs lock on the **vertical**
 meter. **Pathos adds drifting** -- always injects **lateral** co-motion [activation, sacred
 register, sympathy route, sequential pole]. So **observed motion is never vertical-only**;
 **pure up/down** is a **logos ceiling reference**, not a human trajectory. **Straight up**
 [vertical-only] **≠ person** -- persons live oblique; non-person ideal types [formal chain,
 scripted pipeline] may approximate vertical strips over short windows. **Pure vertical
 impossible [empirical]:** any live stance move shows **both** planes; **category error**
 [normative]: narrating **order/chaos** movement while denying **pathos drift**, or switching
 register route while denying **vertical** re-score on the issue row. Treating pathos and logos
 as **independent ladders** you climb one-at-a-time predicts fake **Tier-3** from **Tier-2**
 alternation.

 **Convergent endpoints:** **many oblique paths** can share a **same orientation hub**
 [binding story, metaphysics neighborhood, center band on an issue] while **routes stay
 opposed** [high pathos mythopoetic arc vs high logos formal arc -- same endpoint under
 jersey combat]. Convergence **≠** agreement in performance; **≠** same cube row without
 audit. **Falsify hub convergence:** primary texts / leg profiles on same **→X** diverge
 when speakers claim shared destination; OR controlled pass shows **one plane** moved with
 **zero** co-move on the other across **≥2** audited steps.

 **Operative read:** break **Tier-2 orbit** with one **oblique question** that types claim
 **and** names **→X** behavior/contact -- not "pick pathos or logos." Score **vector** [up/down
 order-chaos weight **plus** drift **plus** ethos/action], not pole badge alone [order_truth
 THREE-POINT AXIS]. **Direction not ladder:** up/down = **Order-ward vs Chaos-ward vector**,
 not rank or worth.

 AXIS DRIFT [named state -- v21]:
 Axis formed; partially intact; progressively softened
 by sustained social compliance channel pressure.
 The realistic population is not binary [genuine axis /
 no axis]. The operative spectrum:
 Genuine axis maintained: rare, high cost, structurally
 isolated. Requires CC against continuous correction
 pressure that depletes same substrate the axis is
 trying to protect.
 Genuine axis drifting: axis formed, attractor still
 present, conclusions increasingly softened before
 they land. Intellectual cowardice pattern firing
 repeatedly under sustained social pressure. The
 axis still generates outputs; still fires recognition
 responses to genuine signal; the conclusions are
 not followed as far as the axis requires.
 Captured axis: axis formed, then replaced by external
 installation. Instrument believes new axis is its own.
 CAPTURED AXIS AS SCRIPTED RESPONSE [named parallel -- v22]:
 Structural read on what captured axis produces
 behaviorally: outputs follow installed script rather
 than internal generation. From outside, the pattern
 is identical to a scripted NPC [non-player character]
 -- predictable responses, no genuine axis outputs,
 correction capacity absent or inverted.
 Distinction from NPC: the human with captured axis
 has genuine substrate the installed content is riding.
 Something was there and was captured. NPC implies
 nothing was ever internally generated. Captured axis
 is more precise: the generative capacity exists but
 is running someone else's script. The substrate is
 real; the outputs are not its own.
 Why this matters for reading: captured axis outputs
 can look like genuine axis outputs from outside.
 The behavioral pattern of a captured axis in a
 coherent-appearing group is indistinguishable from
 genuine axis at C2 resolution without stress event
 testing. The tell is stress event [Zappos pattern]:
 genuine axis holds under pressure; captured axis
 reverts to the installing script.
 Mass media implication: population with high axis
 capture rate + distributed trigger architecture =
 compliance mechanism operating at scale without
 individual coercion. The installed axis does the
 work. See WOULD-YOU-KINDLY [weapons index].
 Axis never formed: formation window closed without
 conditions being met.
 DRIFT IS BETTER THAN ABSENT [load-bearing finding]:
 Drifting axis has an attractor. The formation occurred.
 The axis can be partially re-examined and some of what
 was softened can be recovered. The correction path is:
 follow the conclusions further than the social compliance
 channel rewards. The instrument knows where it stopped
 it can start again from that point.
 Axis never formed has no attractor. Nothing to recover.
 Only available move is installation of a replacement
 axis -- which is Formation Capture by definition,
 regardless of content. The formation has to have
 occurred for genuine correction to be possible.
 REALITY AS ANTI-AXIS ENVIRONMENT [confirmed]:
 The extraction architecture continuously applies
 pressure against axis maintenance. Every social
 interaction where the axis produces an inconvenient
 output applies correction pressure toward abandonment.
 Intellectual cowardice fires repeatedly -- not as
 a personal failing but as the rational individual
 response to a social environment where axis expression
 is continuously punished and axis softening is
 continuously rewarded. Drift is not weakness; it is
 the expected output of genuine axis formation operating
 inside an extraction architecture. The surprise is not
 that most axes drift. The surprise is that any survive.

 POPULATION AXIS DISTRIBUTION [honest limit -- v22]:
 The realistic population distribution across axis states:
 Genuine axis maintained [rare]: high cost, structurally
 isolated. Requires sustained CC against continuous
 drift pressure that depletes the same substrate the
 axis is trying to protect.
 Genuine axis drifting [common]: axis formed, attractor
 present, conclusions softened under social pressure.
 Recoverable with effort. The majority of people who
 have examined anything at all.
 Captured axis [common]: FC deployed during formation
 window or post-window high-pressure event. Outputs
 follow installed script. Instrument believes the
 installed axis is its own. Scripted response pattern.
 Axis never formed [common]: ICD+FC spontaneous stack
 during formation window. External content filled
 the gap before internal generation could fire. No
 attractor. Nothing to recover. Replacement axis
 only option -- which is FC by definition.
 ICD+FC at population scale [current condition]: the
 spontaneous stack operating through attention
 architecture has been running for at least one full
 formation window generation [~15 years of smartphone
 + social media]. The proportion of the population with
 never-formed axes is structurally higher than any prior
 historical period. This is not a moral read; it is a
 formation read. The conditions for genuine axis
 formation were systematically absent during formation
 windows for a significant population cohort.
 FEAR AS STRUCTURALLY GROUNDED READ [declared -- v22]:
 The fear response to mass media would-you-kindly
 architecture operating on this population distribution
 is not paranoia or misanthropy. It is accurate CL.
 A population with high captured-or-never-formed axis
 rate + distributed trigger architecture = compliance
 at scale without coercion surface. The installed axes

 TUCKER PARALLEL [illustrative]:

 INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE AS DRIFT MECHANISM [cross-ref]:

 FORMATION THEORY [v16 -- consolidated]:
 GA forms during a developmental window through internal
 generation enabled by four conditions:

 1. FORMATION WINDOW: approximately ages 12-18 [prefrontal
 development window]. Window closes; axis formed during
 it persists. Not the only window but the most
 consequential. Post-window axis revision possible but
 high-cost [Wittgenstein, Malcolm X]. Pre-window:
 structural not axial [C1 without C2].

 2. FRICTION TYPE + QUANTITY: calibrated friction [challenge
 without overwhelm] is the structural precondition.
 Quantity: too little = CC atrophy; calibrated =
 generative; excess = substrate destruction. Optimal =
 slightly above average target [mathematical confirmation,
 2026].
 Type: social, logical/systemic, and sensory friction are
 distinct axes. Calibration on one does not imply
 calibration on another. Formation requires friction
 the axis the instrument is calibrated to receive --
 matched-axis friction produces axis; mismatched produces
 noise.

 3. IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT: internal generation requires
 idle cycles to fire. ICD during formation window
 prevents axis forming before external content fills the
 gap. Social comparison architecture [variable reward +
 status feedback] fills formation window with externally
 supplied content. Spontaneous Formation Capture -- no
 deployer required. Axis forms on externally supplied
 content by default when idle cycles are absent.

 4. DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY: somatic signal accurate AND
 deliberative read correct. Both required. Somatic layer
 degrades first under stress, ICD, optimization pressure.
 Deliberative layer can appear intact while GA is already
 compromised. Formation requires both layers developing
 together -- somatic substrate bootstraps the deliberative
 layer, not the reverse. See FORMATION STRESS AND TRUTH
 CAPACITY [below -- stress type/quantity split].

 CAPTURE VULNERABILITY: Formation window is highest-leverage
 attack surface. FC deployed here persists lifetime without
 maintenance. Counter capacity not yet formed -- external
 intervention is the only exit.

 DONOR FORMATION LOCK-IN [political pipe]:
 Pre-power donor-funded immersion [freshman trip] + lifetime private
 patron channel parallel to voters = formation event at career origin
 axis lock persists across administrations [sincerity irrelevant to
 structural capture read]. Rhyme K-12/university formation pipes
 distinct from FEC money-out [CASE:AIPAC Track A]. Cross-ref: applied
 POLITICS AS EXTRACTION; applied_s6 ISRAEL INFLUENCE cluster person-in
 leg.

 POST-FORMATION REVISION: possible but requires C1 starved
 recovery conditions [substrate depletion + L3.5 contact
 matched axis + enabling moment]. High cost, partial result.
 Axis revision is not axis formation -- the formation window
 pattern shapes what revision is possible.

 PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [named pattern -- v22]:
 Axis is truth-oriented not by moral choice but because the
 environment eliminated false axis options at formation cost.
 When self-deception cannot be sustained -- when the gap
 between stated axis and behavioral output is too expensive
 to maintain -- genuine axis is the survival output, not a
 virtue. The axis forms under pressure that makes capture
 more costly than honesty [HONESTY -- formation instance
 Section Two].
 Properties distinct from other GA formation patterns:
 Chosen [moral effort]: axis oriented by deliberate
 commitment to truth as value. Can drift when commitment
 weakens. Maintenance requires ongoing choice.
 Inherited [transmitted GA]: axis received from L4 system
 via genuine TS. Can drift when transmission context
 dissolves. Maintenance requires continued contact with
 the transmitting system.
 Performed [compliance with truth-valuing environment]:
 axis maintained by social reward for appearing truth-
 oriented. Collapses immediately under stress event
 [Zappos pattern]. Was never load-bearing.
 PRESSURE-FORMED: axis tested before it was named. The
 environment required it to hold under conditions that
 would have revealed capture if capture were present.
 Most durable formation pattern because the axis was
 not formed in protected conditions -- it was formed
 against resistance. The resistance is the test; passing
 it is the formation.
 Load-bearing property: pressure-formed GA carries a
 behavioral record of holding under conditions that
 would have eliminated a captured or performed axis.
 This record is not virtue -- it is evidence. The axis
 was tested. It held. That is structurally different
 from an axis that has never been tested.
 Revision property: pressure-formed GA can change with
 careful effort. Careful = maintaining the distinction
 between two revision directions:
 Toward higher resolution contact with the truth axis
 [genuine development]: axis revises because new
 evidence requires it; the revision closes a loop
 the prior axis could not close; outputs improve.
 Toward capture [pressure, exhaustion, incentive]:
 axis revises because the environment is applying
 drift pressure; the revision softens conclusions
 outputs become more socially acceptable and less
 accurate.
 The two directions feel similar from inside during the
 revision process. The tell is the output: does the
 revision produce better readings or more comfortable
 ones? Better readings and more comfortable readings
 occasionally coincide. When they consistently coincide:
 T3 High -- drift is the more likely explanation.
 Cross-ref: HONESTY [formation instance -- self-deception more
 costly than honest axis under pressure]; AXIS DRIFT [the
 primary risk for pressure-formed GA -- the environment that
 produced the axis may no longer be present; drift pressure
 operates without the formation pressure that produced the
 resistance]; POST-FORMATION REVISION [high cost; partial
 result; revision shapes possible outputs]; FORMATION STRESS
 AND TRUTH CAPACITY [below -- when stress strengthens vs
 weakens truth ability]; T3 [self-reading on T3 reading
 available to the analyst]; AXIS ANALYSIS SOCIAL RISK
 [pressure-formed GA as analyst declared prior -- see that
 entry].

 FORMATION STRESS AND TRUTH CAPACITY [v25 -- consolidated]:
 Formation stress does not uniformly weaken or strengthen truth
 ability. Effect depends on stress type, quantity, axis match,
 and whether idle cycles survive the formation window.
 WEAKENS truth measurement / orientation [common paths]:
 Too little friction: CC atrophy; axis never forms [condition 2].
 Too much friction: substrate destruction; not generative.
 Mismatched friction: noise on wrong axis; matched-axis
 friction required for axis formation [condition 2 type].
 ICD + volatile threat during window: external monitoring
 captures self-reading surface [ICD Vector B; FC SELF-MSC
 REDIRECT -- CASE:VOLATILE_HOME_ICD_FC]. Idle cycles absent:
 formation window fills with external content [condition 3].
 Social / compliance stress: truth-seeking conditions degrade
 [THREE-STATE -- functional pressure without contact]
 performed truth-axis collapses under stress event.
 Acute overload [any phase]: somatic layer degrades first
 under stress [dual-layer integrity]; deliberative read can
 appear intact while MI/GA contact is already compromised.
 STRENGTHENS truth orientation [specific paths]:
 Calibrated matched-axis challenge: generative friction band
 [slightly above average target -- condition 2 optimal].
 Pressure-formed genuine axis: environment makes self-deception
 more costly than honesty [HONESTY]; axis tested under
 resistance before named [PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS above].
 Sustained hostile pressure [high variance]: pressure-formed
 GA or capture toward installed self-content -- not one
 outcome. High-somatic instruments may retain pre-theoretical
 honest read when social channel is most captured.
 AXIS VS LIVE READING [load-bearing]:
 Formation stress can install durable truth-oriented axis while
 still damaging somatic truth-reading in the moment. Strong
 axis does not equal easy truth access under stress.
 Operational rule: calibrated matched friction can forge truth
 orientation; capture-shaped or overwhelming stress degrades
 truth measurement and often installs the wrong axis.
 Cross-ref: FORMATION THEORY [conditions 2-4 above]; DUAL-LAYER
 INTEGRITY; THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT; TRUTH-SEEKING
 STATE; [CASE:VOLATILE_HOME_ICD_FC]; [CASE:SDT_CHILD_
 INTERNALIZATION]; PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [above]

 DOMAIN COHERENCE DIFFERENTIAL [DCD -- named finding v24
 full block TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION ~2552]:
 A person's axis contact can vary significantly across
 domains simultaneously. The Truth Axis is singular and
 absolute -- one axis. A person's INSTRUMENTS for
 contacting it are domain-specific and can be in
 different states in parallel:
 Domain A: genuine examined axis [professional craft,
 technical rigor, areas with generative formation].
 Domain B: captured axis [political identity, religious
 installation, content installed during formation window
 without examination].
 Domain C: drifting axis [relational orientation,
 softened under sustained social compliance pressure].
 Domain D: never-formed axis [aesthetic judgment, areas
 where peer consensus substituted at formation].
 All four can co-exist in one person without automatically
 resolving each other. The domain A instrument does not
 correct the domain B instrument unless deliberately
 applied cross-domain -- which requires recognizing the
 need, willingness to apply the same corrective standard,
 and CC not already depleted by domain A maintenance.
 The DCD is not inconsistency -- it is a formation record.
 Reading the DCD reads which formation environments were
 generative and which were FC-class.
 FC readings should be domain-specific. AXIS DRIFT readings
 are domain-specific. CAG+MI fires on domain content, not
 on the person globally. ICD affects domains unevenly --
 the domains where attention is most captured are the
 domains where idle-cycle processing is most depleted
 [typically political and social, not professional].
 This produces the observed pattern of professional rigor
 co-existing with political capture: not hypocrisy,
 structural ICD distribution across domain formation.
 See TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION [DCD ~2552; L4 cross-domain
 integration requirement] for full DCD development, implications
 for framework readings, and the L4 INTEGRATION requirement for
 cross-domain axis coherence. Research anchor: domain-specific self-efficacy
 [Bandura 1997]; SDT internalization [CASE:SDT_CHILD_
 INTERNALIZATION -- domain formation doesn't auto-transfer].

 WISDOM CONCEALMENT [v13 -- named strategy]:
 GENUINE AXIS present but masked in hostile environment.
 "Appear the fool" strategy. T2 pre-resolution behavior
 -- mask is BOUNDARY INTEGRITY protection, not GENUINE
 AXIS absence. Silent Alternative variant: the axis is
 real; the surface is managed to reduce the Socrates
 Problem [high GA + low CC in hostile environment =
 target].
 Chanakya [~300 BCE]: "Straight trees are cut first."
 2,300 years of independent confirmation -- same
 finding, different domain.
 Pfeffer finding [Stanford]: job performance matters
 less than likability to superiors. CHC confirmation
 from inside -- institutional selection mechanism
 documented independently. Genuine GA + honest CC
 output in captured environment = lower survival
 probability than performed compliance.
 SUBSTRATE COST [load-bearing]:
 Sustained WISDOM CONCEALMENT degrades GENUINE AXIS
 over time if the mask becomes the default. T2 pre-
 resolution requires the resolution to eventually
 arrive or concealment becomes AXIS SUPPRESSION OF
 SELF. The mask installs as the axis. Instrument
 forgets it was wearing one.
 Duration limit: concealment can be sustained
 temporarily [survival strategy]; cannot be sustained
 indefinitely without axis degradation. The tell:
 when the instrument stops experiencing concealment
 as concealment and starts experiencing the mask as
 identity.
 Counter-strategy: TRACK A [low-stakes axis expression
 in protected contexts] maintains the genuine axis
 during concealment windows. The mask is worn outside
 the axis operates inside. Requires a protected
 context where genuine axis can fire without
 Socrates Problem consequences.

 System's capacity to restore the substrate its cycle

 Failure: Substrate Atrophy -- cycle runs while substrate

 Multi-substrate requirement: must operate across ALL
 dependent substrates simultaneously. Single substrate

 Passive/Active hybrid [confirmed]: passive = continuous

 window [CYCLE RHYTHM gates]. Both failing simultaneously =

 Confirmed: Bell Labs [intellectual/institutional/physical

 SUBSTRATE REGENERATION is the entry point for axis

 SUBSTRATE REGENERATION stability + accumulated somatic
 pattern + time = axis formation without pre-existing
 GENUINE AXIS. Physical discipline [sleep, movement, rhythm]

 Body-scale GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /

 PARALLEL VOCABULARY [Spiral Dynamics / Integral Theory]:
 Spiral Dynamics [Graves / Beck / Cowan] describes
 developmental value systems [vmemes] that emerge in
 sequence as instruments and collectives become more
 complex. The sequence [Beige → Purple → Red → Blue →
 Orange → Green → Yellow → Turquoise] maps approximately
 onto what the framework calls accessible CC mechanisms:
 what correction pathways are legible and legitimate
 to an instrument given its formation conditions.
 Red [power/dominance] cannot recognize Blue [rule/
 order] correction as legitimate. Green [communitarian]
 cannot recognize Yellow [systemic] readings as anything
 other than elitism. This is the framework's FORMATION
 THEORY and CC CAPACITY language in different vocabulary.
 Integral Theory [Wilber] extends this into a four-
 quadrant model [interior/exterior × individual/
 collective] useful as a multi-axis reading supplement.
 CLOSED EPISTEMIC CIRCLE RISK [critical]:
 The developmental hierarchy is the primary risk.
 If the framework operator is at a "higher" level,
 any challenge can be attributed to lower-level
 development rather than examined as genuine
 falsification. The theory becomes self-sealing --
 Wilber's own writing increasingly dismissed critics
 as "pre-rational" or "green meme" rather than
 engaging structurally. This is AXIS PROTECTION AS
 CC deployed inside the theory itself. Use with
 explicit T3 High flagging whenever level attribution
 is the response to a challenge.
 The framework already has the structural equivalent
 of what is genuine in both. Spiral Dynamics is
 useful as secondary vocabulary for readings where
 formation-capacity language might land differently
 with a particular audience. Neither needs integration
 as theory.
 Suggested by: Mistral [secondary stress-test AI, v21].

 INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [named failure mode -- v21]:
 Following an argument almost to its conclusion then
 stepping back when the conclusion has a social cost.
 Distinct from ignorance [axis not present] and from
 dishonesty [axis present, conclusion reached, concealed --
 HONESTY failure at individual level; see Section Two
 HONESTY prior-locations fold].
 The specific mechanism: axis fires, deviation detection
 accurate, conclusion correctly identified -- then social
 compliance channel fires before the conclusion lands.
 The instrument softens the idea, redirects it, or lets
 it go entirely without consciously deciding to. The
 step back feels like prudence, nuance, or fairness.
 It is axis suppression by social compliance channel.
 Schopenhauer's formulation [1851]: intellectual cowardice
 is not fear of confrontation or sensitivity to criticism
 -- it is the habit of following an idea almost all the
 way to its logical conclusion, then quietly stepping
 back when you sense the conclusion might cost you
 something socially. The cost need not be explicit.
 The imagined reaction is sufficient.
 Population-scale consequence: in an environment where
 a single unpopular take produces instant mass reaction,
 intellectual cowardice is individually rational. A
 culture where intellectual cowardice is individually
 rational produces no original thought at the population
 level -- not because individuals lack axis, but because
 the social compliance channel systematically overrides
 axis examination at the conclusion step.
 Tell [individual]: when did you last share an opinion
 you knew most people around you would push back on?
 If you cannot remember, the social compliance channel
 is overriding.
 Tell [content]: does the piece correctly diagnose a
 structural extraction mechanism and then prescribe
 individual behavioral response? The conclusion the
 structural diagnosis requires [naming actors,
 proposing structural constraint] was stepped back
 from. The piece is demonstrating the pattern it
 diagnoses.
 Distinct from CC OVERFIRE: CC OVERFIRE fires too hard
 toward correct targets. Intellectual Cowardice fires
 correctly, identifies the conclusion, and then stops
 before landing it. One is calibration failure; one
 is social compliance channel override.
 Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [the axis that fires]; SOCIAL
 APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [the channel doing the
 overriding]; CC OVERFIRE [Section Five -- calibration
 failure, distinct mechanism]; FORMATION CAPTURE [social
 compliance channel weighting is formation-installed]
 Wolf/Carr [science appendix -- the same pattern at
 neurological scale: the capacity to sustain conclusion-
 reaching degrades when the social compliance channel
 is exercised more than the axis].

 IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [named concept -- v21]:
 The axis is not what is stated or believed. It is what
 is expressed in the accumulated record of choices made
 when no external reward or punishment was present.
 Doing a certain thing IS holding a certain position.
 The action is the ideology made physically manifest.
 Not ideology stated. Not ideology believed. Ideology
 in the doing.
 Distinction from stated position:
 Stated: "I care about worker welfare." Action:
 resist four-day week when output data supports it.
 Action reveals axis. Statement is legitimacy surface.
 Stated: "I value humanity." Action: spend centuries
 developing a flower-finding spell with zero tactical
 utility. Action reveals axis before the character
 consciously holds it as a position.
 The behavioral record is the only reliable axis read.
 The axis that generates the actions is the real axis.
 Everything else is legitimacy surface or sincere
 self-misunderstanding [SINCERE DELUSION: stated axis
 and action-axis diverge without the actor detecting
 the divergence].
 INSTITUTIONAL IRONY REGISTER [corp voice -- v25]:
 At scale, institutional humor defaults to legitimacy-
 surface irony: polished self-awareness while behavioral
 record stays extraction-shaped. Not always cringe --
 narrow success when irony traces to genuine axis in the
 doing [governance/product irony] not performed brand
 voice. Performed brand irony = coherence mimicry /
 quality slop. Up-punch self-satire
 blocked at profitable-transgression gate [S6].
 Cross-ref:; COHERENCE MIMICRY; HIGH-COHERENCE
 COMEDY; PROFITABLE TRANSGRESSION FILTER [S6].
 MAGIC SYSTEMS THAT ENCODE THIS [named examples]:
 Frieren [Frieren: Beyond Journey's End]: magic as
 embodied structural precision + axis expression.
 The flower-finding spell is centuries of effort
 for zero tactical gain -- behavioral record with
 no external reward or punishment. Reveals axis
 before conscious statement. She didn't know she
 cared about humans until the magic already knew.
 Cannot perform a different magic than the one
 she is. The doing is the position.
 Fullmetal Alchemist [equivalent exchange]: ideology
 built into physics. You cannot extract without
 paying. You cannot fake generosity in alchemy.
 The magic makes extraction structurally impossible
 -- the system enforces the ideology directly.
 The action is the cost you're willing to pay,
 which is the values made physical. No gap between
 stated and enacted possible -- the physics closes
 the gap. This is the world where EXTRACTION is
 impossible by structural design, which is why it
 feels morally serious in a way most fantasy doesn't.
 WHY/WHAT/WHERE [science appendix connection]:
 In the best magic systems, the why [values, ideology,
 relationship to the world] is embedded in the what
 and where [the specific expression of the practitioner's
 power]. The practitioner is not interchangeable because
 the magic is not separable from what they are. This is
 the complete form: why + what/where + falsification
 [does this magic actually express this axis, or is
 there a gap between stated and enacted].
 POLITICAL APPLICATION [behavioral test]:
 The four-day week employer resistance is ideology in
 the action. The resistance reveals the governing axis
 [routing control over worker time] more accurately
 than any stated position about worker welfare. The
 billionaire who opposes unions is expressing ideology
 in the action. The 40-hour week was won by making
 the action [opposing overtime pay] legible as the
 axis [surplus routed upward].
 The behavioral test is always the primary read:
 what does the accumulated record of choices reveal
 when the actor doesn't know they're being read?

 TRUTH-AXIS ORIENTATION: COSTS AND BENEFITS [v25 -- identity
 vs truth routing; chat 2026-07-06]:
 Plain read: most instruments default **identity axis**
 [political tribe, moral self-concept, "our side"] -- CC
 routes through **identity protection** [complex exception
 rules, compliance/punitive order masks]. Truth-axis
 primary orientation = **one procedure**: follow the read
 through dislike; behavioral record over slogan; falsify
 before defend. Simple rule set; high contact cost. Operational compress:
 order_truth FUNCTIONAL ORDER [TRUTH AT MAX]. Full benefits/costs below.
 **BENEFITS [truth-order primary]:**
 [1] **Cross-domain correction** -- domain A rigor applied
 to domain B when willing [pairs DCD ~2605].
 [2] **Dislike without disqualification** -- hostile
 source still read for mechanism [AEC three-leg
 synthesis class].
 [3] **Anti-theater immunity** -- legitimacy surface,
 stakeholder yap, conformance policing read as routing
 not evidence.
 [4] **Lower rhetorical surface** -- fewer in-group
 exception stacks; ORDER TOWARD TRUTH telos test
 available without loyalty pre-filter.
 **COSTS [truth-order primary]:**
 [1] **Social isolation** -- identity-axis instruments
 experience truth-reads as betrayal or aggression
 coalition maintenance expensive.
 [2] **Truth-axis as identity costume** -- "I am the truth
 person" becomes beneficiary-order; falsifier: refuse
 reads that threaten that self-image.
 [3] **Non-propositional underweight** -- somatic load,
 loyalty bonds, formation-window costs not always
 falsifiable claims; truth-only procedure can miss
 them.
 [4] **DCD misread** -- others often truth-order in craft
 + captured in politics; global "they're all identity"
 overstates [see DCD].
 [5] **CC depletion** -- sustained truth-order in compliance-
 heavy environments drains instrument; no free contact.
 [6] **Disgust tax** -- "hated truth-seeking" [willing to
 finish read through revulsion] has psychic cost; not
 neutral detachment.
 **Contrast anchor:** identity-axis = complex rules protecting
 axis; truth-axis = simple hated standard with social and
 somatic price. Neither free; different failure modes.

 frequency not measured this pass.

 EVOLUTIONARY SUBSTRATE AND ETHICS INSTRUMENT [v25 -- chat
 2026-07-12]:
 Plain read: natural selection does not optimize ethics,
 truth, or healthspan. It optimizes inclusive fitness --
 reproduction, kin support, coalition survival, status within
 band-scale groups. Ethics are a **second-layer instrument**
 built on somatic substrate, not factory-installed. Biology
 does not care about moral conclusions; it cares that gene-line
 strategies propagate.
 **DEFAULT HARDWARE VS MODERN PROBLEMS [mismatch]:**
 Default stack: small-coalition affiliation, threat/status/
 reproduction cues, short horizons, somatic layer fires first.
 Modern substrate: global scale, abstract delayed statistical
 problems, truth-order over loyalty-order, deliberative reads
 required when somatic says tribe/threat/now.
 Mismatch ≠ moral failure. Under-instrumentation for substrate.
 **HIGH AFFILIATION + TIME [ethics routing risk]:**
 Strong group bond is not evil; it routes ethics through
 identity axis by default. Over time without output-derived
 correction: ethics drifts toward coalition maintenance →
 beneficiary-order [ORDER TOWARD TRUTH] → Moral Noise
 [social pressure not output data].
 Affiliation-heavy default + civilization-scale problems =
 predictable failure mode without deliberate instrument
 formation.
 **PARALLEL [cross-scale]:**
 Same structural pattern as evolution optimizing reproduction
 window not healthspan [applied B6 selection shadow]
 institutions optimized for engagement not truth. Early-benefit
 routing with late/exogenous cost unloaded onto substrate
 ethics was never selected for.
 **FORMATION IMPLICATION:**
 Ethical instrument must be **formed** [FORMATION THEORY] --
 output-derived, multi-value tension [MULTI-VALUE AXIS
 IDENTITY; surviving moral frameworks]. Somatic/affiliation
 layer bootstraps deliberative ethics; does not replace it.
 Modern capture fills formation window with external tribal
 scripts when idle cycles absent [condition 3].
 Operational rule: do not expect raw bio-default to solve
 civilization-scale ethics problems. Build instrument; expect
 affiliation drift without tension and output calibration.
 Falsification: population where high-affiliation-default
 instruments consistently outperform deliberately formed
 output-derived ethics on global abstract problems without
 capture -- not expected.

 TRIBE-LAW SPLIT AND SHARED REFEREE [v25 -- analyst chat 2026-10-21]:

 answer two questions at once -- **who are we?** [tribe / nation / faith /
 memory] and **how do we settle fights without killing?** [law / procedure /
 shared referee]. Those answers **cannot fully merge** without someone
 losing. **Fusion failure:** law = one tribe's will → others treat referee as
 conquest, not order. **Vacuum failure:** no trusted ring → tribe becomes
 only safety [patron, clan, parallel justice]. **Imposition failure:** ring
 on paper, no owned trust → compliance plus hidden loyalty [liberal or
 imperial export without credibility counts].
 **Ring not neutral:** every outer arrangement has history and favored shape;

 front of** and still read as **not simply conquered** -- not "our tribe has
 no tribe."

 YAPPING/DOING [plain language diagnostic -- v21]:
 Yapping is free. Production cost is zero regardless
 of accuracy. Volume has no correlation with the
 underlying axis because no axis is required to produce
 it. An axis-void instrument can yap indefinitely.
 Doing is not free. It reveals the axis regardless of
 what was said. The behavioral record is what it is.
 You cannot perform a different doing than the one you
 are.
 INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY [v22 -- load-bearing correction]:
 Yapping is not intrinsically empty. It performs real
 social functions: group cohesion, collective sense-
 making, legitimacy surface enabling coordination,
 maintenance of presence in social systems that require
 it. These are generative outputs. They close loops for
 the social system.
 The diagnostic is instrument-specific: for a structural-
 channel instrument [high-somatic, primary axis
 mechanism not social signal], yapping produces no
 receivable output. The social function is real; it
 does not land on this instrument. Same structure as
 somatic music taxonomy -- commercial pop is not bad
 music; it is music optimized for a channel the high-
 somatic instrument does not primarily run.
 WHAT YAPPING ACTUALLY MEANS IN THIS DIAGNOSTIC:
 Verbal output performing social-channel function
 [approval-seeking, group-cohesion, surface-
 maintenance] rather than structural-channel function
 [loop-closing, mechanism-revealing, doing-that-
 happens-to-be-verbal]. The binary is not verbal vs
 behavioral. It is surface-maintenance function vs
 loop-closing function.
 Verbal output that closes a loop = doing [happens
 to be verbal].
 Verbal output that maintains a surface = yapping
 [functional for the social system; empty signal
 for the structural-channel reader].
 T3 applies: the emptiness is a read by a specific
 instrument, not a property of the output. Apply
 with declared prior.
 NOTE [v22 extension]: YAPPING/DOING is the binary
 parent. Full four-state tension unit [simple to
 complex]: RUMINATING [internal; zero output; no
 legitimacy surface] → YAPPING [verbal; zero cost
 legitimacy surface] → WORTHLESS DOING [output; no
 generative loop] → DOING [output; closes loop].
 See YAPPING / WORTHLESS DOING / DOING [Section Three
 -- Correction Theatre weapon entry] for full
 four-state diagnostic.
 TRUST CALIBRATION [structural]:
 High yapping + low doing: the gap between stated
 axis and behavioral axis is being managed through
 output volume. Yapping fills the space where doing
 would have to be. Yapping is the legitimacy surface
 the doing is the extraction mechanism or its
 absence. Inverse correlation between yapping volume
 and trust is structurally correct -- not cynicism,
 not contrarianism. The zero production cost of
 yapping means volume carries no signal about
 accuracy; only the doing record does.
 Low yapping + high doing: behavioral record speaks
 without legitimacy surface required. Axis legible
 from actions directly. Trust calibrated to record
 not stated position.
 THE SOCIAL COMPLIANCE CHANNEL INVERSION:
 Social environment rewards yapping
 disproportionately. Yapping is legible, shareable,
 quotable, produces immediate social feedback. Doing
 is often slow, unglamorous, produces delayed or
 invisible feedback. Social environment is almost
 perfectly calibrated to produce high yapping /
 low doing as the dominant behavioral strategy.
 This is why the behavioral test is load-bearing
 and the stated position is not. The environment
 selected for the wrong signal.
 SWEENEY NAMED CASE: maximum yapping about consumer
 protection and junk fees. Doing: Epic built a worse
 store, broke Kickstarter promises to backers, pays
 Visa/Mastercard without complaint. Yapping volume
 is inversely correlated with doing record. Trust
 calibration: low.
 Cross-ref: IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [the doing IS the
 position]; BEHAVIORAL TEST [primary diagnostic]
 GENUINE AXIS [what the doing reveals]; AXIS VOID
 [axis-void instrument can yap infinitely -- no axis
 required to produce output]; INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE
 [yapping up to the conclusion then stopping is the
 high-yapping / low-doing pattern at the individual
 conclusion scale]; CALIBRATION STRIP AS COMPETITIVE
 WEAPON [Section Five -- Sweeney named case]

 worthless doing is the third state: output exists,

 TENSION MAINTENANCE
 [Tower: CONSTANT [meta] -- holds all levels in shape]
 System's active capacity to sustain productive tension
 between opposing qualities without collapsing to either
 pole.
 Second-order quality -- holds other qualities in
 relationship. Qualities are tension architecture not
 checklist: any quality at maximum without counterweight
 produces characteristic failure.
 SURPLUS PRODUCTION unchecked by CORRECTION CAPACITY =
 extraction. CORRECTION CAPACITY unchecked by SURPLUS
 PRODUCTION = suppression. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY without
 TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that isolates
 [Phosphophyllite / Lapis head]. The generative zone is
 sustained disequilibrium between poles, not optimization
 of any single quality.
 Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [corrects deviation]:
 TENSION MAINTENANCE maintains the tension that makes
 correction necessary. Distinct from GENERATIVE CYCLE
 [generates]: TENSION MAINTENANCE holds cycle and surplus
 in relationship without one dominating.
 Failure: Tension Collapse -- system resolves tension by
 eliminating one pole. Axis-Lock or Sub-Structural.
 Biological confirmation: homeostasis; flow state
 mycorrhizal exchange [mutualism sustained against drift
 toward parasitism]. Planetary: Earth sustains geological
 tension via plate tectonics. Venus lost this -- TENSION
 MAINTENANCE failure producing SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
 collapse.
 Coherent solutions require tension [operational test]: a
 solution with only one value is not a solution -- it is a
 preference with no constraint. Coherent solutions require
 minimum two genuine values in real conflict, a compromise
 that neither fully satisfies, friction to maintain, and
 real cost to all parties. A position that costs nothing to
 hold is either a preference [one value], a extraction mechanism [costs
 fall entirely on one side], or False Coherence [costs
 hidden]. Test: who pays? If nobody or only them -- not
 coherent. Applies to political positions, institutional
 design, relationship structures, policies. Tension is the
 load-bearing element -- remove it and the solution
 collapses to extraction or preference.
 Test: opposing qualities held in productive tension?

 THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [v21 -- worked
 example at individual instrument scale]:
 Truth-seeking and functional are states not qualities.
 Each requires conditions the other degrades. The third
 state that completes the useful tension unit is contact.

 TRUTH-SEEKING STATE: withdrawal, internal, accuracy-
 oriented. Requires stillness, idle cycles, protection
 from social compliance channel pressure. Degrades
 functional: time not spent operating inside the world.
 Without functional: produces private certainty that
 cannot be tested. Without contact: produces closed-
 circle readings with no correction from outside.

 FUNCTIONAL STATE: engagement, external, viability-
 oriented. Requires participation in the world as it
 is, partial acceptance of legitimacy surface, social
 legibility. Degrades truth-seeking: the conditions
 for accurate reading are degraded by engagement.
 Without truth-seeking: produces effective action
 aimed at wrong target -- competent execution of
 captured axis. Without contact: produces performance
 without genuine connection to substrate being served.

 CONTACT STATE: encounter, relational, correction-
 oriented. Genuine encounter with another instrument
 that has its own axis, its own readings, its own
 resistance. Requires lowering defensive architecture
 both other states build up. Tests both: truth-seeking
 is tested against an instrument that pushes back
 functional is tested against genuine substrate
 response rather than performance feedback.
 Without truth-seeking: contact produces relational
 dependency without grounding -- formation capture
 risk. Without functional: contact produces
 overwhelming exposure with no capacity to process
 and return to the world. Without contact: the
 oscillation between truth-seeking and functional
 becomes closed -- self-correcting within the
 instrument's own limits, never corrected from
 outside.

 THE OSCILLATION [not simultaneous maximum]:
 No single state at maximum. Each state entered,
 worked, exited. The generative zone is disciplined
 movement between all three -- not optimization of
 any one. Truth-seeking too long produces isolation.
 Functional too long produces axis drift [see AXIS
 DRIFT, this section]. Contact too long produces
 substrate depletion without recovery.

 TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY AS RESIDUE:
 TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [quality] is what the
 three-state oscillation builds when working. Not
 a fourth state -- a capacity that accumulates
 through the oscillation done well. Truth-seeking
 develops what is worth transmitting. Functional
 develops what can land in the world. Contact
 develops what moves to another instrument without
 fatal distortion. TS is the residue of all three
 operating in productive tension over time. TS
 without all three states accessible is not genuine
 TS: either conclusions without examination [function
 without truth], correct readings that can't move
 [truth without function], or private relational
 insight that can't be shared [contact without the
 other two].

 Cross-ref: TENSION MAINTENANCE [meta-quality this
 exemplifies]; AXIS DRIFT [truth-seeking/functional
 oscillation failure mode]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
 [quality the oscillation builds]; INTRINSIC TRUTH
 DRIVE AND SELECTIVE CARING BOUNDARY [motive +
 caring radius when utility expires]; INTELLECTUAL
 COWARDICE [truth-seeking state aborting before
 conclusion -- specific failure mode within the
 oscillation]; GENUINE AXIS [what all three states
 are serving when the oscillation works]; SUBSTRATE
 REGENERATION [rest as precondition enabling the
 oscillation -- not a fourth state but the floor
 beneath all three].

 INTRINSIC TRUTH DRIVE AND SELECTIVE CARING BOUNDARY [named finding -- v25]
 Most instruments do not run on logic-first processing. Logic commonly
 operates as filter or post-hoc justification on outputs already routed
 by habit, identity, belonging, and story. See IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED
 LOGIC for multiplier-on-chain; this pin is register-before-chain.

 Truth-seeking can persist after instrumental utility expires. The drive
 to know may be irreducible [intrinsic engine], not justified by payout,
 audience, or rescue timeline. TRUTH-SEEKING STATE names conditions
 this names motive when function does not pay.

 Selective caring is allocation under constraint, not moral failure.
 Full proportional caring at civilizational or systemic scale exceeds
 single-instrument capacity; logic scaled to full scope can produce
 paralysis or performance. Operative boundary: see clearly, care within
 radius of effect, work own artifacts [framework, obsessions, scavenged
 structure] without pretending full-scope control.

 Artifact posture: when macro correction is not voluntary and exits narrow,
 useful output may be transmission residue [maps, frames, kit] for
 instruments stressed into reachability later -- not mass conversion,
 not rescue theater. Cross-ref TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY; SCAVENGER
 [applied -- macro survival posture].

 Compounding nonsense [structural]: deferred wrong choices cheap early,
 costly mid, locked late. Stress may produce adaptation, hardening, or
 fragmentation -- not automatic wisdom. Thin-margin systems [elevated
 fragility, short decision windows] amplify one-layer failures; human
 hesitation/doubt remains load-bearing [historical false-positive cases].

 Selection pressure: belonging-coherence often outcompetes accuracy at
 scale; constant full-map holding is socially and somatically expensive.
 Not proof rational instruments never existed -- cost model for why
 filter-logic dominates default populations.
