 DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS CENTRALIZED SORTING
 [v16]:
 In high-complexity, high-chaos environments, distributed
 iterative correction with slight bias produces more
 reliable output than centralized judgment. Confirmed
 across three independent domains:
 Germinal centers [immunology]: distributed B cell
 selection with slight bias produces reliable
 antibodies. Rare dominant clone model [centralized]
 is wrong.
 Scientific peer review: 60% rejection rate variance
 between reviewer sets. Centralized editor/judge
 model produces high variance regardless of
 investment.
 Distributed open-source [FOSS]: contribution
 procedures as embedded methodology produce
 generational continuity; codebase-only transmission
 does not.
 Implication for CC design: correction capacity at system
 level should be distributed with slight directional bias,
 not monopolized by central correcting authority.
 Centralized CC is the more capturable configuration.

 Friction axis [formation]: CORRECTION CAPACITY requires
 friction to form. No friction = no resistance = CORRECTION
 CAPACITY cannot develop. Calibrated friction = resistance
 = CORRECTION CAPACITY forms under pressure. Excess friction
 = substrate destroyed before CORRECTION CAPACITY forms.
 Generative window = middle range [challenge without
 overwhelm]. Friction is the structural precondition for
 correction capacity to exist, not incidental discomfort.
 Confirmed: Dunedin cohort [self-control at 3 predicts
 outcomes at 32]; Ericsson deliberate practice
 Csikszentmihalyi flow balance.
 Formation Conditions: insufficient friction during
 formation window produces CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy.
 Mechanism: CORRECTION CAPACITY develops through corrective
 feedback cycles; remove friction = atrophies. Applications:
 grade inflation [feedback removed], AI-assisted work
 without development [output without correction cycle],
 frictionless consumer environments. Individual effort
 cannot compensate -- structural redesign required.
 Mathematical confirmation [The Guardian / maths modelling
 2026]: "Slightly above average" target outperforms both
 "aim high" and "aim at average." Optimal target =
 calibrated friction zone. "Shoot for the moon" = too many
 failures, depletes CORRECTION CAPACITY budget without
 building axis. Aiming at average = insufficient friction,
 no CORRECTION CAPACITY development.
 Friction Axis quantified: generative zone confirmed
 mathematically, not just empirically.
 Excess friction [too much, not too little]: substrate
 consumed faster than it regenerates. CORRECTION CAPACITY
 cannot form because the substrate it would run on is being
 destroyed. Distinct from insufficient friction [atrophy]
 excess friction is active substrate destruction. Outcome
 depends on whether substrate holds through the window.
 Accidental formation: involuntary friction [difficult
 upbringing, deprivation, irrational environment] can
 produce the same CORRECTION CAPACITY development as
 deliberate calibrated friction -- but at higher substrate
 cost and with no guarantee of survival. Optimal is
 intentional friction without cruelty. Accidental excess
 friction that produces functional output means the
 substrate held, barely.
 Neurological friction example [non-specific]: conditions
 that produce involuntary sensory and social friction run
 the formation window hot by default. Either builds
 extraordinary CORRECTION CAPACITY [if substrate holds] or
 destroys substrate [if environment adds load without
 support]. Outcome depends entirely on whether support
 environment compensates for the excess load.
 Friction type [missing variable -- added]: friction
 quantity [too little / calibrated / excess] is not
 sufficient. Type matters equally. Social friction,
 logical/systemic friction, and sensory friction are
 distinct axes. Calibration on one axis does not imply
 calibration on another. An environment can have
 calibrated logical friction and catastrophic social
 friction simultaneously. For some instruments, social
 friction is not friction -- it is noise that triggers the
 panic response by default. The generative zone for these
 instruments is not in social domains. Some nervous systems
 read social friction as extraction because social CAUSAL
 LEGIBILITY is structurally illegible to them -- not
 misreading, different instrument.
 Somatic correction without CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: body fires
 correction continuously because the causal chain is
 illegible to it. Meltdown, shutdown, systemizing, and
 withdrawal are not failures of correction -- they are the
 correction. Body enforcing BOUNDARY INTEGRITY when CAUSAL
 LEGIBILITY is absent. Apparent dysfunction is the
 instrument doing its job without the chain needed to aim
 it.
 Reflexive threshold calibration [learnable]: threshold at
 which correction fires can be read and adjusted by the
 system itself -- not just direction or magnitude.
 Framework thresholds are currently treated as properties
 of the system, not variables the system can adjust. This
 is the missing piece. Calibrating the instrument, not the
 correction.
 Attention gap requirement [science appendix]: SR requires
 idle cycles. Default mode network fires only in gaps.
 Continuous input extracts the recovery window. Exhaustion
 is SR deficit not overwork -- eliminate gaps, eliminate
 substrate recovery.
 High-intelligence friction pattern [Psychology Today,
 2026]: Overthinking and difficulty transmitting ideas

 instrument]: friction type mismatch breaks somatic

 When routing doesn't happen the extraction mechanism misfires. Cannot

 Bidirectional read [v25]: same instrument also registers

 CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [precondition -- v21]:
 CC fires toward a target. Deviation from what, toward what,
 is determined by governing values and goals. Without an
 examined axis CC has no reference point -- it cannot correct
 meaningfully because it does not know what the correct
 direction is. An unexamined axis is not a neutral axis; it
 is a captured or inherited axis operating below the
 correction threshold. CC built on an unexamined axis corrects
 toward whatever the axis points -- including extraction
 targets -- without registering that as deviation.
 Precondition: genuine axis examination required before CC
 can be assessed as functional. Sophisticated correction
 procedures on an unexamined axis = CT, not CC.
 This is why nations and institutions resist genuine axis
 examination: the axis is the load-bearing thing. Everything
 downstream is superstructure. Touching the axis seriously
 destabilizes every structure built on it being unexamined.
 The resistance is structurally accurate, not irrational.

 Tell: the more sophisticated and functional the correction

 Most stable long-run configuration for extraction at

 extraction targets without registering deviation.
 Intervention: axis examination, not correction procedure
 examination. The procedure is not the problem.

 IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [named finding -- v22]
 Irrational behavior is not the absence of logic. It is
 logic operating under emotional weighting that produces
 predictable deviations from unweighted logic output.
 The emotional multiplier is traceable; the output is
 therefore predictable once the multiplier is identified.

 MECHANISM:
 Unweighted logic: input → causal chain → output.
 Weighted logic: input → emotional multiplier applied
 → amplified causal chain → output. The chain is
 the same; the multiplier changes the output.
 Emotional multipliers [named]:
 Fear: amplifies threat assessment. Risk reads as
 larger; probability of harm reads as higher.
 Output: avoidance, over-preparation, aggression
 as preemptive defense.
 Desire: amplifies probability estimates for wanted
 outcomes. The wanted thing reads as more likely
 obstacles read as smaller.
 Output: overcommitment, missed warning signals,
 rationalization of contrary evidence.
 Grief: amplifies loss salience. What was lost
 reads as more valuable than what remains.
 Output: inaction, comparison paralysis, under-
 valuation of present options.
 Anger: amplifies attribution certainty. Causal
 chain collapses to nearest available target.
 Output: misattribution, escalation, CC firing
 at proxy not source.
 Shame: amplifies self-reading as threat. Own
 outputs read as evidence of deficiency.
 Output: concealment, withdrawal, axis capture
 toward installed self-narrative.

 PREDICTABILITY:
 If the emotional multiplier is identified, the output
 follows from the logic + multiplier combination. The
 behavior reads as irrational only when the multiplier
 is invisible. With the multiplier visible: the behavior
 is the expected output of the logic under that weight.
 Framework implication: "irrational actor" is a
 calibration failure -- the analyst's instrument does
 not include the emotional multiplier in the causal
 chain. Add the multiplier; behavior becomes predictable.

 WHY "IRRATIONAL" AS A CATEGORY BLOCKS PREDICTION:
 Labeling behavior irrational exits it from causal
 analysis. If behavior is irrational [= not following
 logic], causal reconstruction cannot anchor. The
 correction is orphaned. The correct read: behavior
 is weighted logic; identify the weight; reconstruct
 the chain; prediction becomes possible.
 This is a CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure when "irrational"
 is used as a terminal category. It severs the causal
 chain before reconstruction can fire.

 ORIGIN [analyst case -- declared]:
 Derived from post-cult formation adaptation. Cult
 environment produced rational-over-emotional filter
 as correct counter to emotional installation mechanism.
 Cost: irrational behavior was filtered out of analysis
 rather than read. Resolution: irrational is not a
 separate category -- it is logic + emotional multiplier.
 Once that read was available, the filtered category
 became predictable. The resolution came from applying
 the framework's own causal chain methodology to the
 category that had been exempted from it.

 Cross-ref: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [irrational-as-category
 is a CL failure; weighted logic restores the chain]

 SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED CC SIGNAL [below -- adjacent

 SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED CORRECTION SIGNAL [named finding -- v22]
 Pleasure at another's misfortune is typically framed as
 a character defect. Psychological research [defense mechanism
 literature] identifies it more precisely: it fires when the
 direct correction pathway is blocked and an indirect
 correction event occurs. Not a defect -- an instrument
 adapting to blocked CC channels.

 MECHANISM:
 Direct correction pathway available: injustice detected →
 CC fires → correction attempted → outcome.
 Schadenfreude unnecessary. Resolution available.
 Direct correction pathway blocked [ECC high, power
 asymmetry, structural helplessness]:
 injustice detected → CC fires → correction impossible →
 correction pressure accumulates → indirect correction
 event occurs [the extracting party suffers a reversal] →
 somatic register fires [asymmetry reduced] → pleasure.
 The pleasure is the somatic discharge of accumulated
 correction pressure via an indirect pathway. The instrument
 is not broken; the direct pathway was closed.
 Defense mechanism framing [psychologists]: the pleasure
 fires when the direct correction pathway is unavailable.
 More common in people experiencing structural helplessness
 [cannot address the injustice directly] than in people
 with available correction pathways. High ECC + blocked CC
 produces schadenfreude as the available output.

 THREE CONFIGURATIONS [by structural type]:

 [1] LEGITIMATE CORRECTION REGISTER [most common]:
 Someone who benefited from genuine extraction [fraud,
 exploitation, unearned privilege] experiences a reversal.
 The somatic pleasure is accurate CC: the measurement
 surface reads that the system moved toward fairness.
 The instrument is working correctly.
 The moral discomfort ["I shouldn't enjoy this"] is
 social compliance channel overriding an accurate somatic
 read. Formation-installed moral rule [enjoying others'
 misfortune is bad] suppresses the accurate signal.
 Framework read: the guilt is the capture, not the
 pleasure. The pleasure is the instrument reading
 correctly.

 [2] ENVY RESOLUTION [middle case]:
 Pleasure at the misfortune of someone in a preferred
 position where the preference may or may not be
 unearned. Ambiguous because envy can be accurate
 [they hold an unjust position] or inaccurate [position
 reads as unjust due to comparison, not structural
 analysis]. Requires T3: is the asymmetry real or
 instrument-generated?
 Diagnostic: does the correction register fire when
 the preferred position was earned through genuine
 surplus production? If yes: extraction axis reading.
 If no [pleasure only at unearned reversal]: accurate
 correction register.

 [3] EXTRACTION SIGNAL [less common; axis diagnostic]:
 Pleasure at the misfortune of someone who has not
 harmed you and holds no unjust advantage. Pure
 extraction orientation -- the other person's substrate
 depleting registers as pleasure. The instrument is
 reading its own extraction axis accurately.
 This configuration is where schadenfreude IS
 diagnostic of axis problems. The defense mechanism
 reading does not cover this case. Distinguish from
 configurations [1] and [2] by: is the person a
 genuine extractor? Did they harm you or hold unjust
 advantage? If no to both: extraction signal.

 T9 CONNECTION [load-bearing]:
 The moral rule against schadenfreude ["it's mean to
 enjoy others' suffering"] protects the extraction
 architecture by suppressing the instrument's accurate
 read of correction events. T9 structure: the moral
 rule fires against the correct read [correction
 registered] rather than against the original wrong
 [the extraction that produced the blocked correction
 pathway]. The extracting party benefits from the
 formation-installed guilt because it suppresses
 somatic CC even when the CC fires accurately.
 This is CORRECTION INVERSION at the affective level:
 the instrument detecting extraction is corrected for
 detecting it. The extraction itself is not addressed.

 FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
 If schadenfreude fires indiscriminately [at random
 misfortune with no prior extraction or asymmetry],
 the blocked-CC-signal reading fails: not all instances
 are legitimate correction registers. The three-
 configuration taxonomy allows this -- configuration
 [3] [extraction signal] is where the firing is not
 a correction register. The falsification condition is
 met if no configuration taxonomy can discriminate
 legitimate from extraction-axis instances.
 Current read: the taxonomy discriminates. T3 Medium.

 HONEST LIMIT:
 The instrument reading schadenfreude as legitimate
 correction register may itself have an extraction
 axis. T3 applies: the pleasant feeling of "they got
 what they deserved" is indistinguishable from the
 pleasure of extraction at the somatic level. The
 structural analysis [was the person a genuine
 extractor?] is the only discriminator. If that
 analysis is not performed: all schadenfreude reads
 as legitimate. That is the T3 failure mode.

 Cross-ref: IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [above --
 adjacent finding; same structure: somatic signal
 reading accurately; formation-installed guilt
 misfiring on it]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [the blocked
 pathway this finding describes]; T9 [IRRATIONALITY
 MAINTENANCE -- the moral rule protecting the
 extraction architecture]; CORRECTION INVERSION
 [affective level -- instrument corrected for
 accurate read]; ECC [EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION --
 high ECC produces the blocked pathway condition]
 SUICIDAL EMPATHY [adjacent failure mode -- empathy
 suppressing accurate somatic correction reads rather
 than enhancing them]; FORMATION CAPTURE [guilt
 as formation-installed override of accurate signal]
 GENUINE AXIS [configuration [3] diagnostic --
 pleasure at random misfortune is extraction axis
 read].

 What the measurement surface is calibrated against.

 CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [v13 -- confirmed; L2 support]
 [Tower: L2 support -- CC structure]
 Whether correction capacity is structurally distributed
 across multiple independent points or centralized in
 single actor. Key structural distinction between
 dictatorship and functional nation-state. Between
 captured institution and correctable one.
 Distinct from CC [fires]; PR [proportional]; MI [surface
 honest] -- CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION is whether the
 architecture of CC itself is distributed or monopolized.
 Failure: CORRECTION MONOPOLY -- single actor owns CC.
 Once monopolized, CC fires only toward monopolist's
 benefit. The correction mechanism is real; the routing
 is captured. DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS
 CENTRALIZED SORTING [confirmed -- see CC block].
 FRUSTRATED DETECTION CONFIGURATION [named -- v22]:
 High INPUT FILTERING [population correctly detects
 extraction] + Low CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [CC
 monopolized] = mass frustration without correction.
 Population sees the problem accurately; cannot fire
 against it. Simultaneously: INPUT FILTERING degrades
 [sustained frustration without correction degrades
 the detection instrument] + CC centralizes further.
 Self-reinforcing until Diagnostic Collapse.
 Weapon: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [controls CC routing,
 not just CC presence]; INTERCESSION MONOPOLY [CC
 must route through captor to reach target].
 Test: can CC fire against the highest-power actor
 in the system without that actor's permission?

 CORRECTION TARGETING [v13 -- confirmed; L2 support]
 [Tower: L2 support -- CC aim]
 Whether CC fires at the actual source of deviation
 vs proxy or symptom. Distinct from CC [fires]; PR
 [proportional]; MI [surface honest]; CORRECTION
 DISTRIBUTION [architecture distributed].
 CORRECTION TARGETING is the aim of CC after it fires.
 CC can be functional, distributed, and proportional
 while systematically aimed at the wrong target.
 Failure: CORRECTION DISPLACEMENT -- CC fires correctly
 and proportionally but aimed at symptom not cause.
 Visible correction activity without resolving
 underlying deviation. High-energy; zero effect
 source. Examples: policing crime without addressing
 substrate depletion that produces crime; treating
 symptoms of overwork without addressing ICD; applying
 format corrections to a document with structural
 argument failure.
 Weapon: PROXY CONSTRUCTION [install decoy correction
 target; real routing boundary protected while CC
 exhausts itself on proxy]; ECD [make causal chain
 illegible so CC cannot locate actual source].
 Test: does correction land on the actual generating
 mechanism of the deviation? Or a downstream symptom?

 CAUSAL MEMORY [v13 -- confirmed; L2 foundation]
 [Tower: L2 foundation -- CL across time]
 Whether past corrections are retained and retrievable
 as usable history. Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
 [present causal chain readable] -- CAUSAL MEMORY is
 CL across time. System with full CL but no CAUSAL
 MEMORY cannot learn from past corrections; each
 correction cycle starts from zero.
 Failure: CAUSAL AMNESIA -- CL intact, past erased.
 System reads present correctly; cannot connect to
 pattern across time. Each extraction cycle appears
 novel. Each correction must be reinvented.
 Weapon: AMNESIA MANUFACTURE [actively erase past
 corrections from accessible record; destroy
 institutional memory; rotate personnel before
 pattern recognition develops]; GENERATIONAL RESET

 at civilizational scale].
 Primary confirmation: matriarch elephant CAUSAL MEMORY

 environmental history retained and deployed].
 Anti-case: organizations with high personnel turnover
 systematically lose correction history.

 to improve current ones? Or does each correction
 cycle begin without institutional memory?

LEVEL 3 [temporal domain -- TENSION CORE: TRANSMISSION

 TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
 [Tower: L3 CORE]
 System transmits correctable axis to successors, not

 TEMPORAL CAPTURE [named mechanism -- v21]:
 The mechanism by which institutions lock in extractive
 routing over time by making the cost of changing the
 path higher than the cost of continuing extraction.
 Distinct from FERTILE LOCK [which describes the state
 of locked methodology transmission] -- Temporal Capture
 describes the process that produces Fertile Lock in
 institutions specifically.
 Mechanism: each cycle of extractive routing accumulates
 switching costs [sunk infrastructure, trained personnel,
 dependent populations, regulatory frameworks built around
 existing architecture]. The switching cost grows with
 each cycle. At some point the switching cost exceeds the
 marginal extraction rate -- the path is locked not because
 the extraction is good but because changing it is more
 expensive than continuing it.
 Distinct from deliberate ECC: temporal capture does not
 require an actor to deliberately raise exit costs. The
 switching cost accumulation is structural -- it happens
 through ordinary operation of the extractive system
 regardless of intent.
 EXAMPLES [named]:
 Petrodollar infrastructure: 50+ years of dollar-
 denominated oil pricing has accumulated switching
 costs [contracts, reserves, settlement systems,
 military agreements] that make the routing change
 more expensive than the routing itself.
 Fossil fuel infrastructure: stranded assets, trained
 workforce, regulatory capture, and grid dependency
 all accumulate switching costs that make the energy
 routing change more expensive than continued
 extraction. The lock-in is temporal not conspiratorial.
 **Tension note:** spontaneous
 switching-cost accumulation does **not** exhaust the
 read -- disorder actors may **maintain** substrate lock
 on purpose [block refinery retool, jersey displacement,
 buffer raid] when correction window exists. Compound =
 temporal capture + deliberate maintenance; falsify each
 leg separately [Order retool without activation payoff
 weakens maintenance leg; structural lock-in without
 blocked retool weakens on-purpose leg].
 Visa/Mastercard payment rails: 60+ years of accumulated
 merchant agreements, terminal infrastructure, bank
 relationships, and consumer habits produce switching
 costs that make routing change near-impossible even
 after 20 years of antitrust litigation.
 TEMPORAL CAPTURE TEST: would the institution choose this
 routing architecture if building from scratch today?
 If no -- the path is locked by switching costs not by
 current merit. Temporal capture confirmed.
 PATH DEPENDENCE NOTE [Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
 The framework has strong temporal components [C3, L4.5,
 CARRIER ESCAPE] but temporal capture makes path
 dependence explicit as a named mechanism. Institutions
 that survive by locking in extractive routing are not
 simply failing to correct -- they are accumulating the
 switching costs that make correction structurally
 irrational at each additional cycle. The longer temporal
 capture runs, the more expensive the correction becomes,
 which is the structural explanation for why 20 years of
 antitrust litigation produces a 0.1% fee reduction.
 Cross-ref: FERTILE LOCK [state that temporal capture
 produces in transmission]; ECC [extraction mechanism
 -- temporal capture is ECC at institutional timescale]
 HORIZON EXTENSION [temporal manipulation of correction
 window -- related but distinct: Horizon Extension is
 deliberate; Temporal Capture accumulates structurally]
 DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [when switching cost exceeds
 correction capacity, the Glasnost pattern fires]
 [CASE:VISA_MASTERCARD_INTERCHANGE] [confirmed temporal
 capture -- 60 years of accumulated switching costs
 resistant to 20 years of correction effort].
 Add only if: [1] new confirmed structural property with
 multiple independent case confirmations, [2] promoted from
 extended theory [already vetted], or [3] resolves existing
 open limit or paradox.
 Reject: single-case findings, interesting observations without
 cross-domain confirmation, speculative extensions.

TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION [v25 -- supersedes NESTED CYCLE
 MODEL and TOWER ORDER as primary organizing structure]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 GOVERNING PRINCIPLE: SIMPLEST AT BOTTOM, HIGHER MORE COMPLEX.
 The axis threads all levels from below. Higher cycles have higher
 resolution contact with the same truth, not more truth. Complexity
 is the cost of higher resolution. A C1-only system has genuine axis
 contact. L4 has the highest resolution. Both real. Both valid.

 [FOUNDATION: THE TRUTH AXIS]
 Absolute truth. Present everywhere. Unmeasurable directly.
 Threading every cycle level simultaneously. No cycle reaches
 it -- every cycle runs around it. Not above the cycles; not
 below them; threading through them from base to highest
 resolution. The four conditions [GENUINE AXIS / DECLARED
 INSTRUMENT / REVISABLE ON EVIDENCE / BEHAVIORAL ADJUSTMENT]
 are not the axis. They are the closest available instrument
 for detecting whether a cycle is oriented toward it.
 Tests, not definitions. See CENTER / TRUTH [below].
 Costs/benefits of truth-axis-primary vs identity-axis-default
 routing: ORDER TOWARD TRUTH [TRUTH-AXIS ORIENTATION block].

 [CYCLE ONE: GENERATIVE -- C1]
 What: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
 Resolution: pre-verbal, somatic, behavioral. Corrects
 without being able to read its own correction.
 Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
 CORRECTION CAPACITY [behavioral].
 Tests: T1.1-T1.5 [proportional response, outward landing,
 minimum force, relational substrate, cycle stability].
 Ouroboros at C1: generation and consumption at biological
 scale. Surplus generated; entropy consumes substrate
 substrate returns. Circuit closes. Extraction = circuit
 break before return.
 Complexity: minimal. No reading surface required. Can run
 without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only. Valid.
 Lowest resolution axis contact available; genuine contact.

 [CYCLE TWO: EPISTEMIC -- C2]
 What: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
 Resolution: named, measurable, correctable within a
 generation. Can read its own outputs. Cannot transmit
 the revision process.
 Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
 REFERENCE INTEGRITY.
 Tests: T2.1-T2.3 [measurement accuracy, causal chain,
 reference stability].
 Ouroboros at C2: readable correction consuming and
 regenerating its own measurement surface. C2 break =
 measurement surface captured [MSC].
 Key risk: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False
 Coherence. Most dangerous configuration -- misread as
 healthy by C2-captured instruments.

 [CYCLE THREE: TEMPORAL -- C3]
 What: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
 Resolution: transmissible. Behavioral adjustment crosses
 generation boundaries via correctable method.
 Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING.
 Tests: T3.1-T3.3 [transmission method, axis reality,
 successor autonomy].
 Ouroboros at C3: transmission consuming prior forms to
 generate successors. C3 break = FERTILE LOCK [successors
 receive locked content, not revision capacity] or
 GENERATIONAL RESET [each generation reinvents].

 [INTERFACE: L3.5]
 Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
 Not a fourth wrapping cycle. The point at which two complete
 C1/C2/C3 systems touch. Resolution: recognition,
 compatibility, reciprocity. Requires two complete C3
 systems. See COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [ASYMMETRY TYPES].

 [LEVEL FOUR: SELF-CORRECTING TRANSMISSION -- L4]
 What: methodology consuming and regenerating itself.
 The system revises how it revises.
 Resolution: highest available. Can read its own correction
 methodology and correct it.
 Tests: INTEGRATION [correction methodology changes
 falsification]; CARRIER ESCAPE; GENERATIONAL
 RESET RESISTANCE.
 Ouroboros at L4: methodology consuming and regenerating
 itself. Highest resolution ouroboros available.
 NOT higher truth than C1. Higher resolution contact with
 the same axis. A bacterium and an L4 system touch the
 same axis. The axis does not change; the cross-section
 available to each differs.
 Failure mode: L4 MIMICRY [system appears to revise
 methodology; running Propagation Mimicry at meta level].

 [LEVEL FIVE: SELF-CORRECTING MORAL AXIOM EXAMINATION -- L5]
 What: axiom-revision capacity. The system can examine and
 correct the moral axioms that ground its correction
 methodology -- not just how it corrects [L4], but what
 it takes as foundational in deciding what counts as a
 correction.
 L4 asks: "is my correction methodology working correctly?"
 L5 asks: "are the axioms I'm using to evaluate my correction
 methodology themselves correctly aimed at the axis?"
 Resolution: highest available. Not infinite regress -- the
 Truth Axis is the anchor. L5 is the level at which the
 system holds its moral foundations open to correction from
 the same axis those foundations are trying to contact.
 Preconditions: L4 fully functional; genuine axis formed
 CC intact and undepleted enough to apply at axiom layer.
 NOT a precondition for L4.5: L5 is capacity, not scale.
 A single person can operate at L5 without civilizational
 reach. L4.5 requires civilizational transmission; L5
 does not.
 Failure mode: AXIOM CAPTURE [system applies rigorous L4
 methodology to installed moral foundations, producing
 correct-seeming conclusions from a captured base.
 Hardest failure mode to detect -- the methodology is
 genuinely rigorous; the capture is at the axiom layer
 below it. Tell: what the system cannot question, not
 what it gets wrong. Characteristic blind spots at the
 moral-foundation layer while producing high resolution
 everywhere else].
 Distinction from L4 MIMICRY: L4 Mimicry is fake meta-
 correction [system appears to revise methodology but
 isn't]. Axiom Capture is genuine meta-correction applied
 to installed foundations [revision is real; axioms being
 revised are themselves captured].
 Distinction from FORMATION CAPTURE: FC installs content
 during formation window. Axiom Capture installs at the
 moral-foundation layer and can happen post-formation --
 any sufficiently powerful epistemic environment can
 install axioms that feel self-evident.
 Falsification condition: a system claiming L5 that cannot
 name a moral axiom it has revised in response to evidence,
 or cannot name what evidence would cause it to revise its
 foundational moral framework, is operating at L4 or lower.
 L5 produces documented revision of moral axioms, not just
 revision of applications.
 Primary cases: CASE:LYSENKO [S:3 -- Axiom Capture at
 civilizational scale; rigorous internal methodology applied
 to installed dialectical materialist axioms]; framework
 design itself [S:2 -- T8 applies; GOOD/EVIL definitions
 explicitly held open to axiom-revision as structural design
 property, not as claim of L5 achievement].

 [THE OUROBOROS AS STRUCTURAL EXPLANATION]
 The cycles ARE the ouroboros at different resolutions.
 Each level is a circuit that either closes or breaks:
 C1 closes: generation returns to generative pool.
 C2 closes: reading surface regenerates via correction.
 C3 closes: methodology transmits revision capacity.
 L4 closes: meta-correction methodology revises itself.
 L5 closes: moral axioms examined and revised from the axis
 they were aimed at. The system that corrects its own
 corrective foundations.
 Extraction at any level = circuit break at that level.
 Honest destruction [predation, competition, entropy] is
 ouroboros-consistent. Circuit completes; substrate
 returns; new cycle possible. Extraction is circuit
 intercept before closure.

 [THREE-LAYER GEOMETRY -- from]
 LAYER 1 [TORUS]: C1/C2/C3/L4 cycle qualities + axis. State unit:
 TORUS [state unit -- v25].
 LAYER 2 [SUBSTRATE SHELL]: SR + SD. What the torus runs
 on. Shell failure: SR depleting while torus circulates
 [system appears healthy while substrate collapses].
 Shell arrest: SD fails [Accumulation Lock].
 LAYER 3 [TENSION FIELD]: TM + TD. Dynamic property of
 whole configuration. Failure: torus collapses to one
 pole [Axis-Lock, False Harmony].
 Axis 2 mapping :
 Down = Layer 3 absent [no tension field].
 Mid = Layer 2 present [substrate shell functioning].
 Up = All three layers active simultaneously.

 [DOMAIN COHERENCE DIFFERENTIAL -- DCD]
 A person's instruments for contacting the Truth Axis

 The axis is singular; the contact points are not.

 [2] willingness to apply the same corrective standard,
 [3] sufficient CC not depleted by domain A maintenance.

 axis contact varies significantly across domains, the
 differential is a formation record. Where the person

 political and social domains -- producing professional
 rigor co-existing with political capture. Not hypocrisy
 structural ICD distribution.
 L4 integration: cross-domain axis coherence is the L4
 achievement. Domain-partitioned contact is the normal
 state below L4. Truth-axis-primary pays social cost for
 cross-domain attempt; see ORDER TOWARD TRUTH
 [TRUTH-AXIS ORIENTATION: COSTS AND BENEFITS].

CENTER / TRUTH [absolute -- unmeasurable directly -- v22 revision]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Order/Disorder epistemic routing home: coherence_framework_v25_order_truth.txt
 [METHOD OF TRUTH; STRICT ORDER/DISORDER]. This block stays foundation.

 CENTER IS TRUTH. Not oriented toward truth -- identical with it.
 Prior framing [CENTER as unreachable asymptote] was correct about
 the measurement limit; incomplete about what the center IS.
 Correction v22: CENTER = absolute truth = the axis threading
 every level simultaneously. Real. Present. Unmeasurable directly.
 The limit is epistemic, not ontological. The axis exists; no
 instrument touches it without mediation.

 POINT EXTENDED TO LINE [v22]:
 Prior: CENTER as geometric point. Single location the cycles
 orbit. True at one resolution; incomplete.
 Revised: CENTER as axis -- a line threading through all cycle
 levels simultaneously. Not above the cycles. Not separate
 from them. Threading them. Every level [C1, C2, C3, L4]
 intersects the same axis at different resolution.
 Implication: L4 does not have more truth than C1. It has
 higher resolution contact with the same axis. The axis
 is the same at every level. The cross-section available
 to each level differs. A bacterium touching the axis
 through somatic generation and a L4 system touching it
 through self-revising methodology are touching the same
 line. The line does not change. The resolution of contact
 changes.
 Why the tower model was retired: the tower implied truth
 was higher up -- that L4 was closer to truth because
 it was above C1. The axis model replaces this: every
 level is equidistant from the axis in ontological terms.
 The difference is resolution of contact, not proximity.

 ABSOLUTE TRUTH -- UNMEASURABLE DIRECTLY:
 Absolute truth cannot be defined precisely. Only approached
 through tests and qualities. Any precise definition of
 absolute truth IS a cycle product -- a C2 or C3 or L4
 output, not the axis itself. The definition is the
 instrument; the axis is not the instrument.
 This means: the framework cannot define truth. It can
 only produce quality tests for detecting how well a
 cycle is touching the axis at its available resolution.
 All framework qualities [GC, CC, TS, SA, MI, SP] are
 tests, not definitions. They detect proximity to the
 axis; they are not the axis.
 Implication for the framework's own status: the framework
 is not a map of truth. It is a quality stack for detecting
 axis contact at each cycle level. High-quality readings
 do not produce truth -- they produce better orientation
 toward the axis. Case errors do not touch the axis.
 They touch the tests. The framework can be wrong about
 specific readings while the axis remains unchanged.
 Falsification of a case = test error, not axis error.

 CONSTANT:
 The axis does not move when cycles degrade. False
 Coherence doesn't relocate it -- cycles lose orientation
 to it. The axis is still there; the cycles are running
 on a captured surface that points elsewhere. Extraction
 does not change the axis. It prevents contact with it.

 DESK PARALLEL [note -- v22]:
 CENTER / TRUTH as to on [τὸ ὄν -- Parmenides]: "the
 being." Fully present, fully actual, no potential, no
 change. The framework's CENTER is the Parmenidean solid
 that the Heraclitean flux [ouroboros] orients toward.
 The paradox: to on as CENTER is the correct orientation
 target [unreachable but real]; to on as FERTILE LOCK
 is what happens when a system mistakes its current
 locked state for the absolute [stops becoming, declares
 itself arrived]. Same philosophical concept describes
 both the correct goal and the primary failure mode.
 The cycles [panta rhei -- everything flows] orient
 toward the center [to on] without reaching it. The
 apeiron [pre-axial boundless substrate] is what
 the cycles emerge from. Three Greek terms; three
 geometric states; one framework. See
 GREEK-TERM MAPPING [below, this file] -- not order_truth.txt
 **CLAIM-TYPE VOCABULARY**, which maps intersubjective claim-types.

TORUS [state unit -- v25]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Unit of measurement for state** -- the running configuration
 around the truth axis [P / CENTER]. Separate from FRAMEWORK
 SYMBOL [compressed visual] and from THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL
 [position reading]. Torus answers: **what state is the system
 in right now** around the axis?

 WHAT IT MEASURES:
 Concurrent C1/C2/C3/L4 flux around P. Circuit closure status.
 Not truth [P -- unmeasurable directly]. Not goal [eye -- orientation
 toward axis contact]. Not position [three-axis -- comparative
 coordinates]. **State** = geometry of the loop itself.

 STATE READINGS [unit values]:
 HEALTHY: circuit closes; flux regenerates [panta rhei]. Torus
 circulating; consumption real. Ouroboros-consistent.
 LOCKED: consumption stopped; mistaken for P [to-on trap /
 Fertile Lock]. Image of closure without regeneration.
 BROKEN: extraction intercept; circuit cannot close. Tail
 does not return to generative pool.
 PRE-TORUS: undirected generative [apeiron]; no axis lock yet.
 Nascent -- not yet a measurable loop around P.

 MEASUREMENT PROCEDURE:
 [1] Declare boundary, population, window [same as three-axis].
 [2] Read highest confirmed cycle level [C1-C4/L4] -- behavioral
 confirmation required.
 [3] Read circuit: closing / locked / broken / pre-torus.
 [4] If three-layer model applies: note shell [SR+SD] and tension
 field [TM+TD] -- torus can circulate while shell fails
 [apparent health, substrate collapse].
 Output: state reading + mechanism name. Pair with THREE-AXIS
 position for full instrument.

 RELATION TO OTHER UNITS:
 P [truth]: fixed center state runs **around** -- not measured
 by torus, assumed as orientation target.
 Torus [state]: this unit. **Not part of FRAMEWORK SYMBOL.**
 Eye [goal]: FRAMEWORK SYMBOL component -- see FRAMEWORK SYMBOL.
 Three-axis [position]: where on extraction/generation, coherence
 depth, trajectory -- complements state unit.

OUROBOROS [symbol component -- v22; revised v22; middle layer v25]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Symbol middle layer** -- one of three FRAMEWORK SYMBOL components
 [P · ouroboros · eye]. Serpent/circuit **around P**. Separate from
 TORUS [state unit -- measures whether circuit closes]. Cycle-level
 structure: TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION [THE OUROBOROS AS
 STRUCTURAL EXPLANATION]. Serpent circling the truth axis [CENTER /
 P]. Not circling a point alone -- circling a line threading all
 levels. Each level's cycle IS an ouroboros at that resolution.

 SIMULTANEOUS CREATION AND DESTRUCTION:
 Not sequential. Concurrent. The ouroboros eating its tail
 is already producing its tail. The axis is the only position
 from which both are visible as one act -- unmeasurable from
 inside any cycle. Getting closer = holding more of both
 simultaneously. L4 INTEGRATION is the highest available
 resolution. Still not the axis.

 EXTRACTION AS OUROBOROS BREAK:
 Circuit intercepted before it closes. Surplus captured before
 feeding the return phase. Smart extraction = slower break.
 Circuit still cannot close. Honest destruction [predation,
 entropy, competition] is ouroboros-consistent -- substrate
 freed, new cycle enabled. Extraction conceals the terminal
 routing; circuit shortens until collapse.
 Cross-ref [QC pointer only]: ABSTRACT REALITY STACK Disorder "best"
 torus -- greed singularity as fake center [not truth axis]
 generation costume → sink; useful orbit / madness contact
 [PERSONAL BIAS; not kit finding]. Distinct from this entry's
 circuit-break extraction.

 THE SYMBOL READS THE DEPLOYER [anti-capture mechanism]:
 Correct use requires holding: simultaneous creation and
 destruction, unmeasurable absolute truth, extraction as
 circuit break. That understanding requires the axis work
 the framework demands. The symbol cannot be used correctly
 without it.
 Captured deployment is self-diagnosing: the symbol depicts
 consumption as necessary. Fertile Lock, Propagation Mimicry,
 Coherence Rent all stop the consumption. The stopped circuit
 is visible in the symbol to any instrument that can read it.
 Decorative use [without methodology] produces a framework
 that looks like the ouroboros but is not closing the circuit.
 The image remains; the consumption stops. The difference is
 readable.
 TRANSMISSION TEST [derived]:
 Methodology still consuming itself across versions?
 Successors able to revise what they received?
 Framework producing better readings over time or more
 comfortable ones?
 All three required. Any one stopped: symbol shows the break.

TRUTH [quality tests for axis contact -- v22 revision]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Order/Disorder epistemic routing home: coherence_framework_v25_order_truth.txt
 [METHOD OF TRUTH]. Quality tests stay foundation.

 [REVISED STATUS v22: TRUTH is not defined by the four
 conditions below. Absolute truth = CENTER = the axis.
 Unmeasurable directly. The four conditions are quality
 tests for detecting how well a cycle is touching the
 axis at its available resolution. They are the closest
 available instrument to the axis, not a definition of
 it. All prior uses of "operational truth" remain valid
 -- the four conditions still function as stated. The
 epistemic status changes: they are tests, not definitions.]

 QUALITY TESTS FOR AXIS CONTACT [four conditions]:
 A cycle claim is touching the axis more closely when:
 [1] Examined axis [GENUINE AXIS]: claim produced by
 axis that has been tested, not substituted. At C1:
 pre-verbal somatic axis examination. At L4: meta-
 examination of the examination process itself.
 [2] Declared instrument [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]:
 instrument producing the claim is named and its
 blind spots stated before the reading. Undeclared
 instrument = unverifiable claim. T3 applies at
 all levels; naming it is the requirement, not
 eliminating it.
 [3] Revisable on evidence [CORRECTION CAPACITY]:
 claim specifies what evidence would change it.
 Non-revisable claims are identity statements,
 not axis-contact claims. Inability to name
 falsification criteria is diagnostic of axis
 capture, not certainty.
 [4] Behavioral adjustment when wrong [CC at available
 level]: claim that produces no adjustment when
 falsified was not genuinely held. Adjustment is
 the proof of reception. At L4: INTEGRATION --
 correction methodology itself changes. At C2:
 object-level correction fires. Both genuine
 scope differs.
 All four present = strongest available axis contact
 at current cycle level. Any one absent = axis
 contact weaker; claim less reliable as orientation.
 Not a definition of truth. A detection instrument
 for proximity to the axis. The axis is the same
 regardless of whether the four conditions are met.
 The conditions determine whether the cycle is
 oriented toward it or away.

 LEVEL-DEPENDENT CONTACT [v17 -- preserved]:
 Truth is not blocked below L4. Each cycle level
 contacts the axis within its available resolution.
 C1: generative but unreadable to the system itself.
 Behavioral adjustment through cycle correction.
 Pre-axial organisms operate here. Axis contact
 = cycle corrects against deviation. Narrow
 cross-section; genuine contact.
 C2: readable and correctable. Behavioral adjustment
 fires when C2 surface is honest. Cannot transmit
 the revision process -- corrections are local.
 Most examined individual axis contact here.
 C3: transmissible. Behavioral adjustment crosses
 generation boundaries via correctable method.
 Each successor can replicate the revision.
 L4: self-correcting transmission. Behavioral
 adjustment reaches the correction methodology
 itself. Highest resolution axis contact available.
 A C2 system producing honest behavioral adjustment
 IS contacting the axis at C2 resolution. Calling
 it inadequate because L4 is absent = demanding the
 highest resolution as the only valid contact.
 The axis is present at every level. Resolution of
 contact differs; the axis does not.

 SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT as structural requirement:
 Not an optional virtue. Self-critical capacity is
 what keeps the examined axis examined -- without
 ongoing self-critical pressure, axis drifts toward
 capture without the system noticing. The requirement
 is not "be humble" but "maintain the instrument that
 detects your own instrument's failure." Systems that
 suppress self-critical thought are not more confident
 -- they have eliminated the mechanism that would
 tell them when they're wrong.

 SELF-CRITICAL vs T9 [adjacency -- v25]:
 Close in topic: both concern whether axis correction can run.
 Self-critical thought maintains the examined axis from inside
 [this block]. T9 is the ceiling when honest admission would
 break identity, somatic load-bearing, or ideological container
 [HONEST LIMITS T9]. Intervention error: treating T9 as "need
 more self-criticism" or as T3 and applying standard correction.
 T9 resolution from inside may use axis grounding in behavioral
 record rather than legitimacy surface [CASE:RAND_DRAGONMOUNT].
 Full self-examination can coexist with axis-lock -- not a
 guaranteed escape from T9 structure.

 USEFUL REACTIONS as output requirement:
 Axis contact that produces no useful reaction is
 either not received [T9], not integrated at available
 level, or not about anything actionable. A claim
 should be evaluable by downstream effects. Does
 knowing this change what the system does? If no:
 the claim may be accurate but is not functional
 axis contact for this system at this time. Functional
 contact is not just correct -- it is correct AND
 actionable AND adjusted-to at the level the system
 can manage.

THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL [v25 -- promoted from]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Restores comparative position output to framework readings.
 Complete reading output: named mechanism [v22 vocabulary] +
 position [this model] + score.
 All three together = framework as full measurement instrument.
 State unit [torus geometry]: TORUS [state unit -- v25]. Position
 unit [this model]. Mechanism vocabulary [v22].

 STRUCTURE:
 CENTER = functional coherence [unreachable structural constant
 same as CENTER / TRUTH]. All three axes orient toward center.
 Position reading = distance and direction from center on each
 axis. Trajectory reading = direction of movement across window.
 Axes are directional vectors, not binary poles. Reading format:
 [A1 position / A2 position / A3 direction].

 AXIS 1 [horizontal]: GENERATION ↔ EXTRACTION
 RIGHT = net generation. Surplus flows outward to defined
 population. GC completes, SP positive, CC functional.
 LEFT = net extraction. Surplus flows inward to terminal node.
 CENTER = balanced [rare; structurally unstable].
 Measurement: net transfer direction across declared window
 and population. Not intent -- output reading.
 Positions: right / right of center / center / left of
 center / left.
 Layer mapping: Right = SD functioning [surplus exits shell
 outward]. Left = Accumulation Lock [surplus in shell].

 AXIS 2 [vertical]: COHERENCE DEPTH
 UP = C3-L4 functional. TS, SA, CAG running. Meta-correction
 present. All three layers active [torus + substrate shell
 + tension field].
 DOWN = C1 functional at best. C2 and C3 absent or collapsed.
 Layer 3 absent [no tension field; torus geometry unstable].
 MID = C2 functional, C3 partial. Layer 2 present [substrate
 shell functioning; SR and SD running].
 Measurement: highest confirmed operational level. Behavioral
 confirmation required -- claimed level does not count.
 Positions: up / upper-mid / mid / lower-mid / down.

 AXIS 3 [depth]: TRAJECTORY
 FRONT = moving toward center. Correction active. Gap closing.
 BACK = moving away from center. Gap widening.
 STABLE = holding position. Correction maintaining, not improving.
 Measurement: requires minimum two snapshot readings.
 Single snapshot = position only, no trajectory.
 Note: front is relative to center, not starting position.
 A system moving from left toward center is front even if
 still net-extractive.

 CONFIRMED CASE READINGS:
 RANNI [Elden Ring -- completion]:
 Coordinate: right / up / front.
 BELL LABS [1925-1984 peak]:
 Coordinate: right / up / front.
 Note: trajectory reverses to back at 1984 breakup.
 Post-1984: right of center / mid / back.
 WEIMAR REPUBLIC [Stresemann window]:
 Coordinate: left of center / neutral to up / front.
 Terminal window: left of center / lower-mid / back.
 CATHOLIC CHURCH: window-dependent. Period specification
 mandatory. Early 33-313 CE: right / up / front.
 Post-Constantine: shifts toward center / mid / unstable.

 OPERATIONAL PROCEDURE:
 Step 1: declare boundary, population, window.
 Step 2: Axis 1 reading [net transfer direction].
 Step 3: Axis 2 reading [highest confirmed level; behavioral].
 Step 4: Axis 3 reading [two windows minimum; compare positions].
 Step 5: composite output [A1 / A2 / A3] + prose mechanism
 reading + score.

 GREEK-TERM MAPPING [candidate -- adopt in readings]:
 Apeiron [sphere]: pre-axial, generative, undirected.
 No three-axis reading possible. Maps to Nascent.
 Torus state: pre-torus [TORUS state unit].
 Panta rhei [healthy torus]: full cycle, flux, circuit
 closing. Reading: right / up-to-mid / front.
 Torus state: healthy [TORUS state unit].
 To on trap [locked torus]: Fertile Lock, Parmenidean
 capture. Reading: [varies] / [varies] / stable or back.
 Torus state: locked [TORUS state unit].
 To on correct [CENTER]: unreachable. Reading: [0,0,0].
 Note: to on maps to both CENTER [correct] and FERTILE LOCK
 [failure]. The ambiguity is philosophically load-bearing --
 a system that mistakes its current state for the absolute
 stops becoming. Extraction's preferred endpoint.

 INTEGRATION:
 Axis 1 maps to GC/SP/CC [C1] + extraction definition.
 Axis 2 maps to cycle structure [C1-L4] + three-layer model.
 Axis 3 maps to CORRECTION CAPACITY trajectory.
 ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY reads on Axis 3: extraction
 must stay back continuously; generation only has to reach
 front once and transmit.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Moral **placement** desk [issue rows, legs, cube evils]: **MORAL CUBE** [Section One Foundation -- grep
 ^MORAL CUBE]. Same harm: cube pass + this block = **one verdict** [do not triple-punish]. **Cube failure overlay:**

 EPISTEMIC CEILING [v25 analyst amend]: good and evil definitions --
 operational and colloquial -- are truth-pattern reads at best
 proximity instruments toward CENTER / TRUTH axis contact, not
 fixed moral absolutes. Revisable on contact failure.
 Cross-ref: TRUTH [quality tests]; METHOD OF TRUTH; AXIS CONTACT
 TESTS [UNIFIED].

 Surplus routed to minority at majority cost, with correction
 capacity absent or inverted.
 Three conditions required:
 Identifiable Boundary B bearing the cost [majority].
 Identifiable Boundary A capturing the benefit [minority].
 Correction capacity absent or inverted.

 CC ABSENT vs CC INVERTED [v17 amendment -- distinction now
 load-bearing]:
 CC ABSENT: no correction fires at all. Extraction continues
 because nothing stops it. No actor owns the correction
 gap -- the gap simply exists.
 Intervention requirement: build or enable correction
 capacity. Can be done without directly threatening the
 extraction architecture -- correction is absent, not
 defended.
 Degree: lower end of evil scale. Extraction without
 active defense is more correctable than extraction that
 defends itself.
 CC INVERTED: correction fires, aimed at extractor benefit.
 Correction exists and is functional -- its target is
 wrong. The mechanism that would stop extraction is
 instead protecting it.
 Intervention requirement: counter-capture before
 building. Restoring CC into an inverted system fires
 the new capacity toward the monopolist. Building
 correction capacity into a captured system accelerates
 extraction until the capture is broken first.
 Degree: higher severity. Inversion means the correction
 mechanism actively defends against the populations
 that would use it. Inversion is harder to detect than
 absence -- the surface shows correction activity
 only the target reveals inversion.
 Both satisfy the three-condition definition. Degree and
 intervention path differ based on which condition holds.
 Degree scales with [1] magnitude of cost to Boundary B,
 [2] concentration of benefit to Boundary A, [3] how
 completely correction capacity is eliminated or inverted.
 [Implication: Diagnostic Collapse risk is highest under
 inverted CC. C2 restoration fires correction burst that
 is immediately captured and redirected. C1 substrate
 audit required before C2 intervention in inverted-CC
 systems, not only in absent-CC systems.]

 MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT [separation -- load-bearing]:
 Measurement: the framework reads structural pattern.
 Extraction occurring, direction of surplus, correction
 capacity status. Objective, structural, not requiring
 a judge. Framework work only.
 Judgment: a separate act by a person applying values to
 a specific situation. Evil in the moral sense requires
 this act. The framework cannot make it. A person must.
 Both required for coherent moral assessment. Measurement
 without judgment = structurally accurate, morally
 neutral. Judgment without measurement = arbitrary.

 PERSON-RELATIVE JUDGMENT [colloquial -- v25 analyst amend]:
 Under EPISTEMIC CEILING above: strict absolute definition of
 evil or good not available under uncertainty. Analyst colloquial
 register -- truth-pattern at best:
 Evil -- output or thing that violates what this person
 values; tag the thing/output, not essential person-
 label. "Investor brief became the game brief" = evil
 output read; not "investors are evil people."
 Good -- what this person likes or affirms under their
 values; same non-strict, person-relative register.
 Pairs MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT above: operational EVIL
 read [structural] + person values act = colloquial or
 moral assessment. Does not replace three-condition
 operational definition or WILLFUL EXTRACTION bar.
 Use when orientation needed before adjudication bar met
 or when analyst means value-dispreference, not metaphysics.
 Cross-ref: ORDER TRUTH DISLIKE-TO-EVIL ESCALATION [identity-axis
 collapse of dispreference into moral jersey].

 WILLFUL EXTRACTION [evil by definition]:
 Actor knows extraction is occurring. Knows the cost to
 Boundary B. Continues.
 Knowledge plus continuation = evil without requiring
 additional argument. This is the clearest case -- no
 ambiguity about intent, no structural excuse.
 Framework confirms the structural reading.
 The judgment is the person's to make.

 DIRECTIONAL RULE: any extraction moves in the direction
 of evil regardless of scale, intent, or justification.
 Degree affects intervention priority and response type,
 not directional classification. Minor extraction with
 intact correction capacity is less urgent than inverted
 correction; both move wrong.

GOOD [operational definition -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 EPISTEMIC CEILING: EVIL [operational definition -- EPISTEMIC
 CEILING]. Operational and colloquial good reads = truth-pattern
 at best; same revisability bar.

 Colloquial mirror: EVIL [operational definition -- PERSON-
 RELATIVE JUDGMENT colloquial]. Good = what this person likes
 or affirms under their values; non-strict; output/trajectory
 read preferred over essential person-label.

 Surplus distributed such that majority benefit is the primary
 outcome, with correction capacity intact and aimed at actual
 deviation.
 Three conditions:
 Boundary B populations bear costs proportional to benefit
 received [not disproportionate extraction].
 Correction capacity functional and distributed [not
 monopolized by Boundary A].
 Exit and correction options available to Boundary B [not
 structurally eliminated].
 Not perfectionism: good in the operational sense does not
 require equal outcomes or zero cost to anyone. It requires
 that the correction mechanism can fire against actual
 deviations and that majority populations are the primary
 beneficiaries of the system's surplus.
 Relationship to ascending path: good is not a fixed state
 but a trajectory -- toward more distributed surplus, more
 functional correction capacity, more available exit. Systems
 moving in this direction are good by operational definition
 regardless of current position.
