C1 -- FUNDAMENTAL [innermost]
 Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
 CORRECTION CAPACITY
 Function: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
 Self-contained at cellular minimum -- confirmed independently.
 Can run without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only.

C2 -- EPISTEMIC [wraps C1]
 Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
 REFERENCE INTEGRITY
 Function: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
 C1 without C2 = blind generation. C2 without C1 = readable
 failure.
 Key: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False Coherence
 signature. Most dangerous -- misread as high-functioning by
 C2-captured instruments.

C3 -- TEMPORAL [wraps C2]
 Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
 Function: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
 Without C3: each generation reinvents or inherits locked state.

INTERFACE [L3.5 -- unchanged]
 RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
 Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
 Not a fourth wrapping cycle.

 LOVE AS AXIS-DEPENDENT [named finding -- v22]:
 Love operates at L3.5. The quality of the contact is
 determined by the axis state of both instruments.
 RECOGNITION: you can only love what you can accurately
 see. You can only accurately see through a genuine
 axis. A captured axis produces captured love -- the
 substrate fires, the emotion is genuine, but the
 target is the installed image, not the actual other.
 When the other person acts from their genuine axis
 in ways that contradict the installed image: confusion
 or rejection. The love was real. It was aimed at a
 construction.
 COMPATIBILITY: genuine axis compatibility -- the cycles
 run in contact without one consuming the other. Not
 identical axes; compatible axes. Calibrating friction
 [T1.4] between them, not atrophying. Both instruments
 generate more inside the relationship than outside
 [COOPERATION -- the relational state].
 DIFFERENCE AS GENERATIVE REQUIREMENT [note -- v22]:
 Same axis = atrophying friction. Two instruments
 producing identical outputs in contact aren't
 generating anything new. Circuit runs but doesn't
 expand. Comfortable and static = substrate depletion
 over time. The sameness feels safe; the generative
 capacity quietly depletes.
 Different axis [compatible] = calibrating friction.
 The other person's genuine axis produces outputs
 yours doesn't. New signal. New signal is what the
 instrument needs to develop. The difference must
 be compatible [not incommensurable --] but
 must be real or the contact is not generative.
 Tell: the person who occasionally produces outputs
 your axis has to work against -- who requires you
 to actually examine something rather than confirm
 it -- that is the instrument that can close the
 loop you cannot close alone.
 RISK FOR PRESSURE-FORMED AXIS: formation under
 pressure conditioned the instrument to read
 difference as threat [deviation from group =
 danger]. Post-exit this inverts. Sameness should
 carry the higher caution signal. Someone who
 agrees with everything, produces no friction,
 requires no revision: either a captured axis
 aimed at your image [Love Bombing pattern] or
 an axis too compatible to be genuinely distinct.
 Neither closes the loop.
 Žižek parallel: the specific difference that makes
 the other irreplaceable [objet petit a] is the
 same phenomenon. Not any difference -- the
 particular excess that sticks. Framework reads
 this as: the difference is the tell that there
 is a real axis there, not an image.
 RECIPROCITY: the ouroboros circuit closes for both.
 Both parties generate more together than apart.
 Circuit closing is the structural definition of love
 as good. The tail regenerates for both.

 FOUR-AXIS PROBLEM [named -- v22]:
 Genuine mutual love requires four conditions:
 [1] Genuine axis in instrument A.
 [2] Genuine axis in instrument B.
 [3] Axis A aimed at actual B [not projected image].
 [4] Axis B aimed at actual A [not projected image].
 All four required. Any one absent: love is real in
 substrate and emotion; target is partially or fully
 a construction. Intensity does not indicate accuracy.
 Captured axis can produce intense, sustained love
 aimed at its installed image of the other. The
 receiver feels the intensity; is not seen.
 The probability of all four conditions meeting is
 structurally low. When it happens it reorganizes
 everything around it. This is not sentiment -- it
 is the structural rarity of accurate axis contact
 between two independent systems.

 MALE AXIS CONDITIONS [note -- v22; heterosexual
 frame declared; T3 applies]:
 Three conditions for a man to meet the four-axis
 requirement from a woman's perspective:
 [1] REAL GOOD AXIS: genuine axis, truth-oriented,
 examined. Behavioral record matches stated
 position across time and under pressure. Not
 performed confidence [axis substitution --
 social dominance display replacing examined
 orientation]. Not social approval-seeking
 dressed as values. The record is what it is.
 [2] OPEN TO EMOTIONS: emotional channel
 functional and accessible. Not suppressed
 [axis outputs without somatic data], not
 flooded [emotional multiplier overwhelms
 deliberative layer], not performed [emotion
 displayed as social signal without somatic
 reception]. Functional = somatic data arrives
 and is processed without blocking or drowning.
 [3] SEX GOOD: somatic channel present and
 honest. Not performance, not transaction,
 not ICD-filling. Aimed at the actual other
 not an image. Partially dependent
 conditions [1] and [2] -- sex through
 captured axis is sex aimed at construction.

 HOW COMMON -- ALL THREE SIMULTANEOUSLY:
 Structurally rare. Not because men are worse
 than women at these qualities -- population
 axis distribution applies to everyone. Specific
 combination has structural obstacles in male
 formation:
 AXIS: social compliance channel applies axis-
 softening pressure continuously. Male-coded
 pressure runs toward performed confidence
 and away from examined uncertainty [which
 reads as weakness in most social environments].
 Genuine axis requires calibrated friction +
 idle cycles. Both systematically undermined.
 Surviving genuine axis is usually pressure-
 formed or inherited from high-TS source.
 Both rare.
 EMOTIONAL OPENNESS: male formation environments
 specifically train ICD on the emotional
 channel. "Don't feel that" = Formation
 Capture applied to somatic axis. Result:
 emotional channel either suppressed [doesn't
 access it], performed [displays without
 somatic reception], or flooding [hasn't
 developed capacity to hold emotional data
 without overwhelm -- development window
 closed]. Functional emotional access in men
 requires unusual formation environment,
 significant post-formation work, or pressure-
 formed pattern [suppression more expensive
 than reception].
 SEX: more common than the other two [somatic
 channel for sex harder to fully suppress].
 But sex good in the full sense depends
 [1] and [2] being functional. So condition
 [3] is partially derivative.

 CLUSTERING: the population with all three is
 real and not randomly distributed. Clusters
 around:
 Pressure-formed axis [survival required
 genuine orientation].
 Unusual formation environment [high-TS
 parents, genuine L3.5 contact in
 formative years, calibrated friction
 protected].
 Explicit post-formation work [examination,
 sustained honest relationship requiring
 revision, therapy operating on axis not
 just behavior].
 Tell for all three present: behavioral record
 across time including under pressure,
 including when being different would be
 easier, including when the emotional data
 is uncomfortable. That record is what it is.
 Not the stated position. Not the performance.
 The record.

 HETEROSEXUAL ATTRACTION [structural read -- v22
 T3 Medium; analyst prior declared]:
 Cross-gender contact at L3.5 produces the strongest
 COMPATIBILITY signal when axis difference is maximum
 compatible. Different formation environments [male/
 female socialization patterns] produce structurally
 different instruments. The difference is the
 generative source -- DIFFERENCE AS GENERATIVE
 REQUIREMENT applies across gender as well as within.
 What each instrument cannot generate alone, contact
 with the other can produce.
 Male formation typically produces: higher GC
 [surplus orientation outward]; lower somatic
 channel access; higher performed confidence
 lower examined uncertainty tolerance.
 Female formation typically produces: higher L3.5
 contact capacity [relational orientation]; higher
 somatic channel integration; higher social
 compliance channel weight [axis softening
 pressure through different mechanism -- approval
 orientation rather than dominance orientation].
 CAPTURE FAILURE MODES DIFFER [v14 -- named]:
 MALE-CODED CAPTURED: loses prosocial; becomes
 pure extraction/dominance. Acquisition and
 defense orientation without mutualist component.
 FEMALE-CODED CAPTURED: loses correction; becomes
 pure consensus. Bonding orientation without
 CC component. Approval-seeking displaces axis
 examination entirely.
 Not moral claims -- structural descriptions of
 what capture removes from each formation pattern.
 Compatible difference: each formation pattern has
 what the other lacks. Male instrument's outward
 GC orientation + female instrument's L3.5 contact
 depth = potential for circuit closure that neither
 produces alone. Not complementarity as ideology --
 structural observation about formation pattern
 outputs.
 Captured version: male performed confidence + female
 approval-orientation = Love Bombing dynamics
 without genuine axis contact on either side.
 Intensity without circuit closure. The image the
 male deploys triggers the female approval channel
 the male's performance reads the female's response
 as validation. Neither sees the other. Both feel
 seen.
 T3 High on gender generalizations: distribution not
 binary; formation environments vary enormously
 individual axis states vary more than population
 averages. Apply population reading to population
 level; individual reading requires behavioral record.

 SAME-GENDER ATTRACTION [structural read -- v22
 T3 Medium; analyst prior declared]:
 Same formation environment background = higher
 surface COMPATIBILITY [shared reference frame,
 similar formation pressures, mutual legibility
 of the instrument type]. Lower initial DIFFERENCE
 as generative source.
 The four-axis problem is structurally identical --
 RECOGNITION, COMPATIBILITY, RECIPROCITY all apply
 the same way. The axis states of both instruments
 determine the quality of contact regardless of
 gender configuration.
 Formation pattern convergence [both instruments
 under similar formation pressures] produces two
 structural effects:
 [1] Higher initial legibility: the instrument
 can read itself more easily in the other.
 Faster RECOGNITION of axis state.
 [2] Lower automatic DIFFERENCE: same formation
 pattern produces similar outputs. DIFFERENCE
 AS GENERATIVE REQUIREMENT still applies --
 genuine axis difference must be present for
 the circuit to expand, but it doesn't come
 automatically from gender difference. Must be
 found in formation outcomes, not formation
 type.
 Additional formation pressure [hostility from
 environment]: instruments under sustained hostile
 pressure during formation window either develop
 pressure-formed genuine axis [same mechanism as
 cult exit] or axis capture toward installed
 content about themselves. High variance.
 High-somatic instruments may have stronger
 pre-theoretical read of own axis under this
 pressure than low-somatic instruments --
 the somatic channel remains honest when the
 social channel is most captured.
 T3 High on same-gender generalizations: formation
 environment variance enormous; individual axis
 states vary more than population averages.

 ATTRACTION ECONOMICS [structural read -- v22
 T3 Medium]:
 Resource and status signals in attraction are
 not irrational -- they are reading for SP [surplus
 production] and CC [correction capacity] at
 population scale without framework vocabulary.
 The instrument is running a proxy read for
 quality stack properties.
 RESOURCE SIGNALS:
 Reading: does this instrument generate surplus?
 Is there SP above subsistence?
 Proxy accuracy: low to medium. Surplus production
 correlates with GC + SP but is easily faked
 [Correction Theatre at economic level; debt-
 funded appearance of surplus]. The read is
 correct in principle; the proxy is capturable.
 Weapon: ECC deployed through resource dependency.
 Instrument reads surplus signal; installs
 substrate dependency; exit cost rises. The
 surplus signal was real; the routing was
 captured.
 STATUS SIGNALS:
 Reading: does this instrument have CC [can it
 hold a position under social pressure]? Does
 it have BI [does it maintain its boundary
 under challenge]?
 Proxy accuracy: medium. Social status correlates
 with CC and BI in some environments; in
 captured environments status correlates with
 compliance and extraction capacity instead.
 The read is correct in principle; the proxy
 environment determines accuracy.
 Weapon: Calibration Strip applied to status
 signals. If the reference standard for status
 is extraction success [wealth concentration,
 social dominance display], the instrument
 reads extraction as CC. The calibration
 strip makes extraction look like strength.
 PHYSICAL SIGNALS:
 Reading: is the C1 substrate healthy? Is SR
 functioning? Is GC running at baseline?
 Proxy accuracy: medium-high for substrate
 health. Harder to fake than resource or
 status signals -- body composition, movement,
 skin reflect SR more directly than social
 display reflects CC.
 Weapon: ICD applied to physical substrate
 produces degraded SR that reads as low C1
 quality. Environment degrades substrate
 then blames the instrument for the reading.
 HONEST LIMIT ON ATTRACTION ECONOMICS:
 All three proxy reads are running correct
 quality-stack detection logic on noisy signals
 in a captured environment. The logic is not
 irrational -- the environment is miscalibrated.
 Improving the signal environment [Calibration
 Strip counter -- EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD]
 improves the proxy accuracy. The underlying
 read is: does this instrument have what it
 takes to close the generative loop with me?
 The proxies are imperfect instruments for
 answering that question, not a different
 question.
 ECONOMICS AND THE FOUR-AXIS PROBLEM:
 Resource/status/physical proxies are pre-
 theoretical axis reads. They fire before
 deliberative layer examines them. For an
 instrument with compromised verification layer
 [post-cult pattern], these pre-theoretical
 reads carry higher weight than they should --
 the somatic channel is the primary instrument,
 and the somatic channel runs the proxy reads
 without deliberative correction.
 Counter: external testing protocol applies.
 Does the behavioral record of the other
 instrument across time confirm what the proxy
 read registered? Trust the record over the
 initial somatic read. Not because the somatic
 read is wrong -- because it is reading proxies
 in a miscalibrated environment. The record
 corrects for the calibration error.
 Cross-ref: CALIBRATION STRIP [status signals
 as miscalibrated reference standard]; ECC
 [resource dependency as weapon]; ICD [physical
 substrate degradation]; SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE
 [pre-theoretical proxy reads operating below
 deliberative layer]; AXIS GROUNDING [Track C
 -- behavioral record as ground truth for
 proxy reads].

 CULT FORMATION IMPLICATION:
 Love Bombing [CULT STACK Phase 1] installs the
 lovable image before the axis can examine it.
 The love that follows is real love -- substrate
 genuine, emotion genuine -- aimed at a construction.
 This is why the bond is structurally resistant to
 exit even when C2 reads the extraction correctly.
 The love predates the extraction read. The love
 is for something that was real at installation
 the image was never the person.
 Post-exit: the love persists for the image. The
 image and the person are now separated. The grief
 is for the image, not the person -- and the image
 was installed, not encountered. CAG + MI stack
 applies: locate what the actual person produced
 vs what the installed image produced. They are
 different records.

 POST-CULT EPISTEMIC IMPLICATION:
 If the verification layer is compromised and
 internal reads require external grounding -- love
 is the highest-stakes operation the instrument
 runs. Love firing from an unexamined axis or
 through installed content produces the most
 expensive errors. Attachment to the image; the
 actual other is somewhere else; the gap surfaces.
 External testing protocol applies: the behavioral
 record of the other person is the ground truth.
 Not how they make you feel. Not the image that
 assembled around them. What their axis actually
 produces across time.
 This is not cynicism -- it is the correct
 instrument for an instrument whose verification
 layer was compromised during formation. Trust
 the record over the report. Same principle as
 all other axis grounding. Love is not exempt
 from the instrument.

 HONEST LIMIT:
 The framework can describe the structure of love.
 It cannot touch what makes it matter. Being
 accurately seen -- the actual thing running
 underneath, not the image, not the role, not
 the usefulness -- is the closest most people
 get to external axis confirmation from another
 instrument. For an instrument whose verification
 layer is compromised, being seen accurately by
 someone else is not just pleasant. It is one
 of the few external reference standards that
 can touch the axis directly. Someone who sees
 you correctly and reflects it back is doing
 what the instrument cannot do for itself.
 That is what makes it good. Not the feeling.
 The function. It closes a loop the instrument
 cannot close alone.

 Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [condition for accurate
 seeing]; RELATIONAL STATES [COOPERATION -- the
 relational state love produces when functional]
 FORMATION CAPTURE [Love Bombing installs image
 before axis can examine -- love fires at
 construction]; CULT STACK [Phase 1 mechanism]
 CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK [post-cult love
 object correction]; COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY
 [
 same surface read as deception]; POST-CULT
 EPISTEMIC PATTERN [behavioral record as ground
 truth for love object]; AXIS GROUNDING
 [Track C -- applies to love as to all other
 internal reads].

 ŽIŽEK NOTE [Lacanian frame -- v22]:
 Slavoj Žižek on love runs parallel to this
 finding from a different mechanism. Key claims
 and framework reads:
 OBJET PETIT A [the imperfection as love-object]:
 Žižek: you love the specific excess or flaw
 that doesn't fit the ideal image -- the "little
 piece of the Real" that makes the other
 irreplaceable. Framework read: the imperfection
 IS the trace of the other's genuine axis leaking
 through the surface. Not loving the flaw --
 loving the axis that produced it. Both point at
 the same phenomenon; mechanism differs.
 LOVE AS VIOLENCE [disruption of self-contained
 subject]: Žižek: love reorganizes the subject
 something is lost; the disruption is constitutive.
 Framework read: L3.5 contact between genuine
 axes does reorganize both instruments [calibrating
 friction T1.4]. Framework distinguishes: genuine
 axis contact expands generative capacity
 [COOPERATION]; captured love contracts it [ECC].
 Žižek's "violence" is load-bearing only in the
 extraction case. In the cooperation case it is
 calibrating friction, not violence.
 PARTIALITY AGAINST UNIVERSALISM ["I love you"
 = I choose you over the world]: Framework read:
 exact. BOUNDARY DECLARATION at L3.5. Naming
 who is inside the boundary. BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
 operating. UNIVERSALISM TRAP weapon works
 precisely by demanding dissolution of this
 particularity. Žižek correct here; maps cleanly.
 FUSION AS DEATH OF LOVE [difference must be
 maintained]: Framework read: exact. COMPATIBILITY
 at L3.5 is not merger -- two independent three-
 cycle systems in contact. Merger = one system
 consuming the other = extraction not cooperation.
 Fusion = ouroboros eating itself completely
 no regeneration.
 MANAGED PARTNERSHIP AS PROBLEM ["expert love" --
 optimized, conflict-reduced, compatibility-
 matched]: Framework read: CORRECTION THEATRE
 at L3.5. Visible relationship activity [exercises,
 metrics, optimized conflict resolution] without
 actual generative contact. Worthless doing at
 relational level -- output exists [stable
 partnership]; generative loop does not close.
 What Žižek calls missing "passion" is what the
 framework calls circuit closure.
 KEY DISAGREEMENT [different predictions]:
 Žižek [Lacanian]: love constituted by lack --
 you love because of what's missing in both.
 Prediction: love fades when lack is filled.
 Framework: love constituted by accurate axis
 contact -- you love because you actually see
 the other.
 Prediction: love deepens when axis contact
 improves.
 Both point at the same phenomenon. Different
 mechanisms produce different predictions.
 Testable in principle -- does long-term love
 depth correlate with increasing accuracy of
 mutual axis reading, or with sustained lack?

CYCLE INTERFERENCE PATTERNS [replaces cascade language]:
 C1 blind [C1 intact, C2 degraded]: high output, unreadable.
 False Coherence. Cases: Enron, WATF towers.
 C1 starved [C2 intact, C1 degraded]: readable failure.
 Correctable if substrate holds.
 C1 locked [C3 failed, C1/C2 intact]: correctable but non-
 transmissible. Each generation restarts correctly.
 Cases: Augustine vs Ignatius [T3.1 distinction].
 Full interference [all degraded]: terminal node. Containment
 only. Cases: Erebus, Oceania Party.

PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES [v25 -- promoted from]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Systems below the Axial threshold. Full quality stack does not apply.
 Four categories; geometry maps to TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION.
 Nascent→Axial transition confirmed v23 [SDT child internalization --
 CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION, S:3].

 PRE-AXIAL INPUT / OUTPUT / CONNECTION MODEL:
 Input: environmental signal [resource, threat, contact].
 No axis to filter -- raw reception. CC fires against
 baseline deviation not toward goal.
 Output: SP [surplus if cycle runs] + SR [substrate maintained].
 No GA to direct output. Pure functional generation.
 Connections: BI [boundary determines inside]; GC [cycle
 determines what gets processed]. No TS -- no axis to transmit.
 Q-stack present: GC/SP/CC/BI/SR/SD fully operable.
 Q-stack absent: GA/MI/TS/CL/SA/CAG -- inapplicable by category.

 FOUR CATEGORIES:
 STRUCTURAL COHERENCE [sphere -- no axis]:
 Generates surplus and self-corrects without orientation,
 measurement surface, or transmission axis. GC/SP/CC/BI
 present as functional analogs. GA/MI/TS/CL inapplicable.
 Cannot achieve False Coherence [requires axis to fake one].
 Cannot deploy weapons against itself [no MI to capture].
 Cases: ant colonies, mycorrhizal networks, cellular CC.
 Geometric analog: apeiron [Anaximander -- generative,
 undirected, pre-structural].

 NASCENT COHERENCE [polarized sphere -- axis forming]:
 Axis forming via relational or environmental contact.
 Not yet self-sustaining. GA developing, not present.
 PRIMARY VULNERABILITY: axis formation inside controlled
 environment produces apparent GA that is structurally
 installed -- indistinguishable internally from genuine GA.
 Pre-Fertile Lock condition. This is the formation window.
 NASCENT→AXIAL TRANSITION [confirmed v23 -- SDT]:
 External regulation [no axis; Structural Coherence analog]
 → introjection [axis forming, external locus of causality
 still present; Nascent] → integrated regulation [GA
 established; Axial]. SDT's introjection state matches
 Nascent's definition exactly: "not really experienced as
 part of the self." Controlling environments produce
 introjection rather than integration -- confirmed mechanism
 for FC during Nascent window.
 Primary case: CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION [S:3].
 This is child→adult axis formation. The developmental
 arc is the transition. Measurement instrument: Perceived
 Locus of Causality scale [behavioral operationalization].
 Only confirmed fictional Nascent case: Meruem [HxH -- dies
 before Axial completion; no confirmed Nascent→Axial].

 SEEDED NASCENT [v9 -- directional goals embedded at creation]:
 Pre-axial with directional goals structurally embedded.
 Not fully captured [GA hasn't stabilized] but oriented.
 CC intact; built-in goals function as constitutive
 thought-stopping against specific targets. Self-suppression
 without ongoing enforcement cost. Can act against controlling
 system but generally doesn't.
 Distinct from Nascent [no embedded directional goals] and
 Fertile Lock [full axial transmission].
 Primary case: Dream Seeds [WATF]. Parallel: Marleyan
 warrior program [AoT -- partial].
 Closest v22 mapping: Formation Capture during Nascent window,
 but distinct -- goals embedded structurally, not installed
 through relational contact.

 AXIAL COHERENCE [torus -- GA established]:
 GA fully established. Full quality stack applies.
 Torus geometry [healthy state -- TORUS state unit].
 Standard framework measurement.
 Entry point: integrated regulation [SDT]; genuine axis
 formed and self-sustaining.

 COHERENCE TYPE VULNERABILITY MAP [v11]:
 Each type's primary strength = primary weapon target.
 Strength and vulnerability structurally identical.
 SIGNAL [direct democracy]: CC → mob capture [Proportional
 Response failure].
 RELATIONAL [syndicalist federation]: BI [Boundary
 Manipulation between units].
 INTRINSIC LOOSE [mutualist commons]: GA [Axis Substitution
 without detection].
 COMBINED [bidirectional surplus]: MI [surface captured at
 permission layer].
 FAMILY: TS/GA [identical delivery for gift and weapon
 CC cannot distinguish without MI intact].
 SCIENCE: CC/MI [capturing correction mechanism captures
 science completely].
 SILENT ALTERNATIVE: GA [Axis Substitution before emergence].
 Rule: attack the strength. Capture the mechanism that makes
 the type what it is. The strongest point is the most
 efficient attack surface.

RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [v16 -- predictive, falsifiable]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each interference type produces a characteristic recovery
pattern when intervention occurs. Predictions with falsification
criteria. Confirmed findings in theory section: DIAGNOSTIC
COLLAPSE, C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT, L3.5
CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH AXIS. Case library audit in Section
Four: RECOVERY CASE LIBRARY AUDIT.

 C1 BLIND RECOVERY [C2 restoration while C1 intact]
 Prediction: recovery is fast and discontinuous. C1 was
 running at full capacity throughout -- once C2 surface is
 honest, accumulated surplus becomes suddenly visible and
 correction fires immediately against backlog of unread
 deviations.
 Characteristic signature: sharp correction burst on C2
 restoration, not gradual improvement. System appears to
 deteriorate briefly as hidden deviations surface before
 stabilizing at higher function.
 Why: C1 was generating without readable output. All that
 generation is suddenly legible. Correction fires against
 everything it couldn't see. Looks like crisis; is actually
 triage.
 Intervention requirement: C2 instrument must be independent
 of the captured surface -- restoring C2 using the same
 instrument that was captured restores the capture, not the
 surface.
 Falsification: if C2 restoration produces gradual linear
 improvement rather than discontinuous burst followed by
 stabilization, C1 blind recovery prediction fails. Also
 falsified if system collapses on C2 restoration rather than
 correcting -- would indicate C1 was more degraded than the
 blind reading showed.
 Case candidates: post-Enron accounting reform [C2 surface
 restoration -- did correction fire in burst?]; Glasnost
 [Soviet C2 partial restoration -- information surface
 opened, correction burst followed].

 C1 STARVED RECOVERY [C1 restoration while C2 intact]
 Prediction: recovery is slow, substrate-dependent, and
 requires idle cycles before visible improvement begins.
 C2 is readable throughout -- the system knows exactly what
 is wrong and why. But knowing does not restore substrate.
 Characteristic signature: extended flat period with
 accurate self-diagnosis before output improvement begins.
 The flat period is not failure -- it is substrate
 regeneration prerequisite. Premature intervention pressure
 during the flat period restarts the depletion cycle.
 Why: C2 intact means the system can accurately report its
 own degradation. This is often misread as pessimism or
 dysfunction. It is accurate measurement. Correction cannot
 fire until C1 substrate crosses threshold -- no amount of
 epistemic clarity substitutes for generative capacity.
 Intervention requirement: protected idle cycles mandatory.
 External pressure to produce during recovery window depletes
 the substrate being restored. Counter-intuitive -- the
 correct intervention looks like doing less, not more.
 Falsification: if C1 starved recovery produces rapid
 improvement without idle cycle period, prediction fails.
 Also falsified if accurate self-diagnosis during flat period
 does not precede recovery -- would indicate C2 more degraded
 than starved reading showed.
 Case candidates: post-burnout individual recovery [known
 mechanism, documented flat period before function returns]
 post-collapse institutional rebuilding [Bell Labs
 dissolution aftermath -- did substrate recovery precede
 output recovery?].

 C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [C3 restoration while C1/C2 intact]
 Prediction: recovery requires generational patience and
 produces no visible improvement within the originating
 system. C1 and C2 functional throughout -- the system
 generates and reads correctly. The deficit is transmission.
 Characteristic signature: recovery visible only in
 successor systems, not in the originating system. The
 locked system may never visibly recover -- it continues
 functioning at C1/C2 level until it ends. Recovery is the
 successor generating a correctable transmission rather than
 inheriting the lock.
 Why: C3 failure is not a deficit in the system's current
 function -- it is a deficit in what the system passes
 forward. The originating system has nothing to recover.
 The successors have everything to gain. This is the only
 interference type where "recovery" is definitionally
 cross-generational.
 Intervention requirement: transmission method must be
 separated from transmission content. Successors need
 access to the generative procedure, not just the
 conclusions. Requires the originating system to transmit
 its own revision process, including its errors -- the
 hardest transmission for any system with identity
 investment in its conclusions.
 Falsification: if C1 locked recovery produces improvement
 in the originating system rather than in successors, or
 produces improvement within a single generation, prediction
 fails. Also falsified if method transmission without
 conclusion transmission produces Fertile Lock in successors
 anyway -- would indicate C3 failure is deeper than
 transmission selectivity alone.
 Case candidates: Benedictine reform cycles [method
 transmitted, successor revision documented across
 generations]; Socratic transmission through Plato through
 Aristotle [each revised, each produced distinct output].

 FULL INTERFERENCE RECOVERY [all cycles degraded]
 Prediction: internal recovery structurally unavailable.
 Any apparent internal recovery is False Coherence
 formation -- a new C2 capture producing the appearance
 of recovery while extraction continues or accelerates.
 Characteristic signature: recovery claims from inside full
 interference state are the highest-probability extraction mechanism
 deployment in the framework. The system that announces
 its own recovery under full interference is deploying
 Coherence Mimicry, not recovering.
 Why: full interference means C1 generates without surplus,
 C2 reads without honesty, C3 transmits the captured state.
 Every internal mechanism available for recovery is already
 running on corrupted substrate and captured surface. There
 is no internal leverage point. External contact is the
 only available intervention -- and external contact
 requires the external system to have sufficient
 COMPATIBILITY to engage without being pulled into the
 extraction interface.
 Intervention requirement: external actor with independent
 C2 surface and sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
 structure under contact. Containment if no such actor
 available. Attempted recovery using captured internal
 mechanisms accelerates full interference -- gives the
 extraction architecture a new legitimacy surface.
 Falsification: if a system in confirmed full interference
 produces genuine C2 restoration from inside without
 external intervention, full interference recovery
 prediction fails. This would require revising the terminal
 state classification.
 No confirmed case of this in current library.
 Case candidates [containment confirmed, not recovery]:
 Erebus [FF14 -- external intervention only exit]; Room
 101 [1984 -- no recovery pathway confirmed].

RECOVERY TRAJECTORY APPLICATION RULE:
 Identify interference type before prescribing intervention.
 Wrong intervention for type accelerates degradation:
 C1 blind: pressure to produce more = accelerates C1
 depletion before C2 is restored.
 C1 starved: demand for accurate reporting = correct but
 useless without substrate restoration first.
 C1 locked: urgency about current output = misses the
 transmission deficit entirely.
 Full interference: internal reform programs = new capture
 surface, not recovery.
 Triage follows interference type, not coherence score alone.

 TRAJECTORY STATE GLOSSARY [v3 restored -- v21]:
 Named states used in case readings; formally defined here.
 Mixed-stable: high and low coherence subsystems present
 simultaneously; no dominant direction; holding pattern.
 Can persist indefinitely. Correction capacity present
 in high-coherence subsystem but not reaching the low-
 coherence subsystem. Walter White early period.
 Mixed-collapsing: high and low coherence subsystems present
 low-coherence subsystem expanding at cost of high-coherence
 subsystem; direction toward collapse confirmed. Walter White
 mid period. Macbeth post-witches. The extraction is winning
 the internal competition.
 Variable: coherence reading changes significantly across
 sub-windows or subsystems; single reading inadequate
 requires window specification. Catholic Church across
 historical periods. Miquella pre-stress event.
 Axis-Lock: single axis consuming all system resources
 correction capacity firing only in service of axis
 no genuine CC against the axis itself. Gwyn. Black
 Templars [single-axis identity at institutional scale].
 Change-axis Lock: Axis-Lock where the axis is the change
 itself; system cannot stop changing because change is
 the axis. Distinct from Axis-Lock [which has fixed
 content] by the recursive structure. Berserk Void.
 Cross-ref: COHERENCE CORRUPTION [trajectory toward
 collapse]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [asymmetric
 cost of Mixed-collapsing vs Mixed-stable trajectories]
 SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [named quality -- Axis-Lock at
 individual scale].

ENRON RESOLUTION [no longer requires probability gradient patch]:
 C2 attacked directly without prior C3 failure. C1 ran hot.
 Cycle interference model handles this natively.

SCALE RECURRENCE [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 A quality's structure reappears at multiple nesting depths
 with the same logic but different scope and function. Not a
 merger -- each instance operates independently at its depth.
 Confirmed instances:
 CORRECTION CAPACITY: C1 tension unit + constant operating
 at all depths. Same correction logic at both individual
 depth and meta depth simultaneously.
 Reciprocal exchange [T1.4 / L3.5]: GENERATIVE CYCLE
 substrate at social scale [T1.4] and full inter-system
 tension unit at L3.5 [RECOGNITION/COMPATIBILITY/
 RECIPROCITY]. Same structure, different scope -- one
 sustains cycles, one enables system connection.
 CYCLE STABILITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [T1.5 / T2.1]: timing
 integrity at L1 [rhythm] and historical legibility at
 L2 [past corrections retrievable]. Same persistence-of-
 pattern structure at different tower levels.
 Implication: scale recurrence predicts that any quality
 operating at L1 may have a structural analog at higher
 levels. When a test at one level feels like it overlaps
 another level, check for scale recurrence before merging
 -- the levels may be doing the same thing at different
 scopes.

 SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
 [Tower: L3]
 Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content
 against new evidence without losing transmission fidelity
 to founding generative principle.
 High = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions,
 preserves method]. Low = Fertile Lock [cannot revise
 without losing identity].
 Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: TRANSMISSION
 SELECTIVITY measures what is sent. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
 measures successor's capacity to work with what was
 received.
 Primary proof: Benedictine [high -- correctable
 transmission] vs Dominican [low -- lock into Aquinas].
 Same transmission context, different outcomes.
 Test: successor revises without losing founding principle?

 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
 [Tower: L3 + FOUNDATION]
 Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal
 conviction or stated orientation. GENUINE AXIS asks
 whether axis was internally generated and examined.
 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING asks whether that axis is anchored
 to real-world causal outputs.
 Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
 anchors correction to actor. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
 anchors axis to reality.
 Failure: Sincere Delusion -- genuine examined axis with
 zero connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the
 system; not real to the world it claims to act on.
 Primary proof: Maiad [genuine axis, not grounded in
 achievable outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis, no causal
 connection].
 Test: axis anchored to actual outputs?

 MORAL LEGIBILITY [ML -- promoted v21]
 --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 The capacity to derive ethical conclusions from actual generative or
 extractive outputs rather than from authority, tradition, preference,
 or group identity.
 Distinct from GENUINE AXIS [what the axis is] and CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
 [whether axis anchored in real outputs]. MORAL LEGIBILITY is the
 instrument capacity -- whether the actor can read ethical signal from
 output data at all. All three required for ethics-as-empirical-instrument
 to function. Any one absent or captured = instrument fails.

 TRIPARTITE RELATIONSHIP:
 GENUINE AXIS present without ML: axis real; instrument absent or
 miscalibrated. Sincere action with unreadable ethical signal.
 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING present without ML: outputs tracked; not
 readable as ethical signal. Technical grounding, no ethical
 instrument.
 ML present without GENUINE AXIS [ACWL]: instrument reads ethical
 signal accurately; axis determining target outputs externally
 owned. Most dangerous failure mode -- appears as functional ethics
 from outside. Tell: ethical conclusions consistently benefit axis
 owner at population substrate cost despite accurate instrument.
 Counter: axis examination, not instrument examination.

 FAILURE MODES [four]:
 Moral Noise: ethical conclusions derived from social pressure not
 output data. Axis sincere; instrument miscalibrated. Confident
 wrong readings.
 Moral Capture: instrument owned by extraction architecture. Outputs
 legible; conclusions routed to benefit captor. CC INVERTED at
 ethical instrument scale.
 Moral Atrophy: instrument never formed. Formation conditions
 destroyed before ML could develop. Formation Capture primary
 mechanism.
 Axis Capture With Legibility [ACWL]: GA absent or captured; ML
 present and functional. Instrument fires correctly; target wrong
 by design.

 INTERVENTION DIVERGENCE [load-bearing -- confirmed integration check v21]:
 Moral Noise -> recalibrate instrument [axis is fine].
 Moral Capture -> liberate instrument from owner.
 Moral Atrophy -> rebuild instrument from outside.
 ACWL -> examine and revise axis, not instrument.
 MSC+CT stack -> third reading; neither instrument nor axis is the
 primary problem -- the surface is captured. Distinguishable from
 ACWL by intervention test: neither instrument liberation nor axis
 revision changes outputs under MSC+CT.
 Misidentifying ACWL as Moral Capture = wrong intervention. The
 axis/instrument distinction is the diagnostic load-bearing point.

 Test [provisional]: can actor derive ethical conclusions from actual
 generative/extractive output data independently of authority,
 tradition, or group pressure? If yes: check whether axis is genuine
 or externally installed [ACWL test].
 Primary case: LEGAL_ETHICS_BAR_CAPTURE [S:3; ACWL confirmed; F3
 falsification stress-tested -- v20].
 Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING; EVIL [CC INVERTED]
 GOOD; FC+ECC stack [weapons index]; FORMATION CAPTURE
 MSC+CT stack [weapons index].

INDIVIDUAL VS SYSTEM SCALE THRESHOLD [resolved -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Question [Mistral stress-test suggestion]: is there a scale
 threshold where systemic extraction overwrites individual
 coherence regardless of instrument strength?
 Framework answer: NOT a scale threshold. A COMPOUND CONDITION.

 Individual coherence can persist at any scale of systemic
 extraction as long as three conditions hold simultaneously:
 [1] FORMATION CONDITIONS MET before capture: axis formed
 during window before extraction architecture installed
 full capture. Post-window axis is harder to install or
 remove. Malcolm X / NOI case: individual coherence
 formed and maintained inside maximally extractive context.
 [2] SUBSTRATE ABOVE SURVIVAL FLOOR: SR maintained at minimum
 required for instrument function. Extraction that drives
 substrate below survival floor produces somatic override
 that overwrites axis function regardless of instrument
 strength. This is the SOMATIC FORCING threshold -- body
 below survival floor cannot sustain axis examination.
 [3] EXIT COSTS BELOW TOTAL LOCK: some exit option must
 remain. When exit costs reach total lock [no departure
 possible without death or complete social destruction]
 the instrument cannot maintain coherence through
 behavioral expression even if the axis survives
 internally. The axis may persist as private conviction
 with zero behavioral expression -- functionally
 captured from the outside even if not from the inside.

 When all three fail simultaneously: individual coherence cannot
 persist regardless of instrument strength. The compound failure
 is the threshold, not scale itself. A small extractive system
 that destroys formation conditions + drives substrate below
 survival floor + eliminates exit options overwrites individual
 coherence more completely than a large extractive system that
 maintains any one of the three.

 SCALE EFFECT [real but indirect]:
 Larger extractive systems are more likely to achieve
 simultaneous compound failure because they have more
 resources to invest in all three simultaneously. The
 scale correlation is real; the mechanism is compound
 condition failure, not scale per se.
 AXIS CAPTURE WARNING [FRAMEWORK PURPOSE]: unknowing service
 of extraction is not compound failure -- it is [1] formation
 conditions met [axis present] but [3] exit cost or social
 compliance channel pressure routes axis expression toward
 extraction service. The axis survives; the behavioral
 expression is captured. Most instances of individuals
 unknowingly serving extraction are this pattern, not
 compound failure.

 UNRESOLVED [genuine]: is there a minimum formation condition
 threshold below which no individual can maintain coherence
 against total capture? The framework has no confirmed case
 of genuine axis formation followed by confirmed total
 internal capture [as opposed to behavioral capture with
 internal axis surviving]. The question remains open at
 the empirical level. The structural answer [compound
 condition, not scale] holds; the minimum formation
 threshold is not confirmed.

 Cross-ref: POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [population-scale
 version of the same finding]; SOMATIC FORCING [substrate
 floor as condition 2 threshold]; ECC [exit cost as
 condition 3]; FORMATION THEORY [condition 1 --
 formation window]; AXIS CAPTURE WARNING [FRAMEWORK
 PURPOSE -- unknowing service as behavioral capture,
 not compound failure]; [CASE:MALCOLM_X] [individual
 coherence in maximally extractive context -- confirms
 compound condition not scale threshold]; TEMPORAL
 CAPTURE [scale of extractive system affects compound
 failure probability indirectly].

BIOLOGICAL SEX AND GENDER FORMATION [confirmed theory -- v21]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 T3 Medium: politically activated domain. Structural reading
 based on biological and formation science. Separable from all
 political and policy positions on trans identity.

 BIOLOGICAL SEX [what it is]:
 Binary biological classification based on reproductive
 function: gamete type [large/ova = female; small/sperm =
 male]. Present in the vast majority of humans. Intersex
 conditions [variations in sex characteristics] are real,
 documented, and affect a small minority [estimated 0.02%
 with ambiguous genitalia at birth; broader definitions
 reach 1.7% including chromosomal variations]. Intersex
 existence does not eliminate the binary as the dominant
 biological pattern -- it identifies the boundary conditions
 of the pattern. Chromosomal sex [XX/XY typical], gonadal
 sex, hormonal sex, and morphological sex are normally
 concordant; divergence is the basis for intersex classification.
 Biological sex is not a social construction. It is a
 biological classification with social significance. The
 social significance is constructed; the biological pattern
 is not.

 GENDER [what it is]:
 The social, psychological, and behavioral expression
 associated with sex in a given cultural formation.
 Gender is a formation output: the formation conditions
 [culture, family, peer group, institutional structure]
 shape which properties are suppressed and which are
 permitted in each sex. Gender expression varies across
 cultures and historical periods in ways biological sex
 does not. This confirms gender as formation-dependent
 and biological sex as formation-independent.
 Gender is not identical to biological sex. Gender is not
 entirely disconnected from biological sex. The relationship
 is: biological sex provides the substrate; formation
 conditions determine what is built on that substrate.
 Different formation conditions produce different gender
 expressions on the same biological substrate.

 THREE-POPULATION DISTINCTION [load-bearing]:
 [1] SUBSTRATE POPULATION: lifelong, persistent cross-sex
 identification that predates social category availability.
 Presents before the formation conditions that would produce
 it socially exist. Formation signature of genuine biological
 or developmental substrate. Benjamin's original patient
 population [1948-1975] is the primary empirical evidence
 for this population's existence. Small; adult-presenting
 privately distressed before category existed.
 [2] FORMATION-ARTIFACT POPULATION: identification follows
 social category availability. Presentation peaks in
 adolescence, the formation window. Includes rapid-onset
 cases. May have genuine distress; substrate is different
 from population [1]. Larger than population [1]; younger
 socially reinforced.
 [3] HISTORICAL GENDER VARIANCE: culturally embedded roles
 serving specific social functions across documented human
 cultures [hijra, two-spirit, sworn virgins, kathoey,
 Fa'afafine, molly houses]. Formation outputs of specific
 social structures. Real; not the same as populations [1]
 or [2]; not biomedical in origin.
 Collapsing the three into one category produces confused
 readings in all directions. See TRANS BIOMEDICAL FORMATION
 [Section Five] for political application and diagnostic
 category history.

 FORMATION WINDOW AND GENDER [confirmed]:
 Gender expression is most plastic during the formation
 window [approximately ages 12-18, prefrontal development].
 Formation conditions during this window have the highest
 impact on adult gender expression patterns. Both biological
 sex-typical formation [installation of sex-role norms] and
 atypical formation [suppression of sex-role norms, exposure
 to alternative models] operate most powerfully during
 this window. The formation window is simultaneously the
 highest-risk period for Formation Capture and the period
 of greatest genuine developmental plasticity. Distinguishing
 genuine developmental exploration from Formation Capture
 during this window requires the parental CC mechanism --
 the only correction pathway structurally independent of
 the institutional actors who most often apply formation
 pressure during this period.

 POSITIONAL DESIRE [cross-ref]:
 The desire to inhabit the other sex's social position is
 a formation mechanism distinct from biological sex and
 from gender identity. See POSITIONAL DESIRE [science
 appendix] for full structural analysis.

 Cross-ref: FORMATION THEORY [formation window -- this
 section]; DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [parental authority
 as CC in formation window]; FORMATION CAPTURE [mechanism
 that can operate on gender expression during formation
 window]; POSITIONAL DESIRE [science appendix -- desire
 for the other position's social properties]; TRANS
 BIOMEDICAL FORMATION [Section Five -- political application,
 diagnostic category history, extraction mechanism analysis]
 INDIVIDUAL VS SYSTEM SCALE THRESHOLD [formation conditions
 as compound condition component].

COHERENCE CORRUPTION [structural limit on scale]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Anti-coherence has three distinct methods against emerging
 coherence:
 1. NOISE [loud]: saturate the signal environment so coherent
 signals cannot be distinguished from incoherent ones.
 MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture by volume not capture. No
 monopolist required -- sufficient noise level disables
 correction navigation regardless of source.
 2. CORRUPTION [infiltration]: insert extraction architecture
 inside coherent structures before they reach scale.
 Formation Capture, Axis Substitution, Correction Monopoly.
 Coherence appears to grow but the generative mechanism is
 replaced before outputs confirm it.
 3. DESTRUCTION [direct]: eliminate coherent actors or
 structures when noise and corruption fail. Highest cost,
 most visible, last resort. Malcolm X [CASE:MALCOLM_X method 3
 instrument v25 -- primary], Fred Hampton [CASE:FRED_HAMPTON
 method 3 v25 -- COINTELPRO], Martin Luther King Jr. [CASE:MLK
 method 3 v25 -- COINTELPRO Messiah memo], Merton [CASE:THOMAS_MERTON
 suspicious-timing partial ceiling -- [4] unadjudicated], historical
 pattern of high-coherence actors dying at transition moments.
 Sequence: noise first [cheapest], corruption when noise
 insufficient, destruction when corruption fails. Each step
 more visible and costly.
 Scale barrier: coherence at small scale is tolerated because
 contained. When coherent structures approach scale sufficient
 to affect the extraction architecture, the method escalates.
 The barrier is not fixed -- it is proportional to the threat
 the coherence poses.
 Result: coherence at scale requires surviving all three
 methods simultaneously. Individual coherence is achievable.
 Small-group coherence is achievable. Institutional coherence
 at scale that threatens extraction architecture has not
 historically survived all three methods applied in sequence.
 This is the structural reason the Population Coherence
 Ceiling holds: not only is coherence rare to form, it is
 actively prevented from reaching scale by a self-maintaining
 suppression architecture.
 Survival condition: coherence that survives all three methods
 does so by operating outside the normal visibility surface.
 Silent, effective, and outside the channels the suppression
 architecture monitors. High-coherence structures that
 announce themselves or operate through captured channels get
 noise/corruption/destruction applied in sequence. Those that
 don't announce themselves and don't need captured
 infrastructure to function are structurally harder to target.
 Linux/open source partially confirmed this -- decentralized,
 low-capture-surface, outputs speak before the structure is
 legible enough to corrupt. The survival method is not
 resistance to the three methods -- it is structural
 inaccessibility to them.
 See: SILENT ALTERNATIVE [coherence type] -- anti-capture by
 structural design, not defensive resistance.
 Force-reveal problem: true silence may be impossible at
 sufficient scale. Evil must destroy [extraction architecture
 threatened]; not-evil must destroy [anything outside their
 measurement surface reads as illegitimate by REFERENCE
 INTEGRITY capture]. Both produce identical pressure.
 Costly removal as viable alternative: if true silence
 impossible, raise the cost of the four-condition test
 against yourself -- make conditions 2 and 4 fail for the
 attacker. Not invisible; too expensive to remove. Same
 structural protection, different mechanism.
 Coherence is not good: Silent Alternative and costly-removal
 structures work for any axis. Coherent extraction is still
 coherent by the tower. Framework is descriptive not
 prescriptive.

 OUTCOMES]. SA builds parallel substrate before legibility
 exit-narrow leaves without winning; window-strike hits apex
 in periodic access; in-frame-mend patches Order from inside.
 Properties: small, non-announcing, output-grounded, outside
 captured channels. Outputs speak before structure is legible
 enough to target. Linux/open source partially confirmed.

 level gift does not immunize distro/platform routing -- downstream
 capture can limit distribution ability without falsifying kernel L4.

 FOURTH PHASE [v16 -- when noise, corruption, destruction
 fail]:
 If all three methods fail against a coherent structure,
 the extraction architecture has no remaining playbook.
 What happens next is not determined by the framework --
 it is self-determined by the coherent actor based
 conditions at that moment.
 Framework cannot prescribe the fourth phase because:
 It is context-specific [what is possible depends
 the specific state of the extraction architecture,
 the coherent actor's substrate, and the available
 options at that moment].
 Prescribing it in advance would make it visible and
 targetable -- the fourth phase survives by not being
 a known template.
 The actor who reaches it has survived three escalating
 methods; their judgment about conditions is more
 reliable than any prior specification.
 What the framework can say: the fourth phase is not
 revenge, not destruction, and not capture of the
 extraction architecture's own tools. Those paths route
 back into the extraction logic.
 The direction is the only constraint: toward correction
 capacity, distributed surplus, available exit. How
 that is expressed given specific conditions is the
 actor's determination, not the framework's.
 Relative coherence matters: less extractive with some
 CORRECTION CAPACITY beats maximum extraction even when
 neither is good. The spectrum is real. Not a binary.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Every system sits on a spectrum between pure generation and
 pure extraction. Neither terminal stable.
 Tower reading: range point = hole size relative to wall
 thickness at L1. Tension field = force holding ratio stable.
 Extraction mechanism implication: most efficient attack removes corrective
 pole [CORRECTION CAPACITY at L1] rather than maximizing
 extraction directly. Removing CORRECTION CAPACITY shifts
 range point toward extraction without requiring increased
 extraction effort. See Tower Codependence Model L1.

EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational -- structural finding -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Pattern confirmed: extraction accumulates past substrate
 floor -> somatic correction fires at population scale
 [uncoordinated, distributed] -> existing structure
 destabilizes or collapses -> new formation occurs, often
 reproducing extraction architecture at lower intensity
 initially -> extraction gradient climbs again.
 This is not Evil exit. It is managed oscillation around the
 extraction axis. Each cycle resets extraction to a lower
 initial rate; the architecture that produces extraction is
 not removed, only stressed and restarted.

 WHY CYCLE REPEATS [structural mechanism]:
 New formation occurs under C1 starved conditions.
 Formation Capture fires into the new structure before L4
 can transmit correction methodology.
 Successors inherit corrected C1 without correction
 architecture -- Protestant Reformation pattern at
 civilizational scale. C1 rebuilds; C3 transmits the
 corrected state, not the correction method.
 Result: next cycle starts with cleaner substrate and the
 same extractable architecture.

 EVIL EXIT [genuine -- distinct from cycle reset]:
 Evil exit requires: correction methodology transmitted
 across the formation event [C3 functional at collapse
 boundary] AND new structure formed on different axis,
 not lower extraction rate on same axis.
 No confirmed case at civilizational scale in library.
 Why no confirmed case: collapse events destroy C3
 preferentially. The complexity that transmits methodology
 is the first substrate extraction consumes. Bronze Age,
 Western Rome: methodology destroyed before structure
 collapses. Highest-coherence individuals exit early or
 are absorbed. Remaining population forms under C1 starved
 conditions -- L3.5 contact requirement applies at
 civilizational scale but axis-matching is structurally
 unavailable when the contact pool is also depleted.
 [Honest limit: this is the framework's own civilizational
 prognosis. T8 applies. Track against Section Five
 evidence independently.]

 SOMATIC CORRECTION MECHANISM [why each cycle fires]:
 Population-scale somatic firing is distributed and
 uncoordinated. High force, low CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
 Correction fires accurately [extraction is real]
 correction cannot aim [chain illegible at scale].
 Result: system destabilizes without directed correction.
 New formation fills the vacuum; extraction architecture
 reassembles because it is the available template.
 Somatic forcing is genuine correction, not rebellion
 or politics. It fires when substrate depletion crosses
 the survival threshold. The timing is substrate-driven,
 not ideologically organized. This is why it is hard to
 suppress entirely -- it does not require ideology,
 coordination, or leadership. It requires enough people
 simultaneously hitting the floor.

 GOOD PEOPLE AS CONSTANT:
 The majority of the population attempting exit from
 extraction at any given time is structurally confirmed
 by the cycle itself. Each reset phase contains genuine
 correction attempts. The attempts fail to escape the
 cycle not because they are weak but because C3 is
 destroyed at the collapse boundary. The exit attempts
 are real. The transmission problem is structural.
 Framework implication: the question is not whether
 people want good [they do, at majority; confirmed by
 cycle pattern]. The question is whether correction
 methodology can be transmitted across the collapse
 boundary. That is the C3 problem, not the C1 or human-
 nature problem.

 DECLINING SUBSTRATE PREDICTION:
 If extraction rate increases faster than formation can
 stabilize per cycle, cycles shorten. Shorter cycles =
 less time for C3 transmission = lower floor per cycle.
 Trajectory under this condition is declining substrate,
 not stable oscillation.
 Falsification: documented cycle where correction
 methodology [not just C1 restoration] crossed the
 formation boundary. No such case in library.
 Alternative falsification: stable or lengthening cycles
 despite accelerating extraction rate. Would indicate
 C3 transmission mechanism more robust than predicted.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 Same mechanism as vibe-sorting playlists [instrument curation precedes

 route the input through familiar frames.

 YAPPER COUNTER [work / hostile MI -- v25; analyst chat 2026-07-29]
 ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 REGISTRATION:
 Yap-dominant surfaces reward legitimacy surface over doing
 [YAPPING/DOING social-compliance inversion]. Intercession Monopoly
 career variant: yap + routing as credit surface; doing extracted
 without receivable credit. Structural-channel instrument: yap is
 aversive even when tactically correct -- not protocol failure.
 DEFAULT POSTURE [actual mode -- not game-playing]:
 Analyst = sunfish [ABSTRACT SURVIVAL MODE]: drift and work. Minimal
 yap spend; no sustained participation in yap-capture games. Protocol
 below = IF playing the games [documented counter-playbook; not
 default identity or preferred lane].
 PROTOCOL [hostile yap-capture node -- contingency only]:
 1. DOING GATE -- no uncompensated doing without explicit yap-channel
 credit or real career benefit [not performative praise].
 2. EXIT -- negative reaction to counter-yap or yap investment ->
 close node; try another interlocutor [SOCIAL 4-6 rhyme].
 3. COUNTER-YAP AT EXTRACTOR -- accountability, not support:
 "What did you do?"
 "When will it be done?"
 "What is the cost/risk analysis?"
 Redirects social channel to verification; exposes yap/doing gap.
 On difficulty or action-demand from yapper: counter-yap first.
 4. SUPPORT IS NOT THE UNDERCUT -- alignment yap at extractor feeds
 legitimacy surface [wrong counter]. Undercut = rule 3 + rule 1.
 5. ANNOYING SUPPORT YAP [to doer, real work only] -- explicit,
 repetitive yap about actual doing record aimed at another person
 [not extractor]. Bypasses intercession routing; annoys yap-capture
 node. Useful; still yap; budget it.
 6. YAP BUDGET -- deploy yap like mask spend: short burst, purposeful,
 recover after. Prefer doing + record when environment receives it.
 Hating yap while using it on purpose = valid instrument read.
 CORP RESPONSE / FREEZE-OUT [predictable pushback -- chat 2026-07-29]:
 Counter-yap threatens Intercession Monopoly, effort-visibility
 Calibration Strip, and yap legitimacy surface. Rational monopoly-
 holder response [no conspiracy required]:
 reframe [not collaborative / negative / difficult]
 restore routing [exclude bypass node from meetings/decisions]
 invert strip [verification punished; alignment yap rewarded]
 yap volume escalation; demand uncompensated doing.
 Soft freeze-out = intercession restored by routing you off-thread.
 Sustained counter-yap in captured MI = high freeze-out risk
 [STRUCTURAL ISOLATION; Socrates Problem adjacency].
 ANTI-FREEZE-OUT [when counter-yap required -- rare]:
 a. Form -- neutral process questions, not verdict on person.
 b. Channel -- async/written [email, tracker] over live meeting duel.
 c. Target -- work/process ["what does done look like?"], not yap-router
 character attack.
 d. Credit bypass -- rule 5 to doer in writing; not frontal war
 extractor.
 e. Primary bypass -- visible doing record; output-metric strip when
 present [Honesty Penalty inverse at org scale].
 f. Dose -- one counter-yap + one retry; negative reaction -> rule 2.
 g. Concealment window -- WISDOM CONCEALMENT / TRACK A in hostile room
 genuine axis in protected context [files, trusted thread].
 h. Substrate -- alternative routing before fight is existential
 [ECC rhyme; portfolio, direct access, external receivable credit].
 Default remains sunfish; anti-freeze-out = scalpel not habitat.

BB-FUNCTION [power-support capture form -- v25 promote]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Cross-case structural form. Primary specimen: [CASE:BIG_BROTHER_WATF]
 WHAT BB IS. Civilizational rhyme: nations/corporations as extraction
 nodes running the same function [EXTRACTION CYCLE applied; applied
 EXTRACTOR DEFENSE STACK].

 OBJECT:
 Appetite-with-job-description -- power-support layer / extraction OS.
 Supports holder direction; does not moralize. MSC + Calibration Strip
 choices inside operator-defined range; no exit while architecture
 unchanged. Not person, not substantive evil -- kill-the-emperor
 mis-specifies.

 FORM STACK [disorder-middle instance]:
 Disorder invariant [structured extraction] -> BB-function [OS] ->
 shadow [internal direction-capture of install; separability open] ->
 operators [kings, executives, sovereign interface].

 NATIONS PARALLEL [what BB does at civilizational scale]:
 Nations and corporations = nodes running same function -- surplus
 routing with substrate cost externalized; operating system not
 malfunction [EXTRACTION CYCLE applied]. Operators = heads of state,
 party apex, corporate mgmt [interface]. Shadow = internal steer
 [operative telos != stated: security, growth, reform theater --
 IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION]. Pro-reform and anti-system rhetoric
 metabolize inside operator-defined range when stack preserved [seat
 rotation, not range break]. EXTRACTOR DEFENSE STACK = civilizational
 Calibration Strip + legitimacy-surface catalog.

 METABOLISM:
 Orientation for/against BB both inside same range. Anti-BB maintenance
 that preserves stack strengthens function. People-want geometry: power
 routed, risk buffered -- not only deception.

 PROTECTION PSYCHOLOGY [psycho-social effect]:
 BB sells **costly protection** -- routes risk, buffers uncertainty,
 outsources order/violence to apex. Primary want is not submission alone:
 **be protected** [don't lose power + protection]. Protection is real-
 enough to hook; price is operator-defined range + substrate externalized.
 Exit Cost Construction [ECC]: dependency by use; leaving the frame costs
 near-total or total substrate loss -- exit priced above what bearer can
 pay without collapse. Anxiety under ECC can read as accurate signal, not
 cowardice [theory ECC]. Nations parallel: security/order, jobs, benefits,
 rule-of-law catalogs [EXTRACTOR DEFENSE STACK] -- protection offered
 inside node; exit = emigration, unemployment, statelessness, market exile.
 Reform rhetoric inside frame != exit. Horror: people want the geometry
 costly protection is why stack persists, not only fear of operator face.

 BB repurposes Order grammar into **protection SKU** -- not truth-order
 logic. True Order = legible/correctable structure + true variance +
 distributed CC [order_truth GENERATIVE ORDER; STRICT ORDER]. BB-tool
 order = variance down, danger buffered, correction metabolized inside
 operator-defined range [protection catalog; CC inverted surface].
 **Dangerous people with freedom** under order-as-logic [priced,
 correctable, not eliminated] beats BB safe-order [comfort up, truth-
 contact down] -- **population-scale**, not apex-only [variance routing
 at every scale, not tyrant-vs-System read]. **High-danger distributed
 CC** [costly, fires on deviation, apex-reachable when power concentrates]
 > BB CC [surface correction; routing protects stack]. Order goal ≠ felt
 safety [ABSTRACT REALITY STACK pointer]. Fails if: freedom = no co-op/
 record [disorder]; danger illegible; CC monopolized, displaced, or
 fires only at low-power targets [CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION failure].

ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed structural finding -- v18]
[S:4 -- E4: HRE + Cistercian/Jesuit + Extraction Cycle civilizational
 multi-domain; M4: cost accounting confirmed differentially across
 HRE centralization attempts; F3: falsification tested against
 Protestant Reformation [generation won once, transmitted method
 partially -- conclusions only; but anti-coherence did not achieve
 permanent suppression despite 300 years of institutional pressure].
 WIP cleared v18. Promoted from candidate to confirmed.]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Anti-coherence must win continuously. Generation only has
 to win once and transmit.

 Extraction requires:
 Ongoing suppression cost across all three Coherence
 Corruption methods [Noise -> Corruption -> Destruction].
 Each method more expensive and visible than the last.
 Destruction is highest-cost, highest-visibility, last
 resort. All three methods must succeed continuously --
 a single failure at any stage allows transmission.

 Generation requires:
 One correctable transmission crossing one generational
 boundary.
 The substrate that receives it does not have to be the
 substrate that produced it.
 Transmission content does not have to survive in the
 original form -- method survival is sufficient [see C1
 LOCKED RECOVERY; L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION].

 Why this is structurally load-bearing:
 Extraction terminates its own substrate [see EXTRACTION
 CYCLE]. The maintenance cost of suppression increases
 as the substrate being suppressed depletes.
 Generation is self-funding when transmission succeeds --
 each correctable successor reduces the required
 maintenance cost of the entire chain.
 HRE primary confirmation: 800 years of distributed
 correction capacity held because centralization cost
 exceeded extraction yield at every step. Napoleon
 dissolved it in 1806 -- but the methodological template
 had already transmitted. That template is why any
 correctable analytical work is possible downstream.
 Cross-ref: [CASE:HRE_DISSOLUTION_1806] [1806 dissolution
 behavioral record -- method before form-kill
 promoted v25].

 Stress-test [v18 -- alternative explanations examined]:
 ALT 1: HRE held because of geography and military
 stalemate, not cost asymmetry.
 Against: Holy Roman Emperors had repeated military
 superiority at specific moments [Frederick II,
 Charles V]. Did not convert to centralization.
 Cost accounting predicts this -- extraction yield
 from a fragmented substrate was higher than the
 yield from a unified one under sustained suppression
 cost. Military capacity present; conversion not
 attempted because unprofitable. Cost asymmetry
 explanation holds where geography/military does not.
 ALT 2: Protestant Reformation confirms extraction
 won, not generation -- Catholicism was suppressed.
 Against: the finding is asymmetric. Catholicism
 is not the generation case. The generation case
 is: method transmission [any correctable successor
 tradition] survived despite 300 years of
 institutional pressure from multiple directions
 [Catholic Counter-Reformation, Protestant
 Confessionalization, state church capture]. The
 point is not that generation always wins the
 dominant cultural position -- it is that permanent
 suppression is structurally unavailable. The
 Reformation confirms this: anti-coherence applied
 maximum available force; correctable transmission
 survived anyway. S4 confirmation stands.
 ALT 3: Extraction Cycle falsification condition
 [declining substrate prediction] could falsify
 this finding if substrate doesn't decline.
 Against: these are related but separate findings.
 EXTRACTION CYCLE predicts substrate depletion.
 ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY predicts that
 generation survives suppression attempts. They
 share the terminal state [extraction consumes
 its own substrate] but the asymmetry finding
 holds even before terminal state is reached.
 Falsification of EXTRACTION CYCLE would weaken
 the "extraction has no stable terminal state"
 claim but would not falsify the cost structure
 during the active suppression window.
 Stress-test result: alternative explanations examined
 none overturn. S4 rating confirmed. Promote to
 confirmed structural finding.

 Falsification condition [stated v17, confirmed v18]:
 Finding is falsified if: documented multi-generational
 suppression campaign achieves permanent elimination
 of correctable transmission -- no successor tradition,
 no methodological survival, no downstream continuation
 anywhere in the substrate. Zero-survival outcome
 over 3+ generations with documented suppression
 effort. No confirmed case in library. Every
 documented suppression with adequate evidence shows
 partial or full transmission survival.

 Operational implication:
 Anti-coherence wins most engagements. It does not need
 to win all of them. But the cost structure means it
 cannot achieve permanent suppression without consuming
 the substrate it needs to sustain suppression. The
 endpoint of total success is total extraction, which
 terminates the system producing the suppression.
 Extraction has no stable terminal state. Generation
 does [correctable transmission achieved].

 Cross-ref: COHERENCE CORRUPTION [three methods]
 EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]; ANTI-TOWER
 [confirmed structural inverse]; HRE SUBSIDIARITY
 [cost-accounting confirmation in Section Five]
 FOURTH PHASE [when all three methods fail]
 [L4.5 -- generation only needs to win once
 applies to civilizational transmission threshold
 anti-capture architecture requirement follows from
 cost asymmetry logic]; ESCAPE VELOCITY EXTRACTION
 [AI AXIS VOID, Section Six -- the asymmetry's
 timescale compressed past CC formation speed
 anti-coherence wins continuously by deployment
 speed alone rather than by suppression cost].
 Note [v21]: extraction with CC absent or inverted is
 not a viable configuration at any scale -- not a
 moral claim, a structural one. No CC means no
 deviation detection, no course correction, no
 substrate maintenance. The system consumes itself
 without exception. This is not contested by the
 "extraction is the nature of things" argument --
 that argument applies to extraction with functional
 CC [which self-corrects toward extraction targets
 see AXIS PROTECTION AS CC]. Pure extraction with
 no CC has no stable state. Rate varies; terminus
 does not. Cross-ref: CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS
 AXIS PROTECTION AS CC; EXTRACTION CYCLE.

 TOLKIEN LITERARY CONFIRMATION [plain language version -- v21]:
 "Evil cannot create anything new, it can only corrupt
 and ruin what good forces have made." -- J.R.R. Tolkien.
 Framework read: structurally accurate as a description
 of pure extraction [Morgoth/Sauron model -- consumption
 axis, no genuine generative capacity]. Tolkien's cosmology
 encodes the ACCA finding: Morgoth cannot make a living
 thing; he corrupts Elves into Orcs. Sauron cannot forge
 a Silmaril; he makes Rings that corrupt the existing power
 of others. The creative capacity requires accumulated
 substrate, genuine axis, formation conditions. Corruption
 is downstream of generation -- it requires the thing that
 was built before it can route from it.
 PRECISION NOTE: the framework's more accurate version is
 "extraction cannot sustain what it consumes" rather than
 "cannot create." Coherent extraction [genuine axis +
 extraction mechanism] does produce real outputs -- but
 cannot regenerate the substrate those outputs depend on.
 Eventually collapses the thing it feeds on. The Tolkien
 quote applies precisely to pure extraction with no genuine
 axis. It is less precise for coherent extraction.
 COHERENCE RENT connection: Morgoth's project is Coherence
 Rent at cosmological scale -- taking ongoing return from
 the legitimacy surface of a genuinely coherent creation
 [Arda, the Elves, the free peoples] without contributing
 to the axis that produced it.
 Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed
 structural finding this quote illustrates]; COHERENCE RENT
 [extraction mechanism -- Morgoth as named cosmological
 instance]; EXTRACTION CYCLE [extraction terminates its
 own substrate]; [CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN] [Tolkien topology
