Epistemic protocols, scoring procedure, reading procedure, measurement instruments.

HONEST LIMITS [T1-T9 -- measurement ceilings and structural limits]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 NOT HONESTY [disambiguation]: measurement ceilings only -- not the
 Section Two HONESTY epistemic standard [symmetric accounting, causal
 chain, solution veto tests]. "Honest limits" = what framework cannot
 resolve from inside; HONESTY = what policy must pass to count as
 correction. Do not conflate.
 HONEST LIMITS: measurement ceilings -- framework can approach but
 not resolve from current position. Not failures; structural
 properties of certain measurement configurations.
 STRUCTURAL LIMITS: underdetermined from inside framework entirely.
 Not open problems -- no empirical exit available internally.

 T1 [RESOLVABLE]:
 Fully measurable from current position with available instruments.
 Parasitic vs mutualistic: track net transfer direction, stress
 behavior, boundary behavior. No structural obstacle to resolution.

 T2 [PRE-RESOLUTION -- indeterminate before stress event]:
 Structurally indeterminate. Not a measurement failure.
 Applies to: genuine vs performed coherence, Contained vs
 Axis-Locked, High vs Total capture, Axis Concealment, Silent
 Alternative axis direction -- all before stress event.
 T2 IS A NAMED STATE, NOT A FAILED READING.
 SUBTYPE A -- CONCEALMENT [reveal type]:
 One axis operative, concealment active. Stress reveals.
 Four indirect discriminating tests [weighted]:
 Resource directionality inconsistent with stated axis.
 Asymmetric information protection under minor pressure.
 Behavioral consistency higher than variability predicts.
 Low-stakes activation consistent with one candidate axis.
 SUBTYPE B -- VARIABILITY [select type]:
 Both axes genuine candidates. Stress selects.
 Indicators: no concealment cost pattern, genuine axis
 variation by context, low-stakes activation switches.
 T2 PERMANENT [v9]: indeterminate state where no stress event
 is structurally possible. Distinct from T2 Pre-resolution
 [stress event will occur] and T3 [analyst cannot reach].
 T2 Permanent: indeterminacy is constitutive -- the system
 cannot be stress-tested by definition.
 Primary proof: [CASE:SOMA] ARK actor [simulation status unverifiable].
 OUTPUT FORMAT:
 "T2 Pre-resolution. [Subtype: A/B, confidence: low/medium.]
 If stress resolves toward [X]: trajectory A.
 If toward [Y]: trajectory B.
 Pre-stress indicators: [weighted list]."

 T3 [ANALYST BIAS -- unreachable from current instrument position]:
 Framework cannot calibrate own axis from inside own operation.
 Applies to framework itself and to analysts with strong priors --
 especially in political cases. T3 applies wherever analyst prior
 is directly relevant to the reading target. Not a demotion --
 a disclosure requirement. Declare T3 status; run adversarial
 check; proceed with caution flagged.

 T4 [STRUCTURAL OBSCURATION]:
 Net transfer direction is systematically the thing most obscured
 by weapon deployment. Structural, not a calibration problem.
 Weapon stacks specifically target the signal that would reveal
 transfer direction. T4 is not resolvable by better analysis --
 the obscuration is the mechanism.

 T5 [PRE-MEASUREMENT STATES]:
 States that are productive, stable, and non-damaging but
 structurally prior to the conditions measurement requires.
 Not failure states. Measurement surface is suspended or
 undefined by the nature of the state itself -- forcing
 measurement onto these states disrupts them.
 Examples: flow states, genuine rest, pre-reflective skill
 execution, deep creative process.
 Common property: productivity depends on measurement surface
 being temporarily suspended. States resolve naturally.

 T6 [OBSERVER EFFECT -- v14]:
 Measurement alters the measured system in ways that cannot be
 corrected for post-hoc. Not a failure of the framework -- a
 property of certain systems. Track A data collection changes
 Track A readings. Applies when observation changes the system
 being observed.
 Distinct from T5 [measurement surface suspended]: T6 cases the
 surface is intact but measurement itself changes the system.
 Primary proof: Hawthorne effect [workers changed behavior when
 observed regardless of condition].

 T7 [FERTILE LOCK SELF-SEALING -- v14]:
 Fertile Lock may be self-sealing by structural necessity, not
 merely empirically uncorrected. Self-initiated correction
 requires: [1] detection that something is wrong, [2] access to
 alternative measurement surface, [3] capacity to revise
 transmitted content. In Fertile Lock, all three are structurally
 absent. Therefore: Fertile Lock correction from inside may be
 structurally impossible. Exit requires external contact only --
 internal correction pathway may not exist.
 Implication: framework's 4/5 empirical exit may be the ONLY
 exit, not just the most likely one.

 T8 [RECURSIVE CLOSURE -- v14]:
 Framework cannot produce a reading of configurations where the
 observer, instrument, and observed are identical. Framework as
 instrument [scoring, predictions] and framework as measured
 system cannot be separated when the analyst is thinking with
 the framework inside the system being measured. Recursion is
 always present when this condition holds.
 Partial resolution: multiple instruments with different MI
 profiles measuring same cases independently. Where all three
 converge = higher confidence [blind spots differ]. Where
 readings diverge = divergence identifies which instrument has
 the blind spot on that case. Discordance becomes data, not noise.
 T8 is not correctable from inside the framework. Disclosure is
 the correction pathway, not the correction.
 CRITICAL DISTINCTION: disclosure makes recursion legible --
 it does not make recursion go away. Framework claiming disclosure
 solves T8 = Correction Theatre.

 T9 [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- v14]:
 Issue that cannot be targeted directly without critical internal
 damage [CID] if resolution fails. The correction pathway requires
 the subject's own axis to move -- external force breaks the
 container before the content can shift.
 PRIMARY [identity]: the identity that makes the behavior legible
 cannot survive the admission. Not a defect -- a structural
 protection mechanism. Civilizational-scale T9: admitting the
 extraction architecture is maintained on purpose collapses the
 civic identity built around progress and civilization.
 SECONDARY [somatic]: body maintains behavior pattern because the
 alternative produces physical destabilization. Structural load-
 bearing function, not weakness or moral failure.
 SECONDARY [ideological]: belief system encodes the behavior as
 correct -- correction feels like axis betrayal not axis
 correction.
 Test: is correction absent because externally blocked [T3/T4],
 or because the admission would produce CID [T9]?
 Operational note: cannot be targeted directly. Measure, document,
 act around it. Expose to observers not subject. Outlast via
 Silent Alternative or approach via T9 SOFT EXPOSURE METHODS
 [Section Six: outcome comparison, historical distance, cost
 accounting]. Direct confrontation = CID without guaranteed
 resolution. Most dangerous intervention error: treating T9
 as T3 [analyst bias] and applying standard correction.
 Do not conflate with self-critical axis maintenance [GENUINE
 AXIS; SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT -- THREE-STATE block]: self-
 critical keeps GA examined; T9 blocks safe admission. Close
 topic; opposite intervention path.
 Cross-ref: SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT [THREE-STATE block]; GENUINE
 AXIS [SELF-CRITICAL vs T9 adjacency]; [CASE:RAND_DRAGONMOUNT]
 [T9 resolution]; T9 SOFT EXPOSURE METHODS [Section Six]; T9 IN
 POLITICS [applied Section Five -- dedicated politics home]
 LIBERALISM AS PARTIAL STATE RELIGION [Section Six -- T9 at
 ideological scale]; CC INVERSION [T9 produces CC inversion --
 correction becomes identity threat].

PRE-RESTORATION C1 AUDIT PROCEDURE [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Problem: Diagnostic Collapse occurs when C2 restoration fires
 terminal C1. The intervention rule [C1 audit before C2 opening]
 requires a procedure for assessing C1 substrate state when C2 is
 captured -- False Coherence hides C1 state from inside. Track B
 unavailable. Track A unreliable [behavioral surface captured].
 Track C is the primary instrument: physical outputs independent of
 intent, available from outside the captured boundary.

 PROXY 1 -- OUTPUT/INPUT RATIO TREND [long window]:
 Track ratio of what system produces vs consumes over extended
 window [minimum 5-10 years institutional; decades civilizational].
 C1 healthy: ratio stable or improving.
 C1 degrading: ratio declining over time even while stated metrics
 hold. Soviet confirmation: GDP growth rate declined every half-
 decade 1965-1985; budget deficit 2-3% to 6% in single year.
 C1 terminal: ratio inverted; deficit financing; external subsidy
 required to maintain output appearance.
 Operational limit: use physical proxies where possible
 [infrastructure construction rate, hard currency reserves,
 emigration rate, caloric availability]. Physical outputs harder
 to falsify than reported statistics.

 PROXY 2 -- T1.4 CONCENTRATION [substrate dependency audit]:
 How many independent substrate sources does C1 run on?
 C1 healthy: multiple independent substrate sources.
 C1 fragile: 1-2 dominant sources. External shock = collapse.
 C1 terminal candidate: single source with known depletion rate.
 Soviet confirmation: oil revenue ~60% of hard currency.
 Oil price collapse [1986] readable from export structure data.
 Procedure: identify top 3 substrate sources. If top > 50% with
 known depletion rate or price volatility: flag T1.4 High.

 PROXY 3 -- INDEPENDENT INSTITUTIONAL OUTPUT [civil substrate scan]:
 Does system produce independent institutional outputs outside
 extraction architecture? Labor organizations, professional
 associations, independent press, academic output, civil society.
 C1 substrate present: independent outputs across multiple domains.
 C1 terminal: no independent output. All organizational life runs
 through or adjacent to extraction architecture.
 Tunisia vs Libya confirmation: UGTT [labor], bar association,
 human rights groups documented in Tunisia pre-2011. Libya:
 42-year deliberate institutional destruction. Zero independent
 output. Readable before intervention.
 Score 0-3: weight by independence from state, documented activity
 record, cross-domain presence [labor + legal + press stronger
 than single domain].

 PROXY 4 -- EMIGRATION / BRAIN DRAIN RATE [substrate exit signal]:
 C1 healthy: emigration at background level.
 C1 degrading: elevated emigration among high-function population
 [engineers, doctors, entrepreneurs]. Substrate self-sorting.
 C1 terminal: mass emigration or systematic brain drain.
 Why readable through captured C2: border crossing data, diaspora
 size, remittance flows. Cannot be falsified as easily as
 production statistics.
 Adjustment: if ECC high [exit costs suppressed], emigration rate
 understates C1 degradation. If ECC high AND institutional
 output low: C1 may be more degraded than signal shows.

 PROXY 5 -- CORRECTION REACH [who does correction fire against?]:
 C1 healthy: correction fires against high-power deviators.
 C1 degrading: correction fires only against low-power targets.
 C1 terminal: correction fires against correction attempts.
 System consuming its own CC to protect extraction architecture.
 Why readable: prosecution records, accountability outcomes,
 dismissal patterns are Track C. Who gets corrected is a
 physical output.
 Libya: 42-year record of correction targeting political
 opposition, not institutional deviation. Score 0 readable.

 COMPOSITE C1 SUBSTRATE SCORE:
 Run all five proxies. Score each 0 [terminal] to 2 [healthy].
 Max = 10.
 8-10: C1 recoverable. C2 restoration likely produces correction
 burst followed by stabilization [C1 blind recovery trajectory].
 5-7: C1 fragile. C2 restoration may produce partial recovery or
 False Coherence reformation. High uncertainty. Extended
 observation recommended before C2 intervention.
 3-4: C1 severely degraded. C2 restoration high risk of Diagnostic
 Collapse. Restore C1 substrate first [independent institutions,
 substrate diversification, exit cost reduction].
 0-2: C1 terminal. C2 restoration = Diagnostic Collapse.
 Containment only. External C2 opening actively harmful.
 [Libya 2011: score ~1. Soviet 1986: score ~2-3.]

 HONEST LIMITS: SL3 [survivorship bias -- Track C long-window data
 biased toward systems that survived to be documented]. T4
 [temporal -- proxies degrade beyond ~50 years for institutional
 cases]. T3 Medium [all five proxies are Track C only]. T6
 [observer effect -- if audit procedure known by system being
 audited, may produce defensive output changes; audit without
 disclosure when possible].
 Cross-ref: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [the failure this procedure
 prevents]; CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [Proxy 5 mechanism]
 EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational -- Proxy 1 trajectory]
 READING PROCEDURE [Section Four -- parent procedure].

GENUINE AXIS VERIFICATION PROTOCOL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Problem: GA cannot be verified from inside a captured system.
 Formation Capture installs axis before examination is possible
 CC runs on top of the captured axis. Introspection insufficient.
 Libet finding: intent precedes deliberative reasoning -- axis is
 prior; reasoning operates on it.
 Solution: three-method triangulation + dual-layer check.

 METHOD 1 -- EXTERNAL TRIANGULATION:
 Require two or more independent instruments with different blind
 spots. Convergence increases confidence; cannot guarantee.
 Instruments with identical blind spots do not triangulate.
 Procedure: name each instrument; identify its capture
 vulnerabilities. If all share a vulnerability: flag T3.
 Two instruments with different blind spots converging: moderate
 confidence. Three or more: high confidence. T8 partial applies.

 METHOD 2 -- PROCESS TRACE:
 GA more legible through formation history than introspection.
 Key indicators:
 Axis revisions under genuine cost [S5 signal] -- tested and
 survived is harder to fake than untested.
 Behavioral consistency under pressure without reasoning
 support -- somatic response under genuine cost harder to
 fake than reasoning.
 Formation history: axis from traceable friction + preserved
 exit is more legible than axis that appeared fully formed.
 Score: clear friction + preserved exit + documented revision
 under cost = process trace positive. Controlled formation = T3.

 METHOD 3 -- FALSIFICATION THRESHOLD:
 Define what output pattern would force a GA-substituted reading.
 If system cannot name that pattern: T3 High on GA.
 Procedure: before reading, state what behavioral outputs would
 confirm GA substitution. Must be specific, observable, not
 already explained away by system's own framing.
 If threshold named but untestable in practice: T3 Medium.
 If named and testable: proceed with reading.

 METHOD 4 -- DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY CHECK:
 Somatic signal accurate AND deliberative read correct. Both
 required. One degraded = capture window open regardless.
 Somatic layer degrades first under ICD, chronic stress,
 optimization pressure. Deliberative layer can appear intact
 while GA is already compromised -- most dangerous configuration
 because C2 reads cleanly on captured substrate.
 Population implication: default modern environments systematically
 degrade somatic layer, making TRANSPARENT COHERENCE the practical
 ceiling for most individuals.

 COMPOSITE GA CONFIDENCE:
 Low: single instrument, no process trace, threshold not named.
 Moderate: two instruments with different blind spots, partial
 trace, threshold named.
 High ceiling [T3]: any recursion closure -- best available
 reading, confidence capped. T8 partial. Does not void reading.

 SHORTHAND [four questions before any GA reading]:
 [1] What would axis substitution look like in outputs?
 [2] What is the formation history?
 [3] What instruments are being used and what are their blind spots?
 [4] Are both somatic and deliberative layers intact?
 If none answerable: document only.
 Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [Section One -- what is being verified]
 T8 [RECURSIVE CLOSURE -- reason this protocol is required]
 SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE [Method 4 -- somatic layer primacy]
 PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [formation history evidence type].

COHERENCE TRAJECTORY MODEL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Problem: True Coherence as calibration point is unreachable
 [Population Coherence Ceiling; circular dependency confirmed].
 Fixed ideal anchor requires confirmed ceiling case. None exists.
 Solution: calibrate against floor [minimum viable C1 -- confirmed
 at cellular minimum]. Measure trajectory not position.

 REFERENCE POINTS:
 Floor [confirmed]: C1 functional [GC + SP + CC]. Real,
 measurable, confirmed independently at cellular scale.
 Ceiling [direction vector, not position]: True Coherence is a
 gradient direction. Ceiling case need not exist to define
 direction. All readings expressed as trajectory relative to
 floor.

 TRAJECTORY MEASUREMENT:
 Step 1: Floor reading. Is C1 functional? At what level?
 Step 2: Cycle interference pattern. Which cycles running?
 Which degraded? Interference type [C1 blind / starved /
 locked / full]?
 Step 3: Trajectory. Compare to nearest historical reading.
 Direction: toward more cycles functional, or fewer?
 Rate: fast / slow / holding / recovering?
 Step 4: Momentum. What is causing trajectory?
 Self-reinforcing or self-limiting?

 OUTPUT FORMAT [replaces coherence score alone]:
 Floor status: [C1 functional / degraded / collapsed]
 Cycle pattern: [which running, which interfering, which absent]
 Trajectory: [toward / holding / away] at [rate]
 Momentum: [cause + self-reinforcing / self-limiting]
 Horizon: [window before trajectory produces qualitative change]
 T3 level: [Low / Medium / High]

 WHY TRAJECTORY BEATS POSITION:
 0.6 degrading fast is more dangerous than 0.4 recovering.
 Position-only reading misses this. Trajectory directly answers
 "where is the leverage?" -- the framework's primary function.
 Cross-ref: HELL AS DIRECTION [Section Eight -- same principle
 trajectory over position]; DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [what happens
 when trajectory read incorrectly]; PRE-RESTORATION C1 AUDIT
 [above -- reading floor before intervention].

AXIOM CAPTURE [weapon -- v25; L5 failure mode]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 A system applies rigorous L4 methodology to installed moral
 foundations, producing correct-seeming conclusions from a
 captured base. The methodology is genuinely rigorous; the
 capture is at the axiom layer below it.

 MECHANISM:
 At L4: system revises how it revises. Meta-correction is real.
 Axiom Capture: the moral axioms feeding the correction
 methodology are installed rather than examined. The system
 applies its genuine correction capacity to premises it
 cannot question because the premises feel self-evident --
 they are the lens through which everything else is evaluated.
 Result: intellectually rigorous outputs from a captured base.
 The conclusions are internally consistent. They follow
 validly from the premises. The premises are wrong.
 Why hardest to detect:
 [1] Methodology appears maximally rigorous from outside.
 [2] System cannot see the capture from inside [captured
 axioms feel like reality, not like axioms].
 [3] The tell is what the system cannot question, not
 what it gets wrong. Axiom Capture produces
 characteristic blind spots at the moral-foundation
 layer with high resolution everywhere else.

 DISTINCTION FROM ADJACENT WEAPONS:
 L4 MIMICRY: fake meta-correction. System appears to revise
 methodology but isn't. Axiom Capture: genuine meta-
 correction applied to installed foundations. Revision is
 real; axioms being revised are captured.
 FORMATION CAPTURE: installs content during the Nascent
 formation window. Axiom Capture can occur post-formation
 -- any sufficiently powerful epistemic environment
 [total institution, ideology, professional formation]
 can install axioms that feel self-evident to a formed
 instrument.
 REFLEXIVITY MIMICRY: captured feedback loop at the
 correction layer. Axiom Capture: functional feedback
 loop at the correction layer, capture one level deeper
 at the axiom layer.

 TELLS:
 What cannot be questioned: what premises are immune to
 the same falsification standard applied to everything
 above them?
 Domain blindness with domain competence: high-functioning
 rigorous analysis in most domains; one domain or one
 type of question consistently exempt from examination.
 Characteristic outputs: rigorous derivations of
 conclusions that serve the installed axiom set.
 The rigor is the tell -- a sloppy system isn't running
 Axiom Capture, it's just sloppy.

 PRIMARY CASES:
 [CASE:LYSENKO] [S:3]: Lysenkoism in Soviet biology 1948-
 1964. Rigorous internal methodology [peer review within
 accepted frame, consistent application of dialectical
 materialism, genuine revision of specific findings within
 the frame] applied to installed axiom [dialectical
 materialism as immune to falsification from biological
 findings]. Produced famine-scale consequences from
 correct-seeming agronomic conclusions.
 Framework design [S:2 -- T8]: GOOD/EVIL operational
 definitions explicitly held open to axiom-revision.
 This is L5 design property, not L5 achievement claim.
 T8 applies -- analyst instrument reading own design.

 Cross-ref: L5 [TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION -- the level
 at which Axiom Capture becomes visible and correctable]
 L4 MIMICRY [distinct -- fake vs genuine meta-correction]
 FORMATION CAPTURE [installs during Nascent window; Axiom
 Capture operates post-formation at axiom layer]; REFLEXIVITY
 MIMICRY [capture at correction layer vs capture at axiom
 layer]; DOMAIN COHERENCE DIFFERENTIAL [Axiom Capture can
 be domain-specific -- DCD at the axiom layer]; GOOD / EVIL
 [operational definitions are instruments aimed at axis,
 not axioms -- L5 holds them open to revision].


L4 MIMICRY [candidate failure mode -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 WHY NEEDED:
 Coherence Mimicry confirmed at L1-L3 [HAL 9000 primary
 proof at L3; multiple extraction mechanism entries for L1-L2].
 L4 confirmation requires positive cases [three confirmed:
 Framework, Benedictine, Jesuit]. Without a named failure
 mode, confirmation criteria are asymmetric -- any apparent
 L4 is confirmatory; no case can disconfirm. Asymmetric
 confirmation = the same structure as Vague Captured Field.
 The framework cannot claim L4 is confirmed if it has no
 mechanism to detect false positives.

 CANDIDATE FAILURE MODES [not yet confirmed]:

 REFLEXIVITY Mimicry:
 System produces visible feedback pathway from correction
 outputs to correction architecture [appears reflexive]
 while feedback is routed to a captured surface. The
 architecture receives what it is supposed to receive --
 but the surface is wrong. Correction outputs are
 processed; the conclusions are captured before
 integration.
 Distinction from standard CC capture: standard capture
 is at object level. Reflexivity Mimicry is capture at
 meta-correction level. The system appears to correct
 its correction patterns while actually correcting
 toward monopolist benefit at meta level.
 Candidate case: academic reform cycles -- meta-reviews
 of peer review process appear reflexive [feedback
 pathway visible]; outputs consistently reproduce the
 captured surface [same journals, same metrics, same
 gatekeepers]. Stress event test: leadership change
 produces no change in captured surface. Possible
 Reflexivity Mimicry.
 Not yet confirmed: no direct T8 examination, no
 mechanism trace established.

 INTEGRATION Mimicry:
 System acknowledges correction pattern failure and
 incorporates language of revision into operating
 structure without changing operating structure.
 Distinct from Reflexive Oscillation [which identifies,
 fails to integrate, cycles back] -- Integration Mimicry
 integrates the language of the correction, not the
 correction itself. Terminology changes; mechanism
 stable.
 Tell: successive rounds of reform language that do not
 change measured outputs. Each generation of reform
 terminology absorbs the previous round's critique
 without changing the mechanism being critiqued.
 Candidate case [existing]: corporate DEI cycles --
 each generation of diversity language absorbs the
 previous critique [we need diversity -> we need
 equity -> we need inclusion -> we need belonging]
 without changing measured representation or
 structural outcomes. Full language Integration
 zero mechanism change.
 Not yet confirmed: mechanism trace required. Could
 be Integration Mimicry or genuine slow-change
 Integration with shorter-than-generation cycles.
 Candidate case [v21 -- Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
 HOLACRACY / FLAT STRUCTURE DEPLOYMENTS. Extraction
 architectures that mimic generative structures by
 adopting distributed-authority language [circles,
 roles, self-management, no managers] while
 reproducing hierarchy under a new name. The
 correction: traditional hierarchy is extractive
 and concentrates CC at top. The Integration
 Mimicry: holacracy absorbs this critique by
 adopting the language of distributed CC without
 transferring actual routing boundary control or
 correction capacity to the distributed nodes.
 The new terminology is integrated; the power
 structure is stable.
 Tell: stress event test -- leadership change or
 financial pressure. If the distributed authority
 collapses to traditional hierarchy immediately
 under stress, Integration Mimicry confirmed.
 Genuine L4 distributed structure would maintain
 distributed CC under stress precisely because
 that is what it is. Mimicry collapses because
 the distributed language was never load-bearing.
 Additional tell: who can fire whom. If the
 "circles" cannot remove the founder or senior
 leadership without external legal mechanism,
 the four-condition test fails at condition 4
 [activation requires central node's permission].
 Fake distributed authority confirmed.
 ZAPPOS STRESS EVENT [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
 Tony Hsieh death November 2020 = completed
 genuine leadership stress event. Result:
 holacracy collapsed in two phases. Phase 1:
 quiet drift away from the model while Hsieh
 still alive [began 2019-2020] -- model was not
 load-bearing even under him. Phase 2: full
 reversion to Amazon hierarchy immediately after
 death. Amazon subsumed departments, laid off
 ~300 employees [20% of workforce], replaced
 branding, reasserted traditional performance
 targets. By 2025: "circles exist, so do team
 leads, but decision-making has returned to a
 more recognizable blend of structure,
 accountability, and leadership. Few would call
 it a Holacracy in the pure sense."
 Key finding: distributed authority was maintained
 by the founder's personal force of will not by
 structural CC. Once the founder departed, Amazon
 [the actual central node -- Zappos sold to
 Amazon 2009] reasserted control immediately.
 Condition 4 confirmed failed: only five people
 in the organization had authority to fire
 someone during Hsieh's tenure. The circles
 could not remove the founder or Amazon without
 external legal mechanism. Four-condition test
 fails at condition 4.
 CONSTITUTIONAL AUTHORITY TEST [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
 HolacracyOne owns and licenses the constitution.
 Circles cannot revise the operating framework
 without constitutional permission from the
 external licensor. Distributed authority
 operates inside a framework it cannot change.
 Condition 4 confirmed failed at constitutional
 level independent of the Zappos case.
 STATUS: INTEGRATION MIMICRY CONFIRMED.
 Language of distributed authority integrated
 mechanism [actual routing control] never
 distributed. The model collapsed immediately
 when the founder's personal enforcement
 capacity was removed. The distributed
 authority was the legitimacy surface; Amazon's
 routing boundary control was the actual
 mechanism throughout.
 Sources: Quartz [2020]; eMarketer/WSJ [2023]
 Medium/Brinsa [2025]; Knowledge@Wharton [2016]
 INSEAD case [2022].
 Measurement instrument: four-condition CC test
 applied to distributed nodes [can they fire
 leadership without permission?] + Generational
 Reset test on founder departure. Both tests
 confirm INTEGRATION MIMICRY.
 Promote to: primary case [S3 -- E3:
 real-world confirmed, multiple independent
 sources, Zappos + constitutional authority
 independent confirmation; M3: mechanism
 traced [founder enforcement as hidden central
 node, Amazon as actual routing controller]
 F2: falsification condition stated and
 tested -- if distributed authority had held
 post-Hsieh, candidate would have weakened
 it did not hold].

 System transmits apparent methodology [looks like T3.1
 high -- procedure is transmitted, not conclusion] that
 is actually a locked conclusion about methodology.
 The transmitted package is "how to run our correction
 procedure" as a conclusion rather than "how to examine
 and revise correction procedures" as a method.
 Tell: the transmitted procedure cannot be revised by
 successors without being told they are doing it wrong.
 Methodology-as-conclusion = Fertile Lock at meta level.
 Candidate case: Agile software methodology [Population
 B -- DISAMBIGUATED v18]. Scrum ceremonies transmitted
 as locked procedure in organizations where questioning
 the ceremony is "not doing Agile right." Meta-question
 absent. The ceremony is the conclusion; revision
 capacity not transmitted. This is specifically
 Population B [see L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION -- Active
 Test Cases for split definition].
 Disambiguation: Population A [Agile orgs that have
 revised their own process based on retrospective
 outputs] is the L4 PROPAGATION confirmation candidate.
 Population B is the Propagation Mimicry candidate.
 These are separate predictions about different orgs,
 not contradictory predictions about the same test.
 Not yet confirmed: requires collecting Population B
 outcome data [Generational Reset on team turnover,
 ceremony preserved unchanged across generations].
 Measurement instrument: defined in L4 PROPAGATION
 PREDICTION entry -- behavioral test, not survey.

 IMPOSSIBILITY ARGUMENT [track -- do not close without
 examining]:
 Argument for impossibility: genuine REFLEXIVITY cannot
 be mimicked because it requires actual feedback to
 correction architecture, not surface performance of
 feedback. At L1-L3, captured surfaces can maintain
 appearance. At L4, the feedback goes to the architecture
 itself -- you cannot fake receiving feedback you are
 not receiving without the fake becoming indistinguishable
 from the real at all levels. If L4 Mimicry is
 structurally impossible, the confirmation asymmetry
 problem is resolved differently: L4 is self-confirming
 by structural necessity once the three tension units
 are operational.
 Counterargument: INTEGRATION Mimicry does not require
 faking feedback reception. It requires that the
 integration step captures the correction output before
 it changes the architecture. The feedback is received
 the integration is captured. This may be possible
 without the impossibility argument applying.
 Resolution: close as impossible only if argument
 eliminates INTEGRATION Mimicry specifically. Currently
 open.

 CONFIRMATION REQUIREMENT:
 L4 Mimicry confirmed when: documented case where all
 three tension units appear functional at T8 examination
 but stress event [leadership change, institutional
 crisis, successor-generation review] reveals meta-
 correction architecture was captured, oscillating, or
 transmitting procedure-conclusions rather than procedure-
 methods.
 Candidate stress-event test: any institution claiming L4
 record with 2+ generations -- does Generational Reset
 occur on leadership change? If yes: Mimicry candidate.
 If no: confirmation strengthens.
 No confirmed case in current library.

 FALSIFICATION OF THIS WIP ENTRY:
 L4 Mimicry does not exist as a failure mode if the
 impossibility argument holds for all three candidate
 subtypes. Close if: [1] impossibility argument
 extended to cover INTEGRATION Mimicry, AND [2] no
 candidate case survives T8 examination with confirmed
 Mimicry mechanism. Keep open if either condition unmet.

L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [v18 -- long_term queue]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Hypothesized state between confirmed institutional L4
 [Cistercian, Jesuit] and L5 [undefined].

 OPERATIONAL DEFINITION:
 L4 meta-correction running at civilizational scale
 with method [not just conclusions] crossing the
 generational transmission boundary intact. Distinct
 from institutional L4 [contained, non-civilizational]
 and from L5 [unknown content, requires L4.5 as
 precondition].

 STATUS: No confirmed positive case in library.
 S3 PATTERN EVIDENCE [v18]: three independent near-cases
 with consistent termination mechanism. Not a confirmed
 case -- termination mechanism is consistent but
 absence of positive case means causal direction is
 not confirmed. Pattern is S3 [E3: three real-world
 independent near-cases; M3: mechanism described +
 confirmed across cases + consistent with ANTI-COHERENCE
 COST ASYMMETRY logic; F2: falsification condition
 stated]. Promote to S4 when positive case confirmed.

 THREE NEAR-CASES [all terminated before transmission
 completed]:
 Athens [~450-380 BCE]:
 Socratic method as institutionalized meta-
 correction. Procedure [dialectic] transmitted not
 conclusion. Cross-domain outputs [Plato, Aristotle,
 geometry, logic] only possible with L4 running.
 Termination: execution of Socrates [399 BCE] =
 correction capacity visible enough to threaten
 political architecture. Destroyed before
 transmission window closed.
 Islamic Golden Age [~800-1100 CE]:
 Translation movement + ijtihad [independent
 reasoning] as institutionalized SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
 at civilizational scale. Cross-domain synthesis
 [algebra, optics, medicine, philosophy] confirmed.
 Termination: Ash'ari theological capture of ijtihad
 [closing of independent reasoning ~1100 CE].
 Correction monopoly constructed at theological
 scale. Diagnostic Collapse candidate. Recovery
 partial, never restored.
 Scottish Enlightenment [~1740-1790]:
 High-density L4-adjacent node [Hume, Smith, Hutton,
 Black, Watt] in genuine cross-domain intellectual
 friction. Outputs [political economy, geology,
 chemistry, steam engineering] only possible with
 meta-correction running cross-domain.
 Termination: outputs extracted, method abandoned.
 Protestant Reformation pattern at Enlightenment
 scale -- conclusions crossed transmission boundary,
 procedure did not. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.

 CONSISTENT TERMINATION MECHANISM [across all three]:
 Correction capacity became visible enough to threaten
 extraction architecture. Architecture absorbed or
 destroyed it before transmission closed. Political
 or theological capture in every case.

 NEAR-CASE STRESS-TEST [v18 -- alternative explanations]:
 ALT 1 [Athens]: Socrates killed for personal enemies
 and impiety charge, not systemic correction threat.
 Against: the impiety charge is the mechanism, not
 an alternative. Correction capacity at civilizational
 visibility = institutional threat. The legal vehicle
 is extraction architecture using available instrument.
 That the charge was formally religious does not
 change the structural function. Plato's subsequent
 withdrawal [Republic as political theory, not
 engaged practice] and Aristotle's caution confirm
 the chilling effect on meta-correction as method.
 Termination mechanism holds.
 ALT 2 [Islamic Golden Age]: decline was Mongol
 invasion [1258 Baghdad sack], not theological
 capture.
 Against: Ash'ari capture of ijtihad is documented
 ~1100 CE -- 158 years before the Mongol sack.
 The correction methodology was already closed
 before the military catastrophe. Mongol invasion
 accelerated substrate collapse but was not the
 termination mechanism for L4.5. Two separate
 events; framework claim is about the theological
 capture of ijtihad, not the military collapse.
 Termination mechanism holds independently.
 ALT 3 [Scottish Enlightenment]: outputs were
 transmitted [political economy, geology] -- this
 contradicts TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.
 Against: this is the TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
 finding, not a contradiction of it. Conclusions
 transmitted; method did not. Adam Smith's market
 conclusions were transmitted as doctrine; his
 method [empirical observation of labor conditions,
 outputs as truth test] was not transmitted as
 structure. Ricardo and subsequent economists
 transmitted Smith's conclusions as locked frame.
 The outputs crossed the boundary; the procedure
 that generated them did not. This is the
 Protestant Reformation pattern at intellectual
 scale. Termination mechanism holds.
 Stress-test result: all three alternative explanations
 examined. None overturn the consistent termination
 mechanism. S3 pattern evidence confirmed v18.

 INTERNET-ERA POSITIVE CANDIDATE [v18 -- open]:
 Anti-capture architecture requirement [per PREDICTION
 below] raises the question whether distributed
 digital infrastructure constitutes an anti-capture
 instrument. Candidate: open-source / open-access
 ecosystem as distributed correction architecture.
 Wikipedia + FOSS + arXiv + peer-to-peer protocols
 collectively distribute correction capacity across
 nodes that no single political or theological actor
 can monopolize.
 Against: platform capture [GitHub/Microsoft, Reddit
 API closures, Twitter/X centralization] shows
 the architecture is not immune to capture. Each
 node individually capturable; the question is
 whether all nodes simultaneously capturable.
 Current status: not confirmed. Requires: evidence
 of meta-correction [not just information] running
 at civilizational scale with method [not just
 conclusion] transmitting across generations.
 Wikipedia has one generational cycle [~20 years].
 Early evidence: methodology [NPOV + talk page
 dispute resolution] shows some structural
 transmission. Counter-evidence: culture wars
 Wikipedia suggest method is under sustained
 capture pressure. Track.
 Note: this is the first internet-era candidate
 in the library. Warrants a dedicated case entry
 once one full generational cycle is documentable.

 PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
 L4.5 requires anti-capture architecture as
 precondition. Correction instrument must be
 distributed enough that no single political or
 theological node can monopolize it.
 Current candidate: none confirmed.
 Falsification condition: confirmed case of
 civilizational L4 transmission completing without
 capture termination. If found, upgrade to confirmed
 finding and open L5 prediction window.

 L5 NOTE:
 Content of L5 unpredictable from current evidence.
 Structural signature probably: something that only
 becomes possible when meta-correction is the default
 operating mode of a civilization rather than the
 exception produced by rare high-density nodes under
 favorable conditions. MORAL LEGIBILITY operating at
 civilizational scale [ethics-as-empirical-instrument
 as default not exception] is one candidate property.
 Cannot confirm without L4.5 completing first.
 Honest limit declared. Do not speculate beyond this.

SCORING AND MEASUREMENT PROCEDURES
============================================================================================================================================

============================================================================================================================================

READING PROCEDURE [12 steps]:
 1. BOUNDARY TEST: all three named and distinct?
 2. CYCLE STATUS: C1/C2/C3 for Boundary A.
 3. TRAJECTORY: direction and rate.
 4. COUNTERFACTUAL: gap between stated function and actual?
 5. CC TEST: distributed or monopolized? Aimed at actual cause
 or proxy? [T2.2 A+B both required]
 6. WINDOW SPLIT: reading change across time windows?
 If yes, name correct window.
 7. ANALYST PRIOR TEST: reading change if prior reversed?
 If yes, flag and state both readings.
 8. ADVERSARIAL READING: invert conclusion. Does it survive?
 9. THIRD POPULATION TEST: who is not in A or B?
 10. EXTRACTION MECHANISM BENEFICIARY CHECK: follow the surplus.
 11. PREDICTION: one falsifiable claim with timeline and
 falsification condition.
 12. EXTERNAL ACTOR DISCLOSURE: name all external actors.
 Failure to name = T3 High on meta-boundary.
 Cross-ref: POLITICAL MEASUREMENT PROTOCOL [Section Five applied --
 steps 1-10 = 10-test battery; pre-reading + companion requirements
 formalized for political entries]; SECTION FIVE EPISTEMIC STATUS
 [applied Section Five -- step 11 maps to causal-claim requirement].

DIRECTIONALITY COMPOSITE [S1-S6 scoring instrument -- v25
 promoted from]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Six independent signatures for axis quality and directional
 assessment. Equal weight. Maps to Axis 2 [coherence depth]
 in the THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL.
 Combined output: [score / S1-S6 composite / geometric
 coordinate] = full framework reading.

 SIX SIGNATURES [S1-S6]:
 S1 NOVEL INPUT RESPONSE:
 1 = integrates genuinely novel inputs requiring axis
 adjustment. 0.5 = evidence present, condition
 compromised. 0 = inaccessible.
 Procedure: present with input contradicting current axis.
 Genuine integration = axis revision visible in subsequent
 behavior. Recombination = input explained using existing
 axis without movement. Score 0 if axis does not move
 despite genuine contradiction. Historical reconstruction
 preferred.
 S2 SCARCITY BEHAVIOR:
 1 = axis holds under resource drop without external supply.
 0.5 = partial evidence. 0 = inaccessible.
 S3 CORRECTION INITIATION:
 1 = corrections happen before external pressure arrives.
 0.5 = timing ambiguous or observer effect. 0 = inaccessible.
 Procedure: identify earliest documented correction signal.
 Reconstruct timeline of external pressure relative to it.
 Correction precedes pressure = 1. Pressure predates
 correction = 0. Ambiguous = 0.5.
 S4 CONTESTATION ASYMMETRY:
 1 = cost consistent across group positions inside boundary.
 0.5 = partial data. 0 = inaccessible.
 S5 SELF-ADVERSARIAL CORRECTION CAPACITY:
 1 = generates and integrates genuine challenges to own axis
 without external prompt, at reputational or personal cost.
 0 = correction only against external deviation, never
 against own axis outputs. 0.5 = partial.
 Strongest single indicator of genuine GA.
 Primary evidence: Emet-Selch [FF14], Wittgenstein, Le Guin.
 S6 EXTERNAL CORRECTION RECEPTION:
 1 = integrates genuine correction from outside boundary at
 reputational or operational cost. Behavior changes in
 direction of correction within window.
 0.5 = engagement without behavioral change.
 0 = structurally deaf to external input.
 Primary proof: South Africa TRC [external pressure →
 internal legal change]. Counterproof: US police reform
 [external correction documented, no behavioral change].

 OUTPUT FORMAT:
 Score / signatures accessed / ceiling flag.
 0.75 from 2 of 6 [ceiling limited] = provisional, flagged.
 0.75 from 6 of 6 = strong claim at same score.

 CHANGE DIRECTIONALITY TEST [companion]:
 Internal change before external pressure = positive signal.
 External-pressure-responsive change = negative signal.
 High coherence system that never changes = early Axis-Lock.
 Low coherence change = negative.

 Cross-ref: THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL [Axis 2 -- S1-S6
 composite maps to coherence depth position]; GENUINE AXIS
 [S5 primary indicator]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [S3, S6]
 TRUTH AXIS AND CYCLES UNIFICATION [vertical architecture
 this instrument scores against]; [scoring procedure
 -- combined output with S1-S6 and geometric coordinate].

AXIS ANALYSIS -- SOCIAL RISK DECLARATION [v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Axis analysis names what an instrument is actually running.
 This is the framework's highest-value and highest-risk operation.
 The risk is not that the reading is wrong -- it is that correct
 readings carry costs the framework does not automatically absorb.

 FAILURE MODES [structural, not ethical]:

 CORRECT READING / WRONG RECEPTION:
 The read is accurate. The receiver has not examined their own
 axis. Naming it before they have arrived at it is not helpful
 -- it is a formation violation. The framework produces true
 readings faster than most receivers can integrate them. Speed
 of reading is not the same as usefulness of reading. A correct
 axis read delivered ahead of the receiver's own examination
 produces defensiveness, not correction. The correction fires
 against the analyst, not the axis.
 Tell: receiver defends the axis being named rather than
 examining it. The reading was correct and useless.

 SOCIAL COST ASYMMETRY:
 Axis analysis in a social context assigns a person to a
 category [axis void, captured axis, genuine axis under FC]
 that carries moral weight in the framework. The receiver
 experiences it as judgment regardless of the analyst's
 structural intent. The distinction between "your axis is
 captured" and "you are bad" is load-bearing in the framework
 and invisible to most receivers. The framework's vocabulary
 does not come with its own translation layer. The analyst
 supplies the structural reading; the receiver supplies the
 social meaning.
 Tell: receiver hears a moral verdict. The framework read
 a mechanism. These are not the same event.

 WEAPONIZATION RISK:
 Framework vocabulary + axis analysis together produce a
 high-precision attack surface. "You're running Correction
 Theatre with a captured axis" is devastating as a social
 weapon regardless of whether it is accurate. High-precision
 structural readings can be deployed extractively. The
 framework has no mechanism to prevent this. The analyst
 does.
 Tell: the reading is delivered to produce a social effect
 [silence, submission, humiliation] rather than to produce
 a correction. The output is the damage, not the diagnosis.
 This is the framework being used as a weapon. Same mechanism
 as any other weapon: accurate-reading-as-extraction is still
 extraction.

 T3 ON SELF-READING [highest priority]:
 The framework's primary instrument is the analyst. Axis
 analysis applied outward without applying inward first is
 the exact error the framework identifies in every other
 system. The analyst's own axis is the highest-T3 item in
 any reading session. Before naming what someone else is
 running: what is the analyst running? What does the analyst
 want the reading to produce? If the answer is not
 "correction" -- stop.
 The framework can read other systems accurately while the
 analyst's axis is captured. The reading is still accurate.
 The deployment is still extraction. Accurate + extractive
 = still wrong.

 ANALYST AXIS -- DECLARED PRIOR [v22]:
 Pressure-formed genuine axis [see GENUINE AXIS --
 PRESSURE-FORMED pattern]. Truth-oriented not by moral
 choice but because the environment eliminated false axis
 options at formation cost. Self-deception was more expensive
 than honesty [HONESTY -- PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS instance].
 The axis is load-bearing in the sense that it has been tested
 under conditions that would have revealed capture if capture
 were present.
 FORMATION CONTEXT [declared -- v22]:
 Cult environment during formation window. High-control
 group deployment [partial or full CULT STACK]. Axis
 formed under capture pressure rather than in protected
 conditions. Exit achieved. Outcome: genuine axis intact
 some installed content likely still running; ongoing
 work to distinguish them.
 Epistemic consequence [named -- not a deficit]:
 Direct trust of internal signal is structurally
 unreliable given formation history. The instrument
 that would verify "this thought is mine" is the same
 instrument that was under capture pressure. Installed
 content feels like genuine axis from inside -- it was
 designed to. Correct post-exit behavior: require
 external grounding before trusting internal reads.
 The axis generates; verification requires external
 anchor. Two separate systems; only the verification
 layer was compromised during formation.
 This is not damaged epistemology. It is accurate
 epistemology given the instrument's known history.
 Singer, Herman, and Lifton all document this pattern
 in post-cult populations. Common. Structurally correct
 response to formation under capture pressure.
 T3 implication: analyst's internal reads carry higher
 T3 than standard. External behavioral record is the
 primary reference. Output verification [what did the
 axis actually produce] takes precedence over internal
 self-report [what the axis feels like it produced].
 AXIS GROUNDING [Track C] is secondary correction --
 compare internal read to record already gathered
 externally; not the first move when internal audit
 is avoided. Primary routing: Output Verification,
 External Reference Standard, L3.5 contact with
 independent instrument.
 Revisable: the axis can change with careful effort.
 Careful = maintaining the distinction between revision
 toward higher resolution [genuine development] and
 revision toward capture [drift under pressure]. The two
 directions feel similar from inside. The tell is the
 output: better readings or more comfortable ones.
 T3 application: the analyst's revision direction is the
 highest-T3 reading in any session. Self-reports of
 "developing the axis" require the same adversarial check
 as any other reading. Does the revision produce better
 readings or more socially comfortable ones? If
 consistently more comfortable: drift is the more likely
 explanation than development.
 This prior applies to all framework readings. It does not
 exempt the framework from its own instrument. The
 framework's outputs are the behavioral record of the
 analyst's axis. That record is what it is.

 WHEN AXIS ANALYSIS SHOULD FIRE:
 Self-examination [no social cost asymmetry].
 Invited reading [receiver asked; reception is prepared].
 Structural documentation [case library; no live receiver].
 System-level reading [institution, not person; mechanism
 not judgment].
 After the receiver has arrived at their own examination
 [the reading confirms, not installs].

 WHEN AXIS ANALYSIS SHOULD NOT FIRE:
 Social contexts where the reading will be received as
 judgment regardless of framing.
 Receivers who have not examined their own axis.
 Contexts where the analyst has a stake in the reading's
 social effect [weaponization risk high].
 Any context where "I am doing a structural reading" is
 being used as cover for "I want to win this".

 HONEST LIMIT:
 The framework produces better structural readings than most
 social contexts can receive. This is not the framework's
 failure -- it is a compatibility asymmetry between
 the reading's resolution and the receiver's current frame.
 The correct response is not to suppress the reading but to
 hold it until the receiver can use it. Undelivered correct
 readings are not wasted -- they inform the analyst's next
 move without producing social damage.
 A reading that cannot be received is not a reading that
 should be delivered.

 MASS ADOPTION HONEST LIMIT [v22]:
 NOT HONESTY [disambiguation -- see Section Two HONESTY]. Adoption
 ceiling / compatibility asymmetry at population scale -- not policy
 ledger standard.
 The framework is structurally blocked from mass adoption
 and this is correct. Not a failure; a property.
 WHY MASS ADOPTION IS BLOCKED:
 The framework requires a genuine axis to use correctly.
 Axis analysis -- the framework's highest-value operation
 -- produces defensiveness not correction in instruments
 without examined axes. T9 [non-reception] is structural
 at population scale. More framework, louder framework,
 better framework does not close that gap. The gap is not
 informational. Would-You-Kindly + population axis
 distribution = the framework's functional audience is
 a small proportion of the current population. The mass
 population includes a high proportion of captured and
 never-formed axes. The framework reads those accurately
 and names them. That reading is experienced as attack.
 CORRECT SCOPE:
 The framework is a Silent Alternative. Operates outside
 captured channels. Does not announce itself. Produces
 legibility of extraction architecture for instruments
 that can use it. Silent Alternatives do not require mass
 adoption to function. They require enough instruments
 with genuine axes to transmit the methodology across
 the generational boundary. One person with genuine
 methodology reaching the next generation outweighs
 a million people with the name but not the method.
 THE REAL RISK [not mass rejection -- capture]:
 The framework getting absorbed as vocabulary without
 methodology before reaching transmission threshold.
 Coherence Rent: extracting return from the framework's
 legitimacy surface without contributing to the axis
 that produces it. Propagation Mimicry :
 "framework-coded" language transmitted as locked
 procedure rather than correctable method. The name
 travels; the tool doesn't. The methodology fossilizes
 into jargon. This is the failure mode, not mass
 rejection. Mass rejection leaves the framework intact
 for the instruments that can use it. Capture hollows
 it from inside while the surface appears coherent.
 OUROBOROS AS STRUCTURAL DEFENSE:
 The symbol actively works against capture by property.
 See OUROBOROS [AS ANTI-CAPTURE MECHANISM].
 A captured framework that stops consuming itself is
 visibly broken by the symbol's own definition.
 The circuit isn't closing. The tell is built into
 the image.

 Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [what axis analysis reads]
 CORRECTION CAPACITY [the output axis analysis should
 produce in the receiver -- if CC does not fire, reading
 was not received]; FORMATION CAPTURE [naming FC to
 someone in a FC state is highest-risk axis analysis]
 COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [
 between reading and receiver]; T3 [analyst prior --
 apply inward before outward]; WEAPONIZATION [framework
 as weapon -- same mechanism as any other weapon]
 SOCIAL COMPLIANCE CHANNEL [receiver's social read
 of the analysis determines reception regardless of
 structural accuracy]; WORTHLESS DOING [axis analysis
 delivered without reception is worthless doing --
 output exists; loop does not close]; OUROBOROS
 [AS ANTI-CAPTURE MECHANISM -- symbol reads the
 deployer]; WOULD-YOU-KINDLY [population axis
 distribution as the structural reason mass adoption
 is blocked]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [correct scope
 for the framework].

TRAJECTORY INDICATORS:
 Toward coherence: CC firing against high-power deviators; C2
 instruments diversifying; extraction mechanisms decreasing or
 more visible; exit costs declining.
 Holding: CC fires against low-power targets only; C2 stable
 not expanding; extraction mechanism deployment steady.
 Away: CC conditions 2+4 weakening; C2 capture accelerating
 exit costs rising; external actor influence growing.

OUTPUT FORMAT:
 Boundary A/B/meta reading: [cycle status + trajectory]
 Centralization score: [0-4]
 Extraction mechanisms active: [list with beneficiary]
 External actors: [named]
 Adversarial reading: [stated]
 Honest limits: [T/SL flags]
 Prediction: [falsifiable + timeline + falsification condition]
 Protocol steps completed: [which ran, which skipped + why]
 Analyst instrument: [profile]
 Confidence: provisional / partial / confirmed

TRACK G [proxy SP indicators by nation/entity -- v18]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Use when Track B [direct output measurement] unavailable.
 Track G reads indirect signals that the primary SP channel is
 functioning or captured. Proxy indicators: whistleblowers active,
 investigative press present, protest movements functioning, market
 signals [capital flight, emigration rates], public inquiries with
 real outcomes.

 SCORING:
 1.0 = proxy channel active and functional
 0.5 = proxy channel present but suppressed [cost asymmetry active]
 0.0 = proxy channel eliminated

 INTERPRETATION:
 Track G = 0 confirms SP near-total capture.
 Track G = 1 + Track A SP absent = EXTERNALIZED SP state [SP exists
 but exits the boundary rather than circulating within it].
 Track G declining over time = extraction architecture consolidating.

 REFERENCE READINGS [v18 -- T3 applies; update when new data available]:
 United States: 0.5 [press functional; whistleblowers active

CLAIM-TYPE ORIENT [what this division is for]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Question:** Is there a word for **in between** objective and subjective?
 **Kit answer:** yes -- the desk terms below, mapped to **three bands**.
 **Not a fourth substance** -- a **routing grammar**: which kind of claim you are making,
 which test applies, which mistake you are avoiding.
 **Scope:** routing discipline only. Truth-contact bar stays order_truth METHOD OF TRUTH;
 substrate falsification stays Real + CASE.

WHY THE BINARY FAILS [objective vs subjective slogans]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **"Everything is subjective"** [full relativism] -- treats layoffs, deal close, and ledger
 events as **mere opinion**; avoids contact when power wants fog. Same error as **full
 relativism mistake** [order_truth METHOD OF TRUTH **Substrate vs pathos**] -- stated here as
 the slogan you will actually hear, not as a second finding.
 **"Nothing but objective"** [Objectivism / chart moralism] -- treats revenue, flop, or
 jersey as **universe verdict**; avoids contact when power wants virtue.
 **"Nothing is neutral"** [order_truth ANTI-NEUTRAL DOCTRINE] -- every frame, montage, venue,
 and referee pose carries **prior**; honesty = **name tilt**, not deny it.
 **Situated knowing** [feminist epistemology rhyme -- Haraway desk]: all contact is from a
 **position**; position ≠ permission to invent substrate.
 Cross-ref: order_truth METHOD OF TRUTH **Substrate vs pathos**; analyst_policy **SUBSTRATE
 PATHOS DESK SPLIT**.

CLAIM-TYPE VOCABULARY [intersubjective desk map -- routing labels only, not school proof]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Use these as **labels for claim-type**, not moral badges.

 | Term | Plain gloss | Kit band [below] |
 |------|-------------|------------------|
 | **Intersubjective** | Shared among persons under common rules; checkable together. | **Band 2** [substrate / procedure] |
 | **Situated** | From a position; prior visible; not private whim only. | **Band 1** + **Band 2** when declared |
 | **Procedural / methodological objectivity** | "Objective" = **follow the procedure** [battery, docket, primary]. | **Band 2** |
 | **Conventional** | True because **we treat it so** [money, rules, game genres]. | **Band 2** [institutional] |
 | **Institutional fact** [Searle rhyme] | Objective **given** collective acceptance [corp, property, closure PR]. | **Band 2** |
 | **Perspectival** [perspectivism] | Better/worse views; no view from nowhere. | **Band 1-2** bridge |
 | **Pragmatic warrant** [pragmatism] | Holds up in **inquiry and practice** over time. | **Band 2** outcome of tests |
 | **Bracketed phenomenology** | Experience described; **realness not asserted** as law. | **Band 1** [sophist ONTOLOGY-BRACKETED PHENOMENOLOGY VALUE] |

 **Reserved -- Band 3 [abstract]:** CENTER / TRUTH as **axis north** -- reachable as
 **orientation**, not as bias-free snapshot [truth-neutral maybe, unreachable].

 vector; **do not** dump Band 3 on Band 1 fights or Band 2 audits.

 settled Band 2. Rhyme applied **TRUE LIES** [Pass 1 literal strong; Pass 2 routing weak] --
 literal often Band 2; activation often Band 1 smuggled as Band 2.

OPERATIVE GOOD AND HARD-MODE PATH MENU [portable -- ABSTRACT export 2026-09-05; T3]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 **Easy-mode:** voluntary menu when enactment rails exist [people start; support after
 small coupled fix] -- **no forced climb**.
 **Hard-mode path menu [primary]:** when **easy enactment rails** blocked [mid capture
 expression≠enactment; no honest where] -- **more path types** open; **not normal-friendly**.
 **Ascension** = **forced climb** [hard-mode path kind only]. **Good** = **smart + voluntary**
 where voluntary leg exists [operative test -- not ethos performance]. **Major force to path**
 = **bad**. **Force as tool** on some paths [membrane hold; stop worst; proportional; stops
 when job done] ≠ **force → force → force** [recursion; tricks count].
 **Real priority:** working falsifiable on record -- cannot fake working long on Real bar.
 Cross-ref: GENERATIVE ORDER [above]; FUNCTIONAL ORDER [stress posture]; applied MID LADDER
 rhymes [enactment blocked -- pointer when assigned]; coherence_framework_v25_ABSTRACT.txt
 ANALYST ORB PATH AND HARD-MODE OPERATIVE ETHIC [PERSONAL BIAS; pointer only].

FUNCTIONAL ORDER [secondary to ORDER -- v25]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 POSITION: Secondary order posture -- subordinate to ORDER telos [ORDER TOWARD
 TRUTH, GENERATIVE ORDER primary]. Not a fourth order type. Operative stance when
 Order is under fracture, capture, or apex-window stress. Analyst-primary
 orientation: truth at max, high contact cost accepted, build what still works.

 PLAIN READ: Fix and maintain usable life under stress -- truth on center axis,
 costs priced in. No lie-mend for comfort [CASE:GOLDMASK lane]. No cosmological exit
 as default [Ranni lane]. No stay-and-stab loop as telos [stabber lane]. May use
 sibling lanes tactically; telos remains operative truth-order, not lane identity.

 ORDER SOLUTION VECTOR [operative core -- moved from METHOD OF TRUTH]:
 Order = solution vector [co-op + effort + farm clean + truth on center axis].
 Functional order = that vector **under stress** when capture, fracture, or
 BB-type apex-window frames threaten contact. Disorder ignores the solution
 lane [refusal of co-op + record + long trace]. Stable extraction ≠ Order.
 Illustration: [CASE:WISMUT_URANIUM_BIOREMEDIATION]. Contrast: FAKE ORDER
 ISLAND [bounded locale pretends Order; outer audit weak].

 TRUTH AT MAX [loads FUNCTIONAL ORDER -- full costs: theory TRUTH-AXIS
 ORIENTATION]:
 Procedure: follow read through dislike; behavioral record over slogan
 falsify before defend. Simple rule set; high contact cost.
 Benefits [truth-order primary]: cross-domain correction; dislike without
 disqualification; anti-theater immunity; lower rhetorical surface.
 Costs [accepted not disqualifying]: social isolation; truth-axis identity
 costume risk; non-propositional underweight; DCD misread; CC depletion
 disgust tax [hated truth-seeking]; trap-map bind under capture [theory
 PERSONAL PROTOCOLS MAP LAYER SPLIT -- operative vs published map
 blind action protocol when honest routing is punished].
 Contrast: identity-axis = complex rules protecting axis; truth-axis = simple
 hated standard with social and somatic price.

 DISLIKE-TO-EVIL ESCALATION [identity-axis default -- chat 2026-08-15]:
 Precondition: us-vs-them on most public routing. Dispreference alone is weak
 coalition signal; moral category ["evil," applied slur, tail-poster urgency]
 is strong jersey. Collapse move: reasoned dislike -> wholesale villain label
 without measurement pass.
 **Procedure [truth-order]:** name mechanism, cost, or mismatch; tag output not
 person; keep measurement [structural read] separate from judgment [person
 applies values]; hostile source still readable [dislike without
 disqualification].
 **Poster parallel:** miscalibrated tail stat on a poster [wrong denominator,
 urgency before base rate] = same salience operator as category slur from
 non-pursuit [applied "incel" on male selectivity / ICD withdrawal --
 separable from forum-toxic or shooter-tail pathology].
 **Not:** denying harm; mandatory neutrality; both-sides jersey theater.
 Cross-ref: theory EVIL [PERSON-RELATIVE JUDGMENT + MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT]
 SELECTIVE ANTI-UNIVERSAL [case B lane]; EDGE CASE UNIVERSALIZATION
 applied S6 UNUSABLE INVITATION [Schrödinger courtship / incel third lane].

 PEACEFUL TRUTH / AGGRESSIVE TRUTH [delivery modes -- same telos]:
 Same axis; different contact surface -- not weak vs strong truth.
 PEACEFUL: substrate, outputs, behavioral record, Step 0, Phase 1 SA [build
 elsewhere], EXIT-NARROW tactical give-up, T9 soft-exposure rhyme [outcome
 comparison without accusation trigger]. Legibility delayed.
 AGGRESSIVE: direct read, public falsification, named instrument + blind
 spots, disgust tax and isolation accepted. Phase 2 loud when forced
 confrontation when MSC+CT blocks quiet contact.
 **Blunt jersey read :** competing greatest-evil thesis +
 severity split requires direct delivery; T9 soft-exposure [outcome comparison,
 policy cost accounting] rejected as inaccurate substitute. TRAP 3 guard:
 blunt ≠ designated-evil inversion. Cross-ref: applied_s6 LIBERALISM AS
 PARTIAL STATE RELIGION [COMPETING GREATEST-EVIL JERSEY extension].
 Costumes: peaceful = silence without generative output [not SA]; aggressive
 = truth-as-identity performance [beneficiary-order in truth mask].
 Default peaceful where Phase 1 runway exists; escalate aggressive when
 silent substrate exhausted and quiet contact blocked.

 ANTI-CAPTURE LANES [effort types -- not SA; partial catalog]:
 **FORM NOT LAW [desk 2026-08-29]:** lane tags + disorder/order forms = routing **compress** --
 name failure shapes; route on substrate facts, not identity or command. **Fiction finds real:**
 cases_fiction specimens not Real [no Step 0 on fiction] -- useful to **locate** mechanism
 confirm on outputs/routing [TEST REAL]. Telos remains truth-order, not lane identity.
 Silent Alternative is one lane, not the name for every exit or strike.
 Lane tags: SILENT | EXIT-NARROW | WINDOW-STRIKE | IN-FRAME-MEND.
 Outcome tags: SUCCESS | PARTIAL | FAIL | STAY-FAIL | OSCILLATE | WRONG-LANE.
 Terminal note [WATF]: window-strike stay without voluntary exit → usual
 madness.
 Public names [adoption]: Build elsewhere | Step back | Keep hitting it |
 Patch the order.
 Social misreads [load-bearing]:
 STRAWMAN GUARD [stabber lazy -- directional; do not invert]:
 **Wrong:** stabber = lazy / under-work / quitter.
 **Right:** stabber → calls **others** lazy; others → median critique
 of stabber = **works too much** [won't stand down past voluntary exit].
 Failure = over-work on wrong ceiling [STAY-FAIL], not sloth.
 Ranni / SILENT -- "too much" [extremist, abandoner]; SA-can-complete not
 most-people win.
 [CASE:GOLDMASK] / mend -- confusion limit [sincere until mend patches lie].
 Stabber / WINDOW-STRIKE stay -- calls **others** lazy; median
 criticism **of** stabber is "works too much". Theory read: **best of
 Maiad + Mulak** [kill-BB telos + weakness from Maiad; sustained axis/
 SP leg from Mulak without Maiad's madness drift]. Upside: kill active
 crazy Mulak, destroy System -- wasted if cannot. Full BB vector catalog
 [high cross-ref]: [CASE:BIG_BROTHER_WATF SOLUTION VECTORS; REFORM READ].
 Maiad [he/him] -- **stabber-like at start**; drifted with madness over
 127 cycles; give-up returns to normal came under drift. Called
 quitter/lazy on give-up rounds [social misread -- not stabber lazy
 inversion]. Axis constant: kill Big Brother. Open: figure out how fast
 [next chapter].
 Mulak -- **active and crazy**; captured for BB usage [live apex operator].
 Stabber-path terminal target. Shadow-held damage-cap alternate:
 [CASE:BIG_BROTHER_WATF SHADOW VS OPERATORS].
 Specimens [grep CASE for full reads]:

 partial [choosers only].

 per-cycle sane; compounding cost if re-entry repeats.
 WINDOW-STRIKE + FAIL / EXIT-NARROW + oscillate: [CASE:MAIAD] [he/him]

 in/out cycles. Constant kill-BB telos; weakness knowledge; voluntary
 return to normal under drift. INTEGRATION failure on pace/method.
 Open: how fast [next chapter].

 WHEN SILENT + TRUTH FAIL [lane routing]:
 Truth at max stays on. What fails is silent substrate path [no Phase 1
 runway, legibility crossed, apex-window-only capture, MSC+CT blocks quiet
 contact]. Then sibling lanes apply -- not truth abandonment:
 [1] Substrate built, exit possible → SILENT + PARTIAL [Ranni].
 [2] No win this round, can return to normal → EXIT-NARROW [Maiad rhyme]
 constant kill-BB telos; tactical give-up; fix **pace/method** not
 goal; don't OSCILLATE blind.
 [3] Apex window, won't stand down → WINDOW-STRIKE [stabber]; synthesis
 bet [Maiad + Mulak theory]: kill active crazy Mulak, destroy System
 wasted grind if falsified. Reform-capture sibling when destroy off table:
 [CASE:BIG_BROTHER_WATF REFORM READ].
 [4] Fracture emergency, confusion limit not hit → IN-FRAME-MEND
 [CASE:GOLDMASK]; wrong telos for functional order; terminal bind
 [CASE:NOBLE_BROKEN_MASK] when grammar outlives faith.
 Default: Phase 1 SA + peaceful truth while substrate allows. Escalate lane
 or aggressive truth only when structurally required.

 STRAW / STEEL TEST [operative QC -- v25; lane routing -- distinct from METHOD
 OF TRUTH EPISTEMIC COSTUME TEST / inner voice]:
 Procedure: **FORM** straw + steel of same object → **MID** band placement
 → **TEST REAL** [Step 0, behavioral record, falsify before defend].
 Kit fix only if fix yields more truth-contact -- not social polish alone.
 **Truth without accepted cost = costume.** Lanes = pick your poison [priced
 failure each]; functional order does not promise clean win.

 [1] **Mend under fracture**
 Steel: responsible repair; exit abandons dependents.
 Straw [stop here]: Goldmask = evil bureaucrat.
 Test: IN-FRAME-MEND wrong **telos**; confusion limit when mend patches lie.
 REAL: outputs farm or rot? [CASE:OCEANIA_PARTY rhyme].
 [2] **Build for us**
 Steel: maintain **our** usable life under stress.
 Straw: fourth ideology / prepper pick one.
 Test: beneficiary-order vs truth-order [ORDER TOWARD TRUTH telos test].
 REAL: surplus routing + symmetric correction on **own** side.
 [3] **Peaceful concealment**
 Steel: prudent concealment until power.
 Straw: peaceful = coward / no truth.
 Test: same telos, contact surface; costume = silence **without** generative
 output; escalate when structurally required.
 [4] **Apex strike** [stabber]
 Steel: only terminal move -- kill operator, destroy System.
 Straw [stop here]: calls others lazy [surface read]; or "works too much"
 as praise [misses STAY-FAIL / upside falsifier].
 Test: **directional** -- calls **others** lazy; median critique of stabber
 = works too much. REAL: upside live or sunk grind [CASE:JAEHWAN]
 [CASE:MULAK].
 [5] **SA as whole program**
 Steel: exit / build elsewhere is the whole move.
 Straw: Ranni = burn everything.
 Test: SA = Phase 1 **tool** not telos; SILENT + PARTIAL [CASE:RANNI]
 BB-type: SA-from-start usually mis-specified.
 [6] **Truth without price**
 Steel: truth-at-max without coalition cost.
 Straw: aggressive = jerk / truth-person.
 Test: costs load-bearing -- isolation, disgust tax, CC depletion, identity-
 costume risk; falsify before defend on own reads.
 [7] **Lane by temperament**
 Steel: pick lane by values or personality.
 Straw: lane = identity cosplay.
 Test: name what failed on **silent path** first [runway, legibility,
 apex-window, MSC+CT]; route on substrate facts not preference.

 RELATION TO SILENT ALTERNATIVE [theory]:
 SA = Phase 1 tool functional order may use; not functional order itself.
 theory SILENT ALTERNATIVE owns three-phase strategy, civilizational scale,
 misclassification of transmission architectures, fourth phase. This block
 owns operative posture, delivery modes, lane choice when SA mis-specified.

 USAGE WHEN TRYING:
 Start functional order: ORDER SOLUTION on substrate that still permits it +
 truth at max + peaceful delivery. If BB-type frame [periodic apex window
 only]: SA-from-start usually wrong -- pick lane explicitly. Neither
 oscillation nor stay-fail is clean win. Mend seductive when fracture hurts
 SA looks like abandonment. Functional order telos: operative truth-order
 under stress, not maximum exit or maximum fight.

 Public rhyme: "Build what still works under stress."
