CHINESE ORDER CROSS-REF [analyst pin -- chat 2026-09-04]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Illustrative routing only. Run kit meters [honesty → anti-demon → Order]. 魔≠恶.
 Each symbol line: symbol / character-English / simple meaning. Explanation follows.
 Fiction moral grammar → WUXIA / 江湖 MORAL REGISTER. Do not file game lanes here.

 GLOSSARY [full definitions -- read before the rows]:
 ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Scope: every symbol in this section, defined once. The rows below carry the
 meter reading and the guards. This block carries the definition, so a reader
 can resolve a term without reading the whole map.
 Reading order: GLOSSARY [what the word is] → SYMBOL TABLE [one-line English]
 → ROWS [where the kit meter attaches] → PROVENANCE [who supplied what].
 Pronunciation: tone marks omitted; the pinyin is a lookup key, not a lesson.

 BAND-WORDS [the mercy / middle / anomaly rows]:
  宋襄之仁 · song xiang zhi ren · Song Xiang's humaneness.
  Literal: the humaneness of Duke Xiang of Song. Names a commander who kept
  ritual form [waiting for the enemy to cross and form ranks] and lost the
  battle. Names folly in mercy performed as propriety. Not kindness; not
  restraint; not the good band. Received from 左传 僖公二十二年, where the
  verdict is criticism; 公羊传 reads the same act as praise. The kit uses the
  左传 read and says so.
  妇人之仁 · fu ren zhi ren · the humaneness of a woman [received wording].
  Literal: the tenderness attributed to a woman in the idiom. Names a specific
  failure recorded in 史记·淮阴侯列传: Xiang Yu weeps over the sick and then
  refuses the fief that would pay a deserving officer. Names tenderness shown
  while the owed payment is withheld. Not a sex claim -- the kit target is the
  structure, not the gender of the phrase. Not the same failure as 宋襄之仁.
  中 · zhong · middle.
  Literal: the middle, the center. Names the fitting measure for a situation.
  Names fit, not compromise. Not an average of two positions. Not median.
  中庸 · zhong yong · middle + constant.
  Literal: the middle applied constantly. Names the good band of this map.
  Names the fitting measure held over time and circumstance [时中]. Not
  blandness; not everyone-pleasing; not kit Good by synonym.
  乡愿 · xiang yuan · village + sincere-looking.
  Literal: the one the village calls sincere. Names the counterfeit of 中庸:
  a person who offends no one by holding nothing. Received from 论语·阳货,
  where it is named 德之贼. Not politeness; not diplomacy; not moderation.
  德之贼 · de zhi zei · virtue's thief.
  Literal: the thief of virtue. Names what 乡愿 does to a moral order: it
  consumes the standing that virtue needs without paying any of its costs.
  怪 · guai · strange.
  Literal: anomaly, portent, prodigy [说文 怪，异也]. Names departure from the
  ordinary course. Descriptive first: the word reports oddity, not guilt. Not
  a moral verdict; not a being-class until later vernacular fiction [妖怪]. The
  kit files anomaly on the disorder side; the language does not.
  狂狷 · kuang juan · ardent + fastidious.
  Literal: the excessive and the overscrupulous. Names the two failures that
  still outrank the fake middle, because both hold a real standard somewhere.
  Received from 论语·子路 [必也狂狷乎]. Not mere intensity; not a mean streak.

 ORDER-WORDS [the right-path / norm / rite / governed rows]:
  正道 · zheng dao · upright + path.
  Literal: the straight road. Names the orthodox path a life or a polity can
  walk; in kit use, the Order-meter target: institutional right path, not a
  speech about it. Not kit Good. Not 大义. A faction quoting 正道 is making a
  claim the meter tests.
  王道 · wang dao · kingly way. / 霸道 · ba dao · hegemon's way.
  Literal: the way of a true king vs the way of the strongest. Names the
  received order pair. Absent from this map as a row; kept as a guard so a
  polity claiming 王道 is filed under 正道 and scored on outputs.
  纲纪 · gang ji · principle + discipline.
  Literal: the main net-rope and its cords. Names the norms a governed order
  runs on. Not personal taste; not a code whose binding is already dead -- a
  spoken code that no longer binds is 纲纪废弛.
  礼 · li · rite.
  Literal: rite, propriety, the ceremonial order. Names the form that tells
  roles how to meet. Ceremony is one piece of it. Not politeness; not a live
  order by itself -- forms can outlive the binding they were built to carry.
  治 · zhi · ordered.
  Literal: governed, well-ruled, usable. Names the ordered pole of the
  language's own pair 治/乱. A realm that can be used. Not kit Good; not a
  prosperous age; not proof the meters passed. A harsh state can be 治.
  盛世 · sheng shi · abundant + age.
  Literal: a flourishing era. Names an order-side outcome: peace, yield, a
  usable generation. Not a moral grade of persons; a rich age can still
  extract. File as result, then run Good separately.
  天命 · tian ming · heaven + command.
  Literal: the charge heaven gives to rule. Names a legitimacy grammar with a
  published removal clause [诗经 天命靡常; 尚书 皇天无亲，惟德是辅]. Names a
  bounded claim, not a blank check. Not unconditional cosmic license; not
  proof the claimant held it. The kit's costume read attaches to a deployment
  that hides the removal clause, not to the doctrine.
  民心 · min xin · people + heart.
  Literal: the heart of the people. Names the received check against which
  the charge is measured [尚书 民惟邦本]. Names a measured object, not a mood
  poll. Not sampling; not a slogan a ruler can self-certify; unfalsifiable
  where every feedback channel is closed.
  革命 · ge ming · remove + mandate.
  Literal: the mandate changes hands. Names the received consequence of a
  failed charge [周易 汤武革命，顺乎天而应于人]. Names a doctrine, not an
  endorsement of any particular removal. Not a pass for whoever shouts it.
  补偏救弊 · bu pian jiu bi · patch the slant + rescue the defect.
  Literal: correct a bias without inventing a new costume. Names the angel
  register of this map: repair work inside an existing order. Not 天使; not
  mercy without a cut; not license to spare the defect.
  匡扶 · kuang fu · rectify + support.
  Literal: hold something upright that is falling. Names active correction on
  behalf of a legitimate order. Not flattery of whoever sits.
  扶正 · fu zheng · support + upright.
  Literal: restore the rightful line. Names restoration work. Pairs with 祛邪
  when the restoration also removes what pushed it off.
  扶正祛邪 · fu zheng qu xie · support upright + expel the pathogen.
  Literal: a medical formula -- support the upright qi, drive out the
  pathogen. Names the usual pair, and names it as analogy: political use sits
  on a medical base. Not vengeance; not a purity purge; expelling 邪 still
  takes the Order meter.
  清平 · qing ping · clear + level.
  Literal: clean and even. Names a settled peace: grievances heard, violence
  not the daily law. Not a slogan; not the absence of conflict -- quiet
  extraction fails it.
  贤臣 · xian chen · worthy + minister.
  Literal: the officer who can carry the order. Names competence plus duty in
  a role. The seat does not confer it. Not fame; not loyalty to a person over
  the measure.
  清官 · qing guan · clear + official.
  Literal: the official whose ledger matches the pay. Names refusal of private
  take while holding public power. A later vernacular word. Not niceness; not
  宋襄之仁; clean hands can still be foolish or harsh.
  循吏 · xun li · following + official.
  Literal: the officer who follows the measure. Names the received good-officer
  type [史记 循吏列传]: rule by instruction and example rather than severity.
  Can be dull; can be slow. Not 清官 [clean hands]; not 贤臣 [competence].
  酷吏 · ku li · cruel + official.
  Literal: the officer whose severity is the method. Names the received harsh
  type [史记 酷吏列传]: order produced by fear and punishment. Can be
  effective; still filed on the harsh side. Not a slur; score methods and take.
  天使 · tian shi · heaven + messenger.
  Literal: a heavenly envoy. Names the imported Christian figure, and modern
  praise [loan word]. Not the kit angel register, which is 补偏救弊 / 匡扶.

 DISORDER-WORDS [the tangle / era / craft rows]:
  乱 · luan · tangled.
  Literal: disordered, tangled, ungoverned. Names the pair-opposite of 治.
  Structured or noisy, the word is the same. Not kit "chaos" as a separate
  band -- Chinese does not make that cut. Desk label 乱无纲 was coined.
  乱世 · luan shi · tangled + age.
  Literal: an age of disorder. Names a duration: the era itself is disordered,
  not one riot inside it. Not a mood; not proof every actor in it is 恶.
  奸 · jian · treacherous.
  Literal: crafty harm worked from inside a role. Names betrayal that uses the
  role's own access. The smile is part of the tool. Not open grab; not 怪.
  邪 · xie · slanted.
  Literal: off the straight line. Names departure from 正道 across a wide
  range: wrong path, baleful craft, unorthodox school. Not "depraved" as the
  only reading; not cartoon evil; not terminal 魔. A path word, not a looks word.
  恶 · e · bad.
  Literal: harm that can be named. Names the evil band. Not a mean streak; not
  demon; open evil can pass the honesty bar and still fail Order.
  奸邪 · jian xie · treacherous + slanted.
  Literal: craft and wrong path together. Names evil that is also skill.
  明抢 · ming qiang · open + snatch.
  Literal: robbery done in the open. Names honest disorder: the take with
  nothing hidden, no costume of law. The publicity is the point.
  明火执仗 · ming huo zhi zhang · bright fire + carry weapons.
  Literal: torches lit, weapons in hand. Names the same open grab in received
  phrasing. Not sneaky malice; not a failed cover.
  阴损 · yin sun · hidden + harm.
  Literal: harm worked from the shadow. Names the demonic mean streak:
  underhanded, petty, morally shabby. Not 恶 by itself; not 魔; a clean cruel
  law is a different row.
  缺德 · que de · lacking + virtue.
  Literal: virtue missing in an ordinary act. Names the daily mean: the person
  who cheats a small bond and shrugs. Not grand extract; not 怪's class.
  魔性 · mo xing · demon + nature.
  Literal: a nature like a demon's. Names nature-level demonic quality, sitting
  with 魔 rather than with the mean streak. Technical reading is Buddhist-
  register; everyday use is slang [魔性BGM = weirdly catchy]. Say which.
  魔 · mo · demon.
  Literal: the demon, the tempter. Names the terminal band: facsimile or void,
  no fixable extract left. Not evil-that-works; not a spirit story by default.
  Guard: people at the terminal fork only. A system is not 魔.
  邪祟 · xie sui · slanted + haunt.
  Literal: noxious spirits that sicken a place. Names spirit-class harm.
  Not a human demon class; not 邪 as a method; not a political faction.
  人惧如魔 · ren ju ru mo · people fear [someone] like a demon.
  Literal: coined desk phrase, no received source. Names the charge that
  outputs make people treat a person as 魔. Not a class assignment; not
  evidence. Fear is the datum to audit, not the verdict.
  画皮 · hua pi · painted + skin.
  Literal: the painted hide a spirit wears. Names costume worn as a body: a
  manufactured surface presented as a person. Not every liar; not default
  fox-seduction. The tell is the worn skin.
  狐媚 · hu mei · fox + charm.
  Literal: the fox's beguilement. Names one mimic type: charm that captures
  through desire. A species of mimic, not the whole mimic class.

 TAKE-WORDS [the squeeze / surplus / era-collapse rows]:
  榨取 · zha qu · press + take.
  Literal: press until the surplus comes out. Names taking by pressure while
  the victim stays formally in place. Modern political-economy word. Not a
  wage; not open robbery.
  剥削 · bo xue · peel + pare.
  Literal: strip and shave away. Names surplus-take: taking what another
  produced. Modern, Japanese-mediated coinage, not a classical rite-word.
  Not a jersey for any harsh rule.
  祸乱 · huo luan · disaster + tangle.
  Literal: calamity riding on disorder. Names harm-events stacked on 乱:
  famine, war, collapse of daily life. Not a moral type of person.
  天下大乱 · tian xia da luan · under-heaven + great tangle.
  Literal: the whole lived world in disorder. Names received disorder at full
  scale. The received phrase for a desk label that tried to say "chaos without
  norms." Not a personality; not kit collapse [崩] unless the world is unusable.
  纲纪废弛 · gang ji fei chi · principle-discipline + abandoned-slack.
  Literal: the net-rope gone slack. Names 纲纪 that is still spoken and no
  longer binding. Not 礼崩乐坏 by itself -- slack can precede ruined rites.
  礼崩乐坏 · li beng yue huai · rites collapse + music spoils.
  Literal: the rite-and-music order broken. Names disorder-max of the
  propriety system. Not an early warning only; not demon-class; not "hypocrisy
  while the machine still works."
  世风日下 · shi feng ri xia · age-custom + day-down.
  Literal: customs worsen day by day. Names a complaint of decay in ordinary
  custom. Not proof by itself -- run the ledger before promoting a proverb.
  崩 · beng · collapse.
  Literal: the structure breaks. Names a break past 乱: the condition life
  cannot use. Not the Chinese word for disorder [that is 乱]; the split is kit.
  崩溃 · beng kui · collapse + burst.
  Literal: the same collapse, in the modern two-character form. Same kit file.

 DESK-COINED [not received -- marked wherever used]:
  乱无纲 · luan wu gang · tangle without norms.
  Coined. No received phrase. Names chaos-without-norms; superseded by 天下大乱.
  直恶 · zhi e · straight evil.
  Coined. No received phrase. Names evil with no cover; superseded by 明抢 /
  明火执仗.
  人惧如魔 · [see above -- desk row].

 SHARED-WITH-WUXIA [defined here; fiction narrowing lives in that topic]:
  义 · yi · rightness. Duty owed to teacher, sect, companion; wider than a
  team jersey, narrower than the Order meter.
  利 · li · profit. Gain set against that duty; not a sermon against benefit.
  大义 · da yi · great rightness. Speech that spends people. Not 正道 -- the
  scale-talk is a claim to test, not the meter target.
  术 · shu · art / method. Craft with no virtue inside it; works either way.
  鬼手 · gui shou · ghost hand. Taboo-healer lane; a nickname outside its case.
  江湖 · jiang hu · river + lake. The unofficial world; in the game topic, the
  martial public. Not the imperial court; not 治.
  Fiction rows for 棋 / 恩义 / 名分 / 心 / 魂 / 杀气 / 奇医 / 方士 stay in the
  WUXIA topic. Do not import their narrowing back into this hub.

 Inline examples [how the table is meant to be read]:
  "He ran 治 without 礼 -- a usable realm, no binding." → governed, no rites.
  "The claim was 天命; the deployment hid 靡常." → revocable charge, term hidden.
  "Her 妇人之仁 was a 画皮 over withheld pay." → tenderness worn as a surface.
  "Not 怪, not 魔 -- 奸: the role's own access used." → anomaly excluded,
   demon excluded, betrayal-from-inside filed.

 PROVENANCE [what comes from China vs framework -- load-bearing]:
 [CN] received term or received doctrine; the source language supplies it.
 [CN/kit] received term, kit assigns the meter reading. Split the two when scoring.
 [kit] framework construct; no Chinese source. Do not read it back onto tradition.
 [desk] coined here; no received phrase. Marked where it appears.
 Default on an unmarked row: the word is [CN]; the "Names ..." line is [kit] reading.
 Failure mode: quoting a [kit] "Names" line as if a classical text said it.

 CHINESE THREE BANDS [kit grouping -- NOT a received Chinese triad]:
 Guard: the classical corpus does not cut its own bands this way. Received axes are
 义/利, 王道/霸道, 君子/小人, 德/力, 正/邪, 治/乱. This grouping is [kit] -- three rows
 the desk reads often, held together for routing. Do not cite it as tradition.

 宋襄之仁 / Song Xiang's humaneness / too nice is foolish. [CN]
 妇人之仁 / woman's humaneness / the received name for that folly. [CN]
 Names mercy past the cut: spare the enemy, skip the price, call the loss virtue.
 Not the good. Not the middle. Not Order. Bounded kindness is not this row.
 Guard: 妇人之仁 is the received wording. The kit claim is the folly, not a sex.
 Guard [CN split -- two different failures]: 妇人之仁 is 史记·淮阴侯列传 [Han Xin on
 Xiang Yu: weeps with the sick, then withholds the fief -- tenderness displayed over
 payment refused]. 宋襄之仁 is battlefield propriety [左传 僖公二十二年]. Do not file
 one as the other. The 韩信 row is the stronger HONESTY OVER COSTUME specimen:
 tenderness as costume over withheld justice.
 Guard [counter-tradition]: 公羊传 praises 宋襄 -- 临大事而不忘大礼. "Too nice is
 foolish" is the 左传 read, not a settled verdict. Mark it as one side of two.

 中 / middle / the fitting measure. [CN]
 中庸 / middle + constant / the middle is the good. [CN]
 Names the good band in this Chinese map.
 Not a bland average. Not kit Good by synonym. Surplus, CC, and exit stay on
 the Good meter. Everyone-pleasing is the fake middle.
 乡愿 / village + sincere-looking / the fake middle. [CN]
 德之贼 / virtue's thief / what that fake middle is. [CN]
 Guard: rhyme only. VALUES-FRAME POOL files 中庸 on V2 V5. This topic does not edit that pool.
 Guard [CN check]: 论语·雍也 中庸之为德也其至矣乎; 孟子·尽心下 乡愿 against 狂狷.
 The "not a bland average" line is 时中, received. Holds.

 怪 / strange / the anomalous. [CN]
 Names anomalous departure, portent, prodigy. The word is descriptive first.
 Not "the freak is evil" -- that headline overstated the source. 说文 怪，异也.
 Not a moral verdict by itself. 论语·述而 子不语怪 is silence about anomalies, not
 a ruling that they are evil.
 Guard: 妖怪 as a being-class in vernacular fiction is later and taxonomic, not a
 verdict. Moral load arrived with genre, not with the character.
 Guard [CN/kit]: the kit files anomaly on the disorder side; the language does not.
 Score the departure with the Order meter, not the word.
 狂狷 / mad + timid / ardent or fastidious. Outranks the fake-nice. [CN]
 Not mere intensity. Not the mean streak. Not terminal demon.
 Guard: game 术 and 鬼手 are not this row. Fiction method → wuxia topic.
 Guard [wording]: "freak" was analyst shorthand, not translation. Use 怪 /
 anomalous / anomalous departure. Reserved desk use of "freak" is fine in speech;
 do not let it stand as the gloss of the character.

 DEMONIC≠EVIL. Three bands stay split.
 Evil takes, and can say so. Demonic is mean or immoral without being the demon
 class. Demon is terminal perform, no graph.
 Guard: English "evil" collapses 恶, 魔, and 魔性. This map does not.

 ORDER WORDS:
 正道 / upright + path / orthodox way a life or a polity can walk. [CN]
 Names the Order-meter target: institutional right path, not a speech about it.
 Not kit Good. Not 大义. A faction quoting the right path is a claim to test.
 Guard: 正道≠大义. Scale-righteousness speech → wuxia topic.
 Guard [CN missing axis]: 王道 / kingly way vs 霸道 / hegemon's way is the received
 order pair and is not in this map. File under 正道 branch when a polity claims it;
 score outputs, not the claim. Do not read 王道 as kit Order by label.

 纲纪 / principle + discipline / the norms. [CN]
 Names the discipline a governed order runs on. Slack norms are 纲纪废弛, below.
 Not personal taste. Not a costume code with the binding already dead.
 Guard: when the bonds are gone, score disorder. Do not still call it 纲纪.

 礼 / rite / the propriety system. [CN]
 Names the form that tells roles how to meet. Ceremony is one piece of it.
 Not politeness. Not a live order by itself. Forms can outlive the binding.
 Guard: collapse of this system is 礼崩乐坏, below. Do not treat a rite as Order.

 治 / ordered / a realm that can be used. [CN]
 Names the ordered pole. Its pair is 乱. A governed state can still be harsh.
 Not kit Good. Not a prosperous age. Not proof the meters passed.
 Guard: 治/乱 is the language's own pair. Do not invent a third Chinese pole.

 盛世 / abundant + age / a prosperous age. [CN]
 Names an outcome on the order side: peace, yield, a usable generation.
 Not kit Good. Not a moral grade of persons. A rich age can still extract.
 Guard: file under order-side results. Run Good on a separate pass.

 天命 / heaven + command / the charge to rule. [CN]
 Names a legitimacy grammar with a published removal clause, not a blank check.
 诗经·大雅·文王 天命靡常; 尚书 皇天无亲，惟德是辅; 孟子·梁惠王下 闻诛一夫纣矣.
 The doctrine states the condition under which the ruler loses it. Bounded claim.
 Not unconditional cosmic license. Not proof the claimant held it.
 Guard [kit split -- load-bearing]: the CLAIM is bounded; the DEPLOYMENT can still
 be costume [theory MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE]. Score the removal clause, not the
 invocation. A ruler who cites 天命 while suppressing every 民心 channel is not
 running the doctrine -- he is quoting it.
 Guard [do not]: file 天命 as unbounded liability without noting 靡常. That inverts
 the source. See theory PRIMARY CASE [China -- Mandate of Heaven] hedge.

 民心 / people + heart / what the charge is measured against. [CN]
 Names the received check on rule: 尚书 民惟邦本; 孟子 得天下有道，得其民.
 Names a measured object, not a mood poll.
 Not public opinion sampling. Not a slogan a ruler can self-certify.
 Guard [measurement]: if every 民心 channel [press, petition, protest, exit] is
 closed, the claim is unfalsifiable by construction -- score Track G, not the word.

 革命 / remove + mandate / the mandate changes hands. [CN]
 Names the received consequence of a failed charge: 周易 汤武革命，顺乎天而应乎人.
 Names a doctrine, not an endorsement of any particular removal.
 Not a license for whoever shouts it. Not kit revolution-good.
 Guard: DEMONIC≠EVIL applies. A 革命 claim still takes the Order meter and the
 honesty meter. 孟子 诛一夫 is a claim about the target, not a pass for the actor.

 补偏救弊 / patch-slant + rescue-defect / correct the bias without a new costume. [CN]
 Names the angel register in this map: remedy, not a heavenly messenger.
 Not 天使. Not mercy with no cut. Not a license to spare the defect.
 Guard: angel register lives here and in 匡扶. Loanword "angel" does not.

 匡扶 / rectify + support / hold the order upright. [CN]
 Names active correction on behalf of a legitimate order.
 Not flattery of whoever sits. Not the expel-the-anomalous formula by itself.
 Guard: pair with 扶正 when the work is restoring the rightful line.

 扶正 / support + upright / restore the rightful line. [CN]
 扶正祛邪 / support-upright + expel-slanted / the usual pair. [CN]
 Names restoration of the rightful.
 Not vengeance. Not a purity purge. Expelling 邪 still needs the Order meter.
 Guard: 祛邪 is the second half. Do not score a purge as 扶正 alone.
 Guard [CN origin]: the pair is medical first [support upright qi, expel the
 pathogen]; political use is an analogy on that base. Do not present it as a
 native political formula.

 清平 / clear + level / clean quiet order. [CN]
 Names a settled peace: grievances heard, violence not the daily law.
 Not a slogan. Not the absence of conflict. Quiet extract fails it.
 Guard: use for a condition of rule. Do not use as a personality compliment.

 贤臣 / worthy + minister / the officer who can carry the order. [CN]
 Names competence plus duty in the role. The seat does not confer it.
 Not fame. Not loyalty to a person over the measure.
 Guard: score the outputs of office. Title is not the word.

 清官 / clear + official / ledger matches the pay. [CN -- later vernacular word]
 Names refusal of the private take while holding public power.
 Not niceness. Not 宋襄之仁. Clean hands can still be foolish or harsh.
 Guard: honesty meter first. 清官 is a result word, not a jersey.

 循吏 / following + official / the officer who follows the measure. [CN]
 酷吏 / cruel + official / the officer whose severity is the method. [CN]
 Names the received office pair [史记 循吏列传 / 酷吏列传]. A paired file, not two
 separate virtues: the tradition judges officers against each other, not in the
 abstract. 循吏 can be dull; 酷吏 can be effective and still filed on the harsh side.
 Not 清官 [clean hands] and not 贤臣 [competence + duty]. Outputs first, then the pair.
 Guard: do not use 酷吏 as a slur; score the methods and the take.
 Guard [CN/kit]: the pairing is received; the meter reading is kit.

 天使 / heaven + messenger / the Christian angel word. [CN -- loan]
 Names the imported figure: messenger, saint-picture, modern "angel" praise.
 Not the kit angel register. That register is 补偏救弊 / 匡扶.
 Guard: hearing 天使 is not evidence of patch-and-correct.

 DISORDER WORDS:
 乱 / tangled / a realm that is not governed. [CN]
 Names the pair-opposite of 治. Structured or noisy, the word is the same.
 Not kit "chaos" as a separate band. Chinese does not make that cut.
 Guard: desk label 乱无纲 was coined [desk]. Prefer 乱, 天下大乱, 纲纪废弛.

 乱世 / tangled + age / disorder long enough to name the era. [CN]
 Names a duration: the age itself is the disorder, not one riot.
 Not a mood. Not proof that every actor in it is 恶.
 Guard: persons in a 乱世 still take their own meter. The age is not an alibi.

 奸 / treacherous / crafty harm from inside a role. [CN]
 Names betrayal that uses the role's access. The smile is part of the tool.
 Not open grab. Not the anomaly class [怪]. Craft can wear 正.
 Guard: pair 奸邪 when the craft is also off the right path.

 邪 / slanted / off the right path. [CN]
 Names departure from 正道. The range is wide: wrong path, baleful craft,
 unorthodox school.
 Not "depraved" as the only reading. Not cartoon evil. Not terminal 魔.
 Guard: fiction narrowing [harmful method inside a story] → wuxia topic.
 Guard [wording]: "anomalous look" belongs to 怪, not here. 邪 is a path word, not
 a looks word.

 恶 / bad / harm that can be named. [CN]
 奸邪 / treacherous + slanted / evil that is also craft. [CN]
 Names the evil band.
 Not the mean streak. Not demon. Open evil can pass the honesty bar and
 still fail Order.
 Guard: DEMONIC≠EVIL. Do not translate every 恶 as 魔.

 明抢 / open + snatch / the take done in public. [CN]
 明火执仗 / bright-fire + hold-weapon / the same open grab. [CN]
 Names honest disorder: robbery with the torches lit, no costume of law.
 Not sneaky malice. Not a failed cover. The publicity is the point.
 Guard: desk label 直恶 was coined. Use 明抢 or 明火执仗.

 阴损 / hidden + harm / mean harm off its own ledger. [CN]
 Names the demonic mean streak: underhanded, petty, morally shabby.
 Not 恶 by itself. Not 魔. A clean cruel law is a different row.
 Guard: file with 缺德. Do not promote a mean streak to demon class.

 缺德 / lack + virtue / virtue missing in the ordinary act. [CN]
 Names the daily mean: the person who cheats the small bond and shrugs.
 Not grand extract. Not the anomaly class [怪]. Not terminal perform.
 Guard: demonic register, mean side. Still DEMONIC≠EVIL.

 魔性 / demon + nature / a disposition like a demon's. [CN]
 Names nature-level demonic quality. It sits with 魔, not with the mean streak.
 Not 阴损. Not 缺德. Not a metaphor for "unkind."
 Guard: if the record is only mean, stay on 阴损 / 缺德. 魔性 is the stronger file.
 Guard [modern sense]: everyday usage is largely slang [魔性BGM, 魔性笑声 = weirdly
 catchy]. The technical reading above is Buddhist-register. Say which one you mean;
 a reader will otherwise hear the meme.

 魔 / demon / terminal perform, no graph left. [CN]
 Names the worst band: facsimile or void, not a fixable extract.
 Not evil-that-works. Not a spirit story by default. Not "someone feared."
 Guard: people at the terminal fork only. A system is not 魔.

 邪祟 / slanted + haunt / noxious spirits. [CN]
 Names spirits that sicken a place or a house. 祟 is suì.
 Not a human demon class. Not 邪 as a method. Not a political faction.
 Guard: do not translate a bad official into 邪祟.

 人惧如魔 / person fear like demon / fright before the meter. [desk -- coined. No received phrase.]
 Names a charge: outputs make people treat the person as 魔.
 Not a class assignment. Not evidence. Fear is the datum to audit.
 Guard: audit outputs. Then file 恶, 阴损, 魔性, or clear.

 画皮 / painted + skin / a human surface it drew on. [CN]
 Names costume worn as a body. The surface is manufactured.
 Not every liar. Not fox-seduction as a default. The tell is the worn skin.
 Guard: use when the presented person is a cover, not a role under strain.

 狐媚 / fox + charm / charm that captures through desire. [CN]
 Names one mimic type: beguilement, the fox's lure.
 Not all mimics. Not 画皮. Not ordinary beauty or ordinary want.
 Guard: file as a species of mimic. Do not make it the whole mimic class.

 榨取 / press + take / press until the surplus comes out. [CN -- modern]
 Names taking by pressure. The victim is still formally in place.
 Not a wage. Not open robbery. The squeeze is the method.
 Guard: modern political-economy word. Pair with 剥削 when the take is surplus.

 剥削 / peel + pare / take the surplus another produced. [CN -- modern, Japanese-mediated]
 Names surplus-take. The word is modern, and it is plain about the take.
 Not a classical rite-word. Not honest open grab unless the take is public.
 Guard: use for surplus. Do not use as a jersey for any harsh rule.

 祸乱 / disaster + tangle / disaster with disorder riding on it. [CN]
 Names harm-events stacked on 乱: famine, war, collapse of daily life.
 Not a moral type of person. Not 乱 alone.
 Guard: score the condition. Persons inside it still take a separate meter.

 天下大乱 / under-heaven + great tangle / disorder at the scale of the lived world. [CN]
 Names received disorder at full scale. The received phrase for a coined
 desk label that tried to say "chaos without norms."
 Not a personality. Not kit collapse [崩] unless the usable world is gone.
 Guard: prefer this over 乱无纲. 乱无纲 was coined [desk].

 纲纪废弛 / principle-discipline + abandoned-slack / bonds spoken, no longer binding. [CN]
 Names 纲纪 that has loosened: roles spoken, enforcement dead.
 Not 礼崩乐坏 by itself. Slack can be corruption before the rites are ruined.
 Guard: repair target is the binding. A new slogan does not retighten it.

 礼崩乐坏 / rite-collapse + music-spoil / the rite-and-music order has collapsed. [CN]
 Names disorder-max of the propriety system: 崩 and 坏 mean broken and ruined.
 Not an early warning only. Not demon-class. Empty ritual with the forms still
 running is a related Confucian point [论语·阳货 礼云礼云，玉帛云乎哉]. It is not
 this idiom.
 Guard: do not use it for "hypocrisy while the machine still works."

 世风日下 / age-custom + day-down / customs worsen on a slope. [CN]
 Names a complaint of decay in ordinary custom.
 Not proof by itself. Elder weather and stigma use this phrase a lot.
 Guard: sometimes it clusters real formation or extract decay. Run the ledger
 before promoting the proverb to a finding.

 崩 / collapse / the usable world breaks. [CN]
 崩溃 / collapse + burst / the same kit collapse. [CN]
 Names a break past 乱: the condition life cannot use.
 Not the Chinese word for disorder. That word is 乱. This split is kit.
 Guard: say the split is kit whenever this row is used. Do not teach it as 乱.

 Cross-ref: ANALYST AXIS DOCTRINE [above]; applied METAPHOR-FORM; ANTI-DEMON
 MEASUREMENT DESK; theory EVIL; theory GOOD; STRICT ORDER / STRICT DISORDER;
 theory MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [PRIMARY CASE -- 天命 row above routes to it];
 theory CHINA 1978 [split-axis -- separate lane]; [CASE:BROKEN_RUUNE] #6
 [China register split -- three layers A/B/C];

WUXIA / 江湖 MORAL REGISTER [game -- CASE:PHANTOM_BLADE_ZERO -- chat 2026-11-03]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 Moral grammar taken from [CASE:PHANTOM_BLADE_ZERO] [S-GAME, 2026].
 Accuracy target is this game. Not the China-order hub. Not nation posture.
 Each symbol line: symbol / character-English / simple meaning. Explanation follows.
 Run kit meters on instances.

 义 / rightness / duty to teacher, sect, and companion.
 In this game, Soul's refusals run on this duty: not a puppet, not a hollow blade.
 Not the hub's 正道. Not a team jersey. Classical 义 is wider than this debt.
 Guard: do not file game 义 as the Order meter.

 利 / profit / the gain set against that duty.
 In this game, 利 is what 义 is asked to lose to: expedience, board advantage,
 a longer life bought by giving the heart up.
 Not a sermon against all benefit. The game uses it as the opposing pole.
 Guard: hub extract words [榨取 / 剥削] are the order-section file when the
 case is not this game's moral pair.

 大义 / great + rightness / speech that spends people.
 棋 / chess-piece / a person spent by that plan.
 In this game, Zuo Shang's license to spend towns. Earnest in his voice.
 Monstrous in the cost.
 Not Order contact. 正道≠大义.
 Pathos pin: UTILITARIAN DÀYÌ = demonic pathos register [sophist OFFICE AS
 BURDEN: burden claimed, the weak pay, cruelty lightened by a scale-noun].
 Still DEMONIC≠EVIL. Not 魔. Not 奸邪 only.
 Guard: do not file 大义 into the hub as 正道 or as the middle-good.

 恩义 / favor + rightness / favor plus the debt it creates.
 In this game, the bond to shifu, companion, brother: grace received, duty back.
 Betrayal cuts because the debt was real.
 Not a contract form. Not 大义. The debt is personal, not realm-scale speech.
 Guard: hub 债 language is not this. Game debt-as-bill is the 债 entry below.

 名分 / name + portion / the slot a person holds toward another.
 In this game, betrayal is a broken slot: teacher, brother, patriarch, sect place.
 The wound is the relation, not a statute.
 Not a legal charge. Not 礼 as the whole propriety system [that is the hub].
 Guard: do not expand one broken 名分 into 礼崩乐坏.

 江湖 / river + lake / this game's martial public.
 In this game, the public where Order, Mandate, and the Lady cult all speak.
 Not the imperial court. Not the hub's governed state [治].
 Guard: classical 江湖 is the unofficial world. Here it is the martial public
 the plot walks through.

 正 / upright / the face a faction claims.
 In this game, Order, Mandate, and the Lady cult each quote it while the
 methods go another way.
 Not a passed Order meter. Not 正道 confirmed. A claim on the marquee.
 Guard: hub 正道 is the meter. This 正 is the costume the meter has to test.

 邪 / slanted / unorthodox or harmful method in this fiction.
 In this game, the methods run under a 正 face: puppets, seals, cult "mercy."
 Not cartoon evil. Not the hub's whole range for 邪. Not terminal 魔.
 Guard: hub 邪 stays heterodox / baleful / unorthodox. Do not import this
 fiction narrowing back into the hub as the definition.

 心 / heart / the moral object that can be installed or stripped.
 In this game, the sculptor installs a heart: hollow [feeling removed] or
 tainted [passion kept, timer running]. Soul keeps the heart that still feels.
 Not a mood. Not 魂. The heart is the install.
 Guard: hub has no 心 row. Do not translate a policy "heart" into this.

 魂 / soul / a thing that can be lost, and the protagonist's name.
 In this game, 魂 is the protagonist's name and a moral object: capture,
 displacement, a blade that can be hollowed.
 Not the hub demon. Not 心. Losing 魂 is capture, not a mean streak.
 Guard: do not file soul-displacement as 魔 because the word sounds demonic.

 杀气 / kill + qi / killing intent. Martial fire, inborn and controllable.
 In this game, the aura of the kill: raised, lost, or still in the body.
 The case states both the intent and the inborn-fire read.
 Not a moral verdict. Not 恶. Fire present is not evil proven.
 Guard: hub does not use 杀气. Leave it in this topic.

 术 / art / craft with no virtue inside it.
 In this game, method sold, installed, or stolen: poison-heart art, remaking
 flesh. The craft works either way.
 Not 义. Not a sermon. A good person can hold a bad 术, and the reverse.
 Guard: hub 邪 is not the name of every 术. File the method here first.

 债 / debt / the bill for the craft.
 In this game, the sculptor's price: he opens the clock, he is owed the heart
 that remakes flesh. The bad end burns the creditor with no payoff.
 Not 恩义. 恩义 is grace. 债 is the bill.
 Guard: do not call the bill a righteousness speech. That is 大义.

 鬼手 / ghost + hand / this game's taboo healer.
 奇医 / odd + doctor / the extraordinary-healer lane.
 方士 / method + man / the methods-master lane.
 In this game, the Flesh Sculptor: 术 plus 债, no 义 sermon.
 He revives both blades and sends Soul for the heart that remakes flesh.
 Not a 邪 architect by default. Not the hub's anomaly row [怪]. Not 魔.
 Guard: 鬼手 outside this case is a nickname. Here it is this person.

VALIDITY / SOUNDNESS / MATERIAL CONDITIONAL [topic 1 -- v25]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 PURPOSE: separate structure-fail from premise-fail; stop collapsing
 English "if" into material →.

 VALIDITY [formal consequence]:
 Argument = premises + conclusion. Valid iff no counterexample case
 where all premises true and conclusion not true. Validity concerns
 form / possible cases -- not actual worldly facts [forall x Calgary
 ch.2]. Formal validity ignores non-logical content [ophthalmologist
 vs eye-doctor conceptual validity ≠ formal].

 SOUNDNESS:
 Sound iff valid AND all premises true. Valid + false premises can
 yield false or ridiculous conclusions [oranges/instruments]. True
 premises + true conclusion ≠ valid [London/Beijing/Paris]. Rebut:
 attack premise truth OR attack validity; logic primarily helps the
 latter [forall x 2.4].

 FRAMEWORK RHYME [METHOD OF TRUTH]:
 Validity fail ≈ Theory∅Real structure [form does not force].
 Premise fail ≈ Real∅Theory / false anchor under sound structure.
 Inductive strength ≠ deductive validity [Calgary snow -- good
 inductive still invalid].

 MATERIAL CONDITIONAL [→]:
 A → B false iff A true and B false; otherwise true [truth table].
 Symbolizes "if A then B" / "A only if B" after paraphrase -- not
 causation, relevance, or temporal order [forall x 5.4, ch.9].
 Indicative material → ≠ subjunctive / counterfactual "would have"
 [Clinton helium balloon -- same T/F pattern, opposite English
 verdict; forall x 10.3]. Do not collapse natural-language relevance
 into →.

 FALSIFICATION: ordinary METHOD OF TRUTH use requires this block
 [would force merge into S1/S2 -- reject EOF shelf]; OR appendix used
 to add FOL scoring axes without analyst assign.

 Cross-ref: METHOD OF TRUTH; L3 pins [validity/soundness +
 material ≠ natural if]
 technical/screenshots/logic_textbooks/.
